# Android / iOS V2 Alignment Audit

Date: 2026-10-08

## Scope

This baseline compares the Android project at:

- `/Users/huxiaoran/Documents/xiaoranProjects/MyGolfSwingApp-Android`

against the iOS project at:

- `/Users/huxiaoran/Documents/xiaoranProjects/MyGolfSwingApp`

The Android app uses `V2*` Kotlin files. On iOS, most product surfaces live under `MyGolfSwingApp/V2`, but the current swing-recording player still lives under `Views/JustSwing` while using the current V2 product direction.

Run `scripts/android_check_ios_alignment.sh` after pulling or editing the iOS repo. The checker verifies that this audit names the current iOS HEAD and any dirty iOS parity files that can affect Android feature or build tracking, including source files and Xcode project/config files such as `project.pbxproj`.

## 2026-10-06 Tracking Snapshot

AI service configuration now has direct device coverage for the complete iOS focus-versus-active lifecycle. Provider focus restores its scoped model/key without switching; verified providers switch directly; active model/API-key edits enter `测试并应用`; successful non-empty tests commit and refresh AI chat chrome; ordinary failure, empty response, timeout, and final provider-persistence failure restore the former provider/model/key/verification snapshot and expose `重试`; actual coroutine cancellation stays silent. The timeout branch was corrected so `TimeoutCancellationException` is treated as a retryable connection failure rather than being swallowed as user cancellation. All nine focused tests pass together on Pixel 7 Pro API 37. Evidence is `ai-service-config-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `d43ea75c5e9679c20912b20ee72c076a3a822c7a2311af45f8a89827b9d7e056`) and `ai-service-config-success-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `0478483c6d81e86db771a4ae521eb1ac73a29803c4f0a8cceff9314ecb216f17`).

AI pre-send configuration recovery is now verified end to end on API 37. Selecting an external provider without a key and sending opens configuration exactly once while retaining the typed question; after a valid key is applied, the same question sends without retyping, and Android clears the draft only after the user message has actually entered the conversation. The nine-test AI chrome class remains green, with the completed handoff recorded in `ai-chat-pre-send-config-handoff-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `448bf162a814f877516be077d7fcd2dc14276d5147ec535cceab2cfb2b17e88f`).

Built-in AI quota display now has direct lifecycle evidence. The top-bar model suffix renders the injected starting quota, refreshes from `qwen-plus (29/30)` to `qwen-plus (28/30)` after a successful streamed reply, and a subsequent switch to DeepSeek renders only `deepseek-chat` without querying or leaking the built-in quota. The nine-test AI chrome class passes together on API 37; evidence is `ai-chat-builtin-quota-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `e1855e972b5b45fd4385357ec857dd9603c7aa6156750a56d6677ca8afb72316`).

AI contextual answers now exercise the real Android prompt-assembly path with a seeded account profile, training profile, active goal, text/video/plaza Memo tags, and two recent swing clips. The captured outbound system message contains every source while the rendered page exposes only a natural coaching answer; neither the system prompt nor the retired Android-only `已接入档案、近期 Memo 与训练记录` pill appears. The same test locks current iOS' send-only composer, while unit coverage fixes the fallback deadline at 75 seconds and the established short-deadline device test preserves partial output. Evidence is `ai-chat-contextual-answer-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `9d7b33c2ae42e960b70a9dfcc4c45e0ee0260e59e705f917c7e59fde420e1ed9`).

Latest 2026-10-08 gate: 1138 Android unit tests pass with zero failures, errors, or skips. The Android-test source currently contains 428 `@Test` cases; the established complete-suite execution remains the 153-test API 37 run recorded below, with the seventeen current C2C rich-message/action/reaction/forwarding/translation/merged-history/voice-input/voice-playback/file-download/delivery-state/history-loading/emoji-interoperability tests, the three current C2C profile/settings surfaces, the nine current AI-configuration states, both Home analysis-source states, and the current coach incoming-activity/student-card/delivery-completion states subsequently passing as targeted API 37 runs. The complete 45-test teaching-space page class was rerun after the supplied empty-state polish and passes on API 37, including learner/coach zero states, `在练 / 在看`, both guided actions, voice drafts, and repeatable invite-poster generation. A fresh eight-test Home analysis recovery suite, three-test API 37 source-sheet run, two-test real-camera recording run, six-test GolfCam source-recovery suite, and two-test API 37 GolfCam source-sheet run cover album launch/import/trim/save failure, retry, analysis-detail routing, ordinary continuous recording, and trajectory picker/import recovery; the six-test Home quick-add/tool run passes all four signed-in/guest creation routes, all six tool callbacks, real reorder/hide/restore behavior, and fresh-composition preference restoration. The six-test Swing Clip Detail run plus the focused Credits Reset, nine-test Credits Purchase, Feedback, AI configuration, legal, Storage Management, 21-test Profile archive/edit/location/settings, 32-test download/calendar/queue/friend/citation/thread runs, nine-test Memo secondary-list page class, twenty-test online/async-download class, and ten-test AI-history class pass on API 37 after their visual/content corrections; the AI-history class passes under both gesture and three-button navigation, and the shared clipboard failure boundary also passes its focused API 37 run.

The current local artifact checkpoint has zero lint errors, 58 tracked warnings, and 5 hints. The debug APK is 168,830,116 bytes with SHA-256 `e879151a54d6218fcc2f590ac159df6d1b71d5608e98de591fb58d7270504552`; the unsigned release APK is 127,489,744 bytes with SHA-256 `c20d1b0022ffdddbc65d9655025974ee75523d843f5d176faa836e0859687b76`. The installed debug build cold-starts on the Pixel 7 Pro Android 17/API 37 emulator in 1.683 seconds, stays top-resumed, and produces no fatal crash log.

The AI conversation's provider and status lifecycle now match the current SwiftUI authority rather than only its capsule geometry. The provider capsule remains display-only and keeps the exact 8dp dot, typography, padding, border, and chevron. The active state now stores iOS' raw `正在思考中` copy so the shared capsule adds exactly one ellipsis, uses 70% ink, and applies the 70% gold base before the existing dot wave. Missing external configuration preserves the draft, opens configuration once, and auto-hides after four seconds; request failure and timeout also auto-hide while retaining localized recovery or partial reply data. Profile reflection still runs in the background but no longer exposes Android-only `正在沉淀到训练档案` or `已记录到档案` overlays that the active iOS page never presents. Nine focused AI-chrome tests pass on API 37. Evidence is `ai-chat-provider-status-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `0bcf794386fc3a85ecfb68502d8f9e020cb8e6f7358cb7e6cd7d5fd862307900`) and `ai-chat-status-timeout-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a8a14e79c8cbe07c3d2970af6df22c152cebbafc6b14767e0f5f4dc560112aa5`).

The same AI page now also follows iOS' default-standard reflection cadence instead of Android's former first-reply/every-fifth approximation. Only each fifth successfully completed reply triggers the periodic background reflection; timeout, failure, and cancellation do not. Leaving, switching conversations, or creating a new conversation reflects after one round for an empty golf profile or three rounds for an existing profile, captures the original conversation id before switching, and resets the new session's round boundary. Unit tests lock the standard/aggressive decision matrix, and the six-test API 37 history run verifies both switch/new handoffs and the reset boundary.

AI history loading now has the same recoverable posture as iOS instead of turning a storage exception into a false empty state. An ordinary load failure keeps the visible current conversation and history cards, presents release-safe localized guidance for four seconds, and leaves retry/navigation available; coroutine cancellation stays silent and preserves the same state. Its genuine zero-session fallback is now measured against the visible medium detent rather than the hidden full-sheet height, so the 32dp conversation glyph and `还没有对话记录` remain centered on screen without an Android-only CTA. The expanded nine-test history class passes on Pixel 7 Pro API 37 in both gesture and three-button navigation. Evidence is `ai-history-empty-gesture-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b9a013b1b62fae10a0d26fe264f0a564932828744854b0ee7a628ef83fce9f52`), `ai-history-empty-three-button-ios-parity.png` (`ac95d71767d063bba51a62345a25b72cb9b8c52f260e8ec021a6d2d6650c7430`), `ai-history-load-failure-gesture-ios-parity.png` (`8c581d1aa93c5e9dcc99ddc0fa8e6030ba35e472e7fab89963f10b888d6c7f71`), `ai-history-populated-gesture-ios-parity.png` (`92cf13db36ccd376b0dc5014a157cb3a0f731659108de4f34be389a9a152b830`), `ai-history-load-failure-three-button-ios-parity.png` (`133fa7917018ace7efae5aa68e79f0ebfe9c02fcf875118c656746c0db158424`), and `ai-history-populated-three-button-ios-parity.png` (`f66611c5359ea445773a728de3b1d48a9a9cc161244e1a69e31fd7b0b530178e`).

The root guest boundary now matches the active iOS `V2RootView` instead of allowing every bottom tab to open anonymously. Home and Tools remain directly readable, while Memo, Interaction, and Me preserve the selected page and present the role-specific iOS title/message in the shared `登录后解锁完整体验` card before entering login. The student root now mirrors iOS relationship-change notices in addition to unread teaching-content notices. A cold-start connection list only establishes the baseline; when an active connection becomes disconnected by the coach, Android chooses the newest real transition, preserves its null conversation target, and displays `已解除连接 / 历史交流仍可查看，当前空间已变为只读`. Tapping the 52dp gold-edged lock notice opens the connection's read-only history space, while the independent close action dismisses it; self-initiated disconnects do not raise a false notice. The root lifecycle now matches iOS' foreground session/update policy instead of validating only once after splash. Android observes real `ON_START` transitions after bootstrap, rechecks the update policy, silently refreshes authoritative account fields, and synchronizes the active role. Explicit deleted-account rejection presents the matching confirmation before returning to a clean guest Home, while timeout, offline, and other transport failures are deferred without logging the user out or clearing local content, matching `SessionManager.validateSession`. Four unit tests cover valid, nested invalid, and transient-failure classification plus the root source boundary; a Pixel 7 Pro API 37 lifecycle test proves bootstrap suppression, signed-in foreground delivery, and guest suppression.

Tencent IM session events now follow the active iOS root contract instead of relying on the next foreground request to discover them. Android registers the SDK listener once, raises the same blocking security alert immediately when another device takes over the account, and only clears local account state and returns Home after the user confirms. An expired UserSig is refreshed and reconnected silently; an explicitly deleted account uses the separate `账号已注销 / 返回首页` variant before cleanup. Two deterministic unit tests cover listener mapping and root ownership, and two Pixel 7 Pro API 37 tests cover both rendered variants and confirmation. Evidence is `root-kick-out-alert-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `fc84625ef1eb6fa8b1867c8a066aaccb17f21e90f77d63728f9e637349d16af0`).

C2C emoji input and display now use the same AtomicX/Tencent protocol as iOS instead of Android's former twelve unrelated Unicode characters. The composer exposes all 62 bundled TUI emoji in an inline 300dp, eight-column `最近使用 / 全部表情` panel with whole-attachment deletion and send controls, persists recent selections, keeps the raw `[TUIEmoji_*]` value for Tencent and draft interoperability, and renders the same TUI artwork in the inactive editor preview rather than an approximate system glyph. Received and translated text replaces recognized codes with the byte-matched TUI artwork while preserving unknown codes as text. All seventeen rich-message tests pass on Pixel 7 Pro API 37; evidence is `interaction-chat-emoji-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `923dcc0c42a2957b91fb8b5bde59e639ee87c1c7ad84ffa6c5ab9125153cc4c7`).

Received Tencent merged-forward messages now match iOS AtomicX instead of falling through to `[暂不支持的消息]`. Android recognizes the merger element, displays the 60%-width reciprocal card with title, four-line abstract limit, divider and `聊天记录` footer, localizes all recognized TUI codes in abstracts with the same 62 Simplified-Chinese EmojiFace names, labels the recent-conversation preview as `[聊天记录]`, and downloads the real submessages into the white iOS-style read-only detail page with sender identities, reactions, media opening, loading/empty/retry states and layer-depth protection. The user-visible source tree contains no current V2 group-chat entry on either platform, so no Android-only group surface was introduced. Pixel 7 Pro API 37 passed the current seventeen-test rich-message class and the focused card-to-detail run; evidence is `interaction-chat-merged-card-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `1e7033e2c765c60f0f0905d5584771f0990e76b8aa0656d1a8b31f31b0c63e34`) and `interaction-chat-merged-detail-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `2c7bbb850033c0ffdc3e6f5e244029b92877988f6f8b1473901d1259d5e19f81`).

The C2C voice composer now uses the same AtomicX hold-to-talk contract instead of Android's former tap-start/tap-finish control. A 200ms hold begins the real AAC recorder, a 20dp upward drag switches to cancellation, release sends or discards accordingly, sub-one-second clips are rejected with the matching copy, and the 60-second ceiling completes the recording. The overlay mirrors iOS' `向上滑动取消或松开发送 / 松开取消发送` text, `MM:SS` timer, fairway/red capsule and live microphone-amplitude waveform. API 37 proves short-tap rejection, the hold threshold, upward cancellation, and the rendered panel; evidence is `interaction-chat-voice-hold-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `1efbfaa49a33d7d56a35ad596c766505c5df6860e49ee3bc46175f773b2f090a`).

Received C2C voice messages now also follow iOS AtomicX inside the conversation bubble. Android replaces the old `语音消息 / N秒` row and full-screen black media preview with an inline play/pause control, eight-bar animated waveform, live `MM:SS` timer, completion reset, and shared playback ownership so starting one recording pauses the previous one. The focused behavior test proves that bubble playback never enters the full-screen media route, and the complete seventeen-test rich-message class passes on Pixel 7 Pro API 37. Evidence is `interaction-chat-voice-bubble-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `cbcbe97e46016daca7212f3ffa1f367a5b913e1ed9cadbac15f475f7a6119d3b`). Voice-to-text remains intentionally unavailable because the Android Tencent IM SDK used by this project does not expose iOS AtomicX's speech-recognition capability.

C2C file messages now follow iOS AtomicX's real download-and-preview lifecycle rather than sending every remote URL directly to an external app. The bubble uses the matching one-line filename, adaptive iOS `ByteCountFormatter`-style size—including real `B` values below 1 KB instead of a false `0 KB`—per-extension document/table/presentation/archive/text/image/audio/video glyphs, and an outer download action on the recipient/sender side. The same formatter keeps teaching-space voice drafts aligned with SwiftUI. Tencent's `downloadFile` operation writes through a partial file into the private `chat_media/files` cache, reports progress, rejects empty or truncated results, reuses valid cached files after reload, and only then opens the correct MIME type through the private FileProvider. API 37 verified incoming/outgoing/local and active-download states as part of the complete seventeen-test rich-message class; evidence is `interaction-chat-file-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `cff0615c02b03028d5be7241d9bc86fc292abb3a7df1dd5811092f01ba5c2fe2`). The real authenticated cross-device download remains in the existing Tencent IM device-QA boundary.

C2C empty and outbound delivery states now follow AtomicX instead of Android's former centered `开始聊天` placeholder and post-failure-only banner. A new text, image, video, voice, or file appears immediately as a 70%-opacity bubble with the iOS secondary spinner; Tencent success replaces that local item, while failure retains the original payload beside the red status icon. Tapping it shows the exact `确定重发此消息吗? ` confirmation and reconstructs the corresponding real Tencent send operation. SDK `SENDING / SEND_FAIL` and `hasRiskContent()` states are also mapped when history is loaded, with the matching `涉及敏感内容` hint. The loaded zero-message page leaves the branded backdrop clear like iOS. All seventeen rich-message tests pass on Pixel 7 Pro API 37; evidence is `interaction-chat-send-states-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b989ebfd942041a111e4e6cda41ce9cf07c5abc394590608335591a1020ecb46`) and `interaction-chat-empty-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `564ed9c5c565db2eef68baa6db7ccb0eaa26e641c5718f70ad366496f6a2376c`).

C2C history loading now matches AtomicX's real paging contract instead of stopping permanently at Android's former latest-50 snapshot. The first request asks Tencent for 20 messages; reaching the visual top requests the previous page from the oldest raw message cursor, preserves the visible keyed row, translations and pending local sends, and keeps polling additions without discarding already loaded history. Initial loading stays visually quiet for 0.5 seconds, then shows the same centered `loading...` treatment, with a three-second callback timeout guard. The pagination merge has deterministic unit coverage and the complete seventeen-test chat class passes on API 37; loading evidence is `interaction-chat-loading-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b7fcdcc2a93f1e8db7a96bb7dce3f6abddcd3a84390aea49bc4102c7c837b3ba`).

2026-10-07 continuation: `V2ShareMemoToSpacesScreen` now follows the iOS `sourceURL != nil` action boundary, so a Memo with neither an existing local file nor a remote URL cannot enter a doomed upload. Its error/completion banners use the matching leading icons, tint, typography, and opacity; selected recipients, optional note, stable request ids, and result messages are saveable. API 37 recreated the real page and retained the selected recipient, note, and enabled send count. `V2StudentSpaceSendConfirmScreen` also replaces its centered Material cellular alert with the same bottom-group hierarchy produced by iOS `confirmationDialog`, and its busy primary action keeps the source-specific label beside the spinner. Refreshed API 37 evidence is `student-space-send-cellular-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `1fcd5701c47078162fed83369a8f6b34b6273d0ab5df8840a0e271c3dfb568db`). The workspace disconnect path now follows the same iOS bottom destructive action hierarchy, chooses `解除好友空间？ / 解除学员连接？ / 解除与教练的连接？` from relationship and perspective, and switches to the exact draft-deletion warning only when a root or reply draft really exists. Thread draft clearing and root/reply deletion now mirror the iOS `confirmationDialog` bottom action hierarchy through one shared component, preserve the exact title/message/action variants, and disable both dismissal surfaces during an active deletion. The full local gate now passes 1034 unit tests; the accumulated API 37 inventory is 133.

The workspace header now follows the actually rendered SwiftUI `workspaceHeader`, not the older supplied mock: the extra peer avatar and explicit refresh button are gone, back/overflow are equal white circles, the relationship title and `教学空间 / 好友空间` subtitle are geometrically centered, the menu uses a horizontal ellipsis, and the switcher remains available with one connection. The current iOS `V2AllMemoListView` is now mirrored as an actual mixed chronological stream. `practicePage/contentSection` preserves the current SwiftUI single chronological timeline once content exists, but its connected zero-state now follows the user-supplied guided design. `spaceStatusStack` and the quick-post completion transition are now aligned too. Text, uploaded video, cellular-confirmed video, and teaching-material success paths display the source-aware delivered message, wait 220ms, open the newly created discussion, and clear the banner at three seconds. The `contentSection` title controls now match the active SwiftUI semantics and geometry. Android replaces the two text pills with the 22sp title/count row, a 32dp `line.3.horizontal.decrease`-equivalent menu label, explicit `最新在前 / 最早在前` entries with check/clock state, and the 32dp circular `rectangle.grid.1x2 / list.bullet`-equivalent toggle. The saved `comfortable / compact` and `newest / oldest` values remain keyed by perspective and connection. The historical-space and cursor-pagination branches now match the rendered SwiftUI behavior. `WorkspaceReadOnlyBanner` composes the disconnection actor from `disconnectedByUserId`, the signed-in account and peer name, then renders the lock-led 12sp medium mist card. `LazyColumn` pagination no longer exposes an Android-only manual button: the last visible root item triggers `loadMoreFeed`, the repository cursor is retained, merged history is preserved, and only the centered fairway progress ring appears while the next page loads. Unit coverage locks self/peer/unknown actor variants plus the automatic pagination source boundary; API 37 evidence is `workspace-read-only-banner-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `7873dd3e58aa1fe5d7cee059c43d5e56c6c79ae5197765da45c6063958ceb87f`).

The discussion page now preserves the same live-reading boundary as SwiftUI `discussionTab`. Android tracks whether the reader is within 96dp of the thread bottom, automatically follows replies sent by the signed-in user, but does not jump or mark a newly arrived peer reply as viewed while older messages are being read. Instead it displays the matching fairway `新回复 / N 条新回复` capsule; tapping it animates to the newest reply, clears the counter, and only then records the thread as viewed. Initial unread conversations still reveal their latest reply. The resources model also retains every source-message id instead of collapsing a saved asset to an untraceable card: one reference returns directly to its bubble, repeated references expose `回到第 N 次引用`, and quoted-message cards can now bring non-root targets into view as well. API 37 evidence is `workspace-thread-new-replies-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `d9a086867501c423afc78857c8a342a07ade44f2ad19a6175e3c84e56a618c5f`) and `workspace-thread-resource-return-menu-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `c9035269514544543ea2a544dbf9fb8493bb478cc8295aa4eb5f2d40f9ac9b6c`).

Thread bootstrap, recovery, and reply history now follow the active iOS branches as well. The content tab renders the known source item immediately instead of hiding it behind a generic spinner; discussion loading uses `正在同步交流记录…`, first-load failure keeps the source and draft while exposing the gold retry card, and resources use their own organizing state. Thread synchronization errors are isolated from composer/media errors so periodic checks cannot overwrite an actionable send failure. Android now also carries the backend `nextCursor`, sends it without first-page version fingerprints, merges older replies chronologically without losing optimistic local sends, advances the paging cursor, and shows the iOS `加载更早的回复` action at the top. API 37 evidence is `workspace-thread-sync-recovery-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `9cb7de6b71bfc342c2b21f8105b60da64d24b17693183ee1e0e6144c71d6096a`) and `workspace-thread-load-older-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a5b02abdd2b978d133de0f82e9362d495c93a994fb5f57b9b1876ae4eaf9623c`).

Conversation-video saving now matches iOS' complete result flow instead of silently changing only local state. After `保存到私有素材`, Android marks every matching root/reply asset as saved, refreshes the thread so the `资料` tab receives the authoritative server state, and presents the exact `素材保存结果 / 已保存到私有素材，并同步到本页“资料”。 / 知道了` alert. Failures keep the discussion open and use the same retry-later fallback as SwiftUI. The reply composer now protects an existing main video with the same SwiftUI replacement policy. A normal second selection opens the exact bottom `替换当前视频？` confirmation; keeping the current video deletes only the unused candidate, while confirmation replaces it without touching already related teaching material. When a coach finishes a new `analysis_recording`, the previous non-analysis main video is automatically demoted to related media when one of the three slots remains. Album/camera/analysis selections now return directly to the draft instead of forcing the optional trimmer, and leaving that trimmer through `不裁剪` keeps the attached original rather than deleting it. API 37 evidence is `workspace-primary-video-replacement-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `f845d8b566ec40d753123b939b1261cb296a65f77c4cb63f0ddb82d2bea531c1`).

Voice replies now follow the active iOS hold-to-talk and draft-first contract instead of Android's former tap-to-start/tap-to-send shortcut. The input shows the exact `按住说话 / 正在请求麦克风权限… / 松开发送 · 上滑取消 / 松开取消` states, starts on touch-down, switches to cancellation above the 52dp threshold, and only finishes on release. A valid recording becomes the single primary draft attachment with the waveform row and `发送时上传` state; it is sent only when the main `发送` action is pressed, participates in replacement protection, persists with a nullable cover path across the 24-hour account/thread-scoped draft, and cannot be saved accidentally as a teaching video. The text placeholder now also resolves to `回复好友… / 回复学员… / 回复教练…` like SwiftUI. API 37 verifies the rendered voice draft and the real upward-cancel gesture in `workspace-thread-voice-draft-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a5edc375959fca8ff9c15028267ff04bff7582b2afca64732e8ccadecd65ac6f`).

Composer-local feedback now stays with the fixed input area like SwiftUI instead of appearing in the scrollable discussion body. Android shows the exact restored-draft notice, media-added and cover-updated transient notices, delivered-recipient summary with voice/video suffixes, the persistent `当前离线，回复已保留，网络恢复后可发送` row, reconnect feedback, and the dismissible `这次操作没有完成` recovery card; queued-delivery recovery continues to take precedence. The thread's large-media confirmation also uses the iOS bottom action group and exact `使用蜂窝网络发送？ / 继续发送 / 暂不发送` contract instead of Android's centered upload dialog, and successful retry/cellular approval emits the normal delivered notice. API 37 verifies these states in `workspace-composer-notices-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `37587f65ca9327c4f8b3173781533bd8a5bfa7079743f8dbc5c455f8735274d0`) and `workspace-thread-cellular-send-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `830ebb182aac07a58807e888e2cfd00d6ddb2e185aee642ec82aa11698fdd3fa`). Persisted voice media now has behavioral coverage proving that an audio draft restores without inventing a cover while an ordinary video with a missing cover remains rejected.

Coach reply delivery now also follows iOS' background-queue acceptance boundary. Metered-media confirmation happens before queue ownership so cancelling leaves the editable draft intact; after acceptance, text, video, voice, and related media transfer atomically to the persistent queue, the composer clears immediately, and the exact attachment-aware `已加入发送队列…` notice appears. Foreground delivery continues independently while the same thread stays editable, with the `已有内容正在发送，这条可继续加入队列` row replacing Android's blocking progress editor; student delivery retains its current-task progress and stop affordance. Network transitions into cellular remain recoverable through the same confirmation. API 37 evidence is `workspace-coach-queue-composer-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `1d5de8c4c203fabc4ead799d3e183a017be95c7a6e80795f7302fdf29b3422b7`).

The reply draft's related-material strip now matches SwiftUI's `relatedMaterialStrip` rather than stopping at static Android thumbnails. It renders the exact `补充视频` count, 92×58dp cards, play/expanded/loading states, removal, a dashed `再加一条` card below the three-item ceiling, and an in-place Media3 preview with the same 1.25 minimum display ratio. Missing signed library video URLs trigger an authoritative library refresh and retain the selection with iOS' retry-later copy. The unreachable centered `添加补充视频` alert and Android-only related-album multi-picker are removed; current iOS adds further related items directly from the private library, while a replaced primary local video can still be demoted into the strip. Material cards carry selected borders, `#` tags, use counts, duration badges, plus/minus/full states and real in-place Media3 preview; multi-select uses the matching selected summary, capacity hint and fixed `已选择 N 个素材 / 完成选择` action. ### iOS source of truth

- The current iOS source is `/Users/huxiaoran/Documents/xiaoranProjects/MyGolfSwingApp` at HEAD `e51fe9c9` (`挥杆录制改用手机麦克风，避免预览输出把声音丢掉。挥杆详情整页滑入，播放器不再跟着底部导航改高度。`) plus four reviewed local product edits: `MyGolfSwingApp/V2/Views/Memo/V2MemoRecordBrowser.swift`, `MyGolfSwingApp/V2/Views/Memo/V2MemoSwingDayList.swift`, `MyGolfSwingApp/Views/JustSwing/JustSwingClipDetailView.swift`, and `MyGolfSwingApp/Views/SwingAnalysis/VideoAnalysisPlayerView.swift`. Their exact bytes are pinned in `docs/ios-parity-dirty-files.sha256`.
- The `03aabbac..e51fe9c9` delta is synchronized on Android: daily-practice capture requires microphone permission, records through CameraX with audio enabled, and now inspects the saved file for an audio track before reporting the same `视频已保存，但没有录到声音，请再录一次` recovery message. Swing detail is a root destination outside `TabsScaffold`, so it covers the bottom navigation at full height; Home, Memo, all-Memo, calendar, imported analysis, capture, and local-access entry points all use that shared destination. The frame/review players retain reciprocal switching and one close callback. Dedicated source tests guard both the audio contract and root-level presentation.
- The current `V2RootView.swift` was reviewed at content hash `a41417a54593af36ca40beba2c0ba4c4c1e9b30d21be0426117a890f9b6e8954`. In addition to the previously reviewed root helpers and `v2OpenStudentConversation` route, it now owns the root-level `V2SwingClipPresenter` overlay described above; both behaviors have Android counterparts.
- The iOS app target currently declares `MARKETING_VERSION = 2.6.1`. Android now uses the same default display version (`versionName = 2.6.1`) while retaining the last locally verified `versionCode = 2`; the next signed Play build must inject a versionCode higher than the real Play Console value rather than guessing that external state locally.
- Android's previous snapshot stopped at iOS `9b99c57d`; the committed delta adds unified account sessions, optional-profile onboarding, account/security polish, dual student/coach identities, private coach invitations, the coach dashboard, student management, review queue, reusable teaching library, and daily-practice/player improvements.
- The logged-out Me tab now follows the current iOS read-only guest experience instead of showing an account-shaped page with zero statistics: it presents the three-image preview strip, browse-first journey, readable-versus-login-required capability columns, account value, explicit no-write boundary, and guest support/legal list. Plaza/Memo journey actions switch tabs directly, account-writing actions enter login, and terms/privacy remain readable without an account. The `HomepageAnalysis` preview uses the byte-identical iOS artwork and the complete screen was exercised top-to-bottom in the side-by-side emulator package without clipping or a runtime crash.
- The logged-out AI destination now follows the current iOS preview-first journey instead of stopping at a generic login banner. It presents the same three visual capability tiles and sample questions, lets a guest place a question into an editable local draft, shows the draft/login boundary, and requests login only for send, history, or config actions. Its top subtitle now uses the exact guest-specific `示例问答 · 发送前登录 · …` hierarchy; the provider/model pill remains display-only like the current iOS call site, and the composer retains the send glyph rather than introducing Android's former red stop-generation state. API 37 verifies the full guest page in `ai-chat-guest-chrome-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `9343604a94b695369634075a7fd38a70cbe39a07ea8665010ae22ab4f9fdba9a`).
- The logged-out Memo tab now follows iOS' preview-first sample experience instead of showing an account-shaped empty library. Android renders the same three video-note samples, three text-note samples, and four-clip practice-day preview without inserting them into Room; the guest-only AI insight is hidden, header/footer totals stay at six Memo records like iOS, and Home/Video/Text/All sample cards open a read-only `Memo 预览` sheet backed by the exact imported quick-add artwork. The preview Hero now also matches the current Swift hierarchy: thumbnail-theme, diagonal green, and bottom-black gradient layers; the translucent `视频片段 / 文字复盘` card; the overlapping white `动作要点 / 练习目标` card; video/text-specific symbols; and the real relative-date fallback. Its account boundary uses the same person-key and shield-lock meanings, and the login callback is dispatched before dismissal like iOS. New/edit/save/goal-link and swing-history actions remain login-gated. Guest Quick Add cards now also carry iOS-style lock badges and show the selected action's exact explanation before offering `返回 / 登录注册`; choosing login preserves the requested goal/video/text/swing destination for post-auth continuation. API 37 verifies both preview variants in `guest-memo-video-preview-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `e80def0c9355623e8b09aa4f37edabcc012a9d73a49162d34912519496a8bf8b`) and `guest-memo-text-preview-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `30fb1eeb34fc18240309e6c7c422d095e2278d477badb68966fcdb0ebace20b3`). The side-by-side emulator also exercised the main sample page, expanded preview sheet, login CTA, guest video list, locked Quick Add sheet, explanatory dialog, and login handoff without clipping or a runtime crash.
- Profile editing and post-login profile completion now use the same blue/pink gender-symbol artwork as iOS instead of Android's earlier text-only choices. The original iOS SVG bytes are retained in Android and guarded by the cross-platform asset map, while lossless 1024-point transparent PNG renderings preserve the artwork in the Android runtime at iOS' 22-point compact size. The completion flow also switches to iOS' 64-point vertical icon/title cards and allows an optional selection to be cleared; ordinary profile editing keeps the 42-point horizontal layout and treats a blank saved value as `保密`. The signed-in profile summary now also renders the matching 18-point symbol beside the username—even when the username is absent—and exposes the same `男性 / 女性` accessibility meaning as iOS.
- Video Memo fallback covers now follow iOS' complete six-theme `V2MemoCoverAsset` mapping instead of reusing one generic grass image: fairway, green-grass, sunset, sand, mist, and forest resolve respectively to the same quick-add video, quick-add swing, analysis, quick-add goal, login-course, and `GolfSwingHero` artwork. The byte-identical 7.6MB iOS PNG is retained as audit authority while Android renders a lossless WebP conversion, cutting roughly 3.8MB from the install package without changing pixels. Android only attempts frame extraction for local file/content references like iOS; remote, Mock, and guest videos with no real thumbnail move directly to their theme photo, while local videos still prefer their real first frame. The side-by-side emulator rendered the guest Memo rail with distinct fairway and green-grass covers, preserved card cropping/navigation, and emitted no runtime crash.
- The new iOS commit includes the tracked backend files `CloudFunctions/README.md`, `CoachWorkspaceSecurity.test.js`, `CloudFunctions/coachWorkspace/index.js`, and `CloudFunctions/videoPlaza/index.js`; the last file preserves the original default-iOS update contract while adding a separate, disabled-by-default Android policy document.
- Its coach client scope includes `CoachDesignPreview.swift`, `CoachHomeView.swift`, `CoachRootView.swift`, `CoachWorkspaceService.swift`, `CoachWorkspaceStore.swift`, `CoachWorkspaceModels.swift`, `CoachLibraryView.swift`, `CoachReviewWorkspaceView.swift`, `CoachStudentsView.swift`, and `StudentSpaceView.swift`.
- Its student interaction/player scope includes `V2StudentInteractionView.swift`, `V2VideoMemoPlayerView.swift`, `MyGolfSwingAppTests.swift`, and the new `V2ShareToSpacesSheet.swift`.
- The committed `docs/coach-student-space-page-review.md` acceptance matrix defines the cross-role release review order and the R/S/C/X page-level pass criteria for identity, relationship, collaboration, workspace, library, and student-only surfaces.
- The committed `PageBack1.imageset`, `PageBack2.imageset`, and `PageBack3.imageset` artwork is active product UI, not merely visual reference. Android now bundles byte-identical `drawable-nodpi` copies and maps the three scenes to the same semantic surfaces described below.

### Android synchronization now in progress

- The Memo root now uses iOS' unified `我的记录` browser instead of Android's retired three-section landing page. It defaults to `挥杆记录`, keeps the same search/filter/month-calendar hierarchy, switches the primary action to `拍摄 / 添加 / 写一条` with matching glyph and accessibility copy, and routes each action into its real capture/video/note flow. Its search control now follows the active Swift render tree exactly: a 44dp white filled row with 14dp corners and no Android outline, rather than reusing the taller shared Material outlined field. It now also follows the latest iOS day-alignment rule: opening the page selects the most recent recorded day and shows its month; after that it returns to today only when a genuinely newer clip is captured today, so browsing an older date is not interrupted by unrelated storage changes. The current iOS day-list behavior is matched as well: calendar day rows use the same 32dp cell with 8dp grid spacing, an empty selected date retains its date header and optional `今天` badge above `这天没有挥杆记录`, cross-year headers include the year, search groups remain newest-day-first, and clips within every day are explicitly oldest-time-first. Swing clips persist an optional 40-character心得 in the existing backward-compatible session JSON, use its first line as the card title, include it in search, expose the same `写一句心得 / 修改心得` editor, and optimistically roll back on storage failure. The current dirty-iOS card refinement is also matched: its 12dp pencil floats in the 28dp top-right circle instead of consuming title-row width, the title reserves 32dp beneath it, and the compact editor focuses immediately, uses the same 14dp outer rhythm with a 4dp title/subtitle gap, 44dp plain white field, persistent 1dp border-line stroke, 12sp medium count, and navigation-safe bottom action rather than a Material outlined field. Video notes now use iOS' 120×84 cover, cover-duration badge, source/date line, two-tag limit, one-line note, and conditional `视频待恢复` row; text notes use the green leading rule, full short-body display, the same 120-character/four-line collapse and expand behavior, relative date, two-tag footer, and `更多` route. Their searches cover the same title/body/source/tag fields and distinguish a true empty library from a no-results query. The clip-detail toolbar also switches between the existing frame-analysis controls and the full iOS-style standalone analysis player with reciprocal `切换到回看 / 切换到逐帧` actions. API 37 verifies the browser filters, dynamic actions, 44dp filled search row, and empty-day hierarchy in `memo-record-browser-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `6e4d38ce21ec22b1a098c8410ca6ee41bd8390b9e7c5f97843814096ac172b1b`), the two content-card hierarchies in `memo-video-text-cards-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `d729c86308b6e767926017f3de5544d1f5edf0851883c46f15f7ca3990c31221`), the refined swing card in `memo-swing-card-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `d887307b357ea6916d9d5d3fffa57d44c77b8e37b8e7517ebebd2c274a16f6d7`), and the focused editor in `memo-swing-note-editor-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `9ecc607190bbcf790d8a334c82f975e964a24f0cc4e947456a8320776495933b`); all captures are 1440×3120.
- Memo `我的记录` now also matches the iOS scroll ownership: the title/dynamic create action, 44dp search row, and three-way filter capsule live in the fixed outer stack, while only the calendar/cards/empty results scroll below them. Record-stack rhythm now follows Swift's 12dp swing-list spacing and 10dp video/text `LazyVStack` spacing. A missing swing thumbnail/first frame remains the same plain deep-green surface as iOS instead of showing an Android-only golf glyph. The guest privacy boundary now matches `V2MemoView` as well: Android forces Memo and swing-session inputs to empty lists at the render boundary whenever logged out, so an asynchronous logout cleanup cannot briefly expose the previous account's in-memory snapshot; all three create actions still route only to login. A same-composition API 37 test first renders seeded prior-account swing/video/text records while authenticated, changes only the login state, verifies all three records disappear, and verifies `拍摄 / 添加 / 写一条` issue three login requests and zero create callbacks. Delete title/body and the timestamp-with-seconds details row also use the exact current iOS copy. `切换到回看` no longer opens the native Android controller player: it now enters the shared full-screen analysis player with screenshot saving, screen-plus-microphone recording and preview, video comparison, horizontal flip, pose, selfie, the complete drawing kit, three real scrub-feel modes, white custom timeline/frame stepping/speed controls, and reciprocal `切换到逐帧`; standalone recordings save through the existing gallery pipeline. The current `稳 / 细 / 帧` controls now apply iOS' exact velocity-sensitive drag-density and inertia presets instead of only changing the selected chip; the ruler uses fixed 160pt-per-second visual spacing, red/white major ticks, fading edges, pause-on-drag, and bounded fling. Its hundredth-second bubble follows the absolute progress slider, and the 126×178 front-camera bubble uses the same iOS safe drag bounds plus a visible move handle. Its 142dp menu is ordered `分享到空间 / 系统分享 / 导出 / 对比 / 分类 / 删除` with matching dividers. The signed-in `自定义工具栏` sheet follows iOS' centered navigation title, exact optional subtitles, grouped white list, colored switches, and functional long-press drag handles. Its saved switches and order now drive the actual discovery row immediately and after fresh composition instead of being a write-only preference. Six focused API 37 tests verify all four quick-add routes and their guest continuations, every tool callback, a real reorder gesture, hide/restore behavior, and fresh-composition persistence; current evidence is `home-tool-shortcuts-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `62b6ea372b8e968f286a8071b9c3e9281ca57038694df0a9a40c01a0cd466257`) and `home-tool-config-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b9e4fde881dad00e3a37beb78bef61af232d537533c1bbeab2eed3ef0548d7ef`). The Plaza categories/search/two-column cards/load-more path all use the existing cloud-backed store rather than static placeholders, and its loading indicator is centered; selecting a category now exits search like iOS, leaving Home clears shared search state, and Home pagination waits until the user approaches the bottom instead of recursively loading every page at first composition. Shared Home/Plaza video cards display immediately, then progressively resolve the current coach profile, strip iOS' leading `Coach ` label, render the remote coach avatar with a letter-gradient fallback, and preserve that avatar through saved-video and watch-history caches. Pull-to-refresh is available on both Home and Plaza, invalidates the coach cache so changed names/avatars can appear, and Home also refreshes the full student unread/connection snapshot like iOS. The shared feature Hero keeps iOS' fixed 200dp card rhythm without Android-only vertical padding. The side-by-side emulator verified the guest avatar, ring, greeting, search field, tool row, live Plaza Hero/grid covers, normalized coach name, remote coach avatar, cache-refresh network path, and bounded initial pagination without a runtime crash.
- A source-level re-audit corrected the previous comparison against the dormant `V2HomeToolBar`: current `V2HomeView` mounts `V2HomeToolShortcutRow`. Android matches its 72dp item width, 52dp circle, 8dp spacing, 16dp section inset, 30dp raster / 18dp symbol geometry, 11sp labels, and 14dp guest lock; `更多` preserves the same guest login gate and signed-in configuration sheet. Android additionally completes the sheet's intended saved-configuration contract: enabled tools and their order are applied to this row and restored on a fresh composition. API 37 verifies horizontal scrolling, More, all six callbacks, real drag persistence, switches, and recreation; current evidence hashes are recorded above.

Android searches text and mixed Memo title/body fields case-insensitively, excludes video-only Memo records like iOS' merged source, sorts matches newest-first, preserves the exact private-source subtitle, and opens the selected record. Active teaching-space filtering, newest unread root selection, deleted-copy filtering, coach avatar fallback, and the exact `connectionId + itemId` route remain intact. - The new Android `挥呗空间` overview reads real student/friend connections, filters coach vs friend spaces, previews coach invitations, and accepts an invite transactionally before surfacing the newly connected space.
- Student space cards now mirror the iOS overview semantics more closely: active connections sort by pin, latest conversation/reply activity, and then unread count as an exact-time tie-breaker; recent-item timestamps override stale relationship timestamps, invalid dates fall to the bottom, and connection id stabilizes exact ties. Each card now shows relationship type, latest title, reply-aware preview, relative activity time, optional latest-video cover, and a dedicated `N 条新互动` route that opens the authoritative unread discussion while the rest of the card still opens the space overview. Learners can persist pin changes; disconnected relationships live in a separately labeled read-only history section with the disconnecting party explained.
- Coach invitations now expose the full current lifecycle instead of only copying a code: Android uses the latest iOS-style full-page invitation surface with QR/code modes, an actual scannable QR image, gallery save, system sharing, code copy, learner-benefit guidance, and accepted/expired/loading/error states. `保存二维码` now composes the byte-identical 576×1024 `CoachInvitePoster` artwork into the same 1728×3072 output as iOS, replaces only the measured QR region, uses high error correction, and overlays the centered `挥呗` mark; an API 37 test decodes the finished branded poster back to the exact invite URL. It shows the single-use expiry, polls `getInviteStatus` while the page is open, updates immediately after acceptance, and can generate another invitation without reopening the page. The learner side is no longer a generic alert: it is the current RoomBackground full page with 8-character normalization, coach preview/verification/expiry/privacy states, CameraX live scanning, torch control, gallery recognition, private-token deep-link handling, and explicit confirmation before connection. Its modal chrome now also follows iOS exactly: leading text `取消`, centered 16sp title, coach-account glyph, and page dismissal for both that action and Android system back; only the explicit `使用其他连接码` action resets a previewed invite. CameraX remains the live decoder, but the screen now uses the 68%-of-short-edge fairway scan window, 18dp corner radius, 45% cutout mask, vertical -36dp optical offset, safe-area-aware 40dp close/torch controls, 17sp title, instruction capsule, and compact `相册` action from `V2StudentInviteScannerView`. Invalid camera/gallery results use the `提示 / 确定` recovery, and a denied camera permission shows `需要相机权限才能扫码 / 去设置` with a real app-settings route instead of repeatedly requesting permission; the friend scanner retains its existing layout. - Connected student/friend spaces now load the real paged feed and conversation thread contract, mark opened content as viewed, and support server-request-ID text posts plus text replies. Read-only disconnected spaces retain their history.
- Coach professional analysis now carries the iOS comparison workflow through the Android reply path: a local comparison video is size/storage checked, trimmed, and opened in a real split or 58%-overlay player with independent/linked seeking, preserved time offset, independent/linked playback, five playback rates, shared mute, drawing tools, and safe replacement cleanup. Its top controls now also match iOS' actionable set: the current top-video frame saves to `Pictures/Huibei`, screen recording can run with or without microphone narration, recording exposes pause/resume/finish/cancel states plus the same `MM:SS` elapsed timer (frozen while paused and accumulated after resume), and destructive back asks before discarding. A finished recording is held until the coach explicitly uses it in the existing trim/cover/reliable teaching-reply draft, saves a copy to `Movies/Huibei`, or discards it, so neither navigation nor asynchronous cleanup can silently remove the file. Modern Android uses scoped-storage saving; API 26–28 uses app-owned Pictures/Movies directories plus media scanning without requesting broad external-storage access. The API 37 device test exercises normal and active-recording comparison controls against two real playable files.
- Both perspectives can now explicitly disconnect an active coach/student or friend relationship from the shared space header. Android blocks the destructive action while reliable deliveries are pending, uses the same `disconnectConnection` backend transition, clears account-scoped drafts for that space only after success, then moves the relationship into read-only history without removing earlier teaching conversations.
- Coach student cards, dashboard activity, and review-queue items now open the same server-backed space/thread contract. A coach can enter a learner's history, open the queued conversation, mark it viewed, publish a text follow-up, and reply in-thread, completing the first bidirectional teaching loop.
