Evidence is `golfcam-landing-current-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b65aa2e7b160bd41affc16ee94bf91396541319ff08927da299e75f2d2daf04f`) plus `golfcam-landing-current-ios-parity.mp4` (H.264 720×1280, SHA-256 `3cf63d3e66916f3cb1069d7397023858826a6c6610f65c333f056d3430c6f191`).
- GolfCam step 5 now follows the active V2 `FlightCurveStepTool` and final-track preview rather than the older normalized-only control range and helper-line treatment. Android uses the exact 126dp edge-to-edge panel, source hint, 50dp full-width timeline with overlaid 50dp gradient frame controls, ceiling-step sampling capped at 60 cells, and commit-on-scroll-end behavior. P1/P2 share iOS' extended x `-0.35...1.35` / y `-0.8...1.4` range, independent 88dp hit targets, 24dp markers and drag haptics; the editor renders only the final default-style trajectory, without dashed control helpers. Curve entry starts at 0.86 zoom with a 0.72...4.0 pinch range, reset action, eye toggle, and 0.32s long-press recovery copy. Curve and current preview frame are persisted with the same 80ms coalescing boundary. API 37 loads a real MP4, drags P1, scrolls and persists the timeline frame, toggles/reveals the curve, performs two-finger zoom/reset and jumps to the impact frame without changing upstream selections. Evidence is `golfcam-flight-curve-current-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `2f1d0d50c57532efe108269d247a03e44046857e41c1764d2000e07547f81943`) plus `golfcam-flight-curve-current-ios-parity.mp4` (H.264 720×1280, 16.58s, SHA-256 `3f46e754714a2cf54f8e584e51381eebf60dd56733275b59ae7b9475232c3273`).
- Teaching-material cards now open the current iOS `CoachLibraryDetailView` instead of handing the video URL to an external Android app. The full-screen RoomBackground destination has centered chevron/menu chrome, aspect-ratio-aware inline Media3 playback, complete title/category/reuse/duration/date metadata, matching scene and latest-teaching-note cards, deduplicated newest-first recent recipients with sent/viewed states and expansion, draft-aware send action, edit action, and safe library-removal confirmation. Media presentation first uses the asset dimensions, then adopts Media3's real decoded video geometry when available; it preserves iOS' 0.2 safety floor without Android's former 0.35–2.4 crop-like clamp, so portrait and ultra-wide assets retain their true shape. Missing or non-playable media now follows the shared cover/video-frame/deep-green golf-placeholder fallback rather than Android's former black playback tile. The overflow action uses the matching trash glyph, and removal opens the iOS-style bottom destructive sheet with the exact history-safe copy instead of a centered Material dialog. The existing share/edit mutations stay connected, and a loaded signed playback asset refreshes the in-memory library card. Three API 37 tests verify the landscape hierarchy, recipient expansion, both footer callbacks, overflow/removal actions, the bottom confirmation hierarchy, and a true 9:16 portrait stage; evidence is `coach-library-detail-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `db680c9c04cc7de4bcc2c93244c312e01a99b93bc7499cbbe38d1daa8010de30`), `coach-library-remove-confirm-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `d1ac08633db79bc088686a11a4b285d5769945d3d954f36cb5041e235daca5b5`), and `coach-library-detail-portrait-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `3a86cb9932d11de8a02fb160cda7730b2cee7fad7607bcd357e14c4eac1f715f`).
- Material editing now follows the full-screen iOS `CoachLibraryEditView` rather than Android's former two-field Material alert. It uses the shared RoomBackground, centered modal chrome, current-cover preview, visible `50`-character title guideline without Android-only input truncation, removable five-tag flow with the same list-card strokes and dashed 62dp creation control, retry card that preserves all typed values, real update mutation, and the same history-safe remove semantics. Detail and edit now share one iOS-style bottom destructive sheet, including exact title, explanatory copy, red action, separated cancel action, scrim, and navigation-safe spacing. Cover recovery follows Swift's order: a valid cover, a generated local or remote video frame, then the deep-green/fairway golf placeholder; retry presentation uses the exact coach gold/muted-gold tokens. The library grid and detail page both enter this shared editor and refresh the in-memory asset after loading or saving. Three API 37 tests cover tag removal/addition, exact save payloads including an over-guideline title, fixed retry copy, and destructive confirmation; `coach-library-edit-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `85679a7553af1364354667c71bb9efba650df09a15aa20efa1d64609c57118da`.
- Edit Goal now preserves iOS' mutation semantics instead of only matching its form: changing the visible cadence subvalue is persisted, legacy day-streak data is normalized through the current weekly cadence UI, and changing a target re-evaluates active/completed status while preserving paused/archived goals. Form drafts, pickers, and the delete confirmation survive Android saved-state recreation; a missing edit target can no longer fall through into accidental goal creation. Six API 37 edit/create tests cover save, cadence, deletion, restoration, the missing-target boundary, and continuous-goal normalization.
- The current complete gate passes all 1138 Android unit tests with zero failures/errors/skips; the Android-test source contains 428 cases, including the fresh eight-test Home analysis recovery suite, three-test API 37 source-sheet run, two-test real-camera analysis/ordinary recording run, six-test GolfCam source-recovery suite, two-test API 37 GolfCam source-sheet run, six-test Home quick-add/tool run, six-test Credits Reset run, two-test low-credit download run, nine-test Credits Purchase run, six-test Swing Clip Detail run, and refreshed teaching-space empty-state proof. Release lint has zero errors, and fresh debug/release APK assembly succeeds. The Android/iOS audit remains current at iOS HEAD `e51fe9c9`, marketing version `2.6.1`, four verified dirty parity files, 35 current coach actions, and 29 referenced/runtime visual assets. The debug APK is 168,830,116 bytes (SHA-256 `e879151a54d6218fcc2f590ac159df6d1b71d5608e98de591fb58d7270504552`); the unsigned release APK is 127,489,744 bytes (SHA-256 `c20d1b0022ffdddbc65d9655025974ee75523d843f5d176faa836e0859687b76`). The stricter release-preflight allowlist remains a separate launch gate.
- Teaching-material sharing now follows the complete current iOS `CoachLibraryShareView` instead of Android's former title/list/note alert. The full-screen RoomBackground page has expandable signed-media preview, exact duration formatting, 500-character optional note, account/asset-scoped draft restoration, single-student automatic selection without creating a phantom draft, pending-work-first then localized-name recipient ordering, multi-student untruncated search/select-all with a 20-recipient cap, connection reload/add-student recovery, offline preservation, value-preserving retry with a renewed idempotency key, and the fixed draft-aware send bar. The shared cover now follows Swift's remote-cover/video-frame/golf fallback order; portrait and square preview containers clamp to iOS' 1.25 minimum display ratio, preserve the second-step cover/play hierarchy, hide native controls until playback starts, and use the upward collapse chevron. A failed single-student delivery is now force-persisted even when its initial selection was automatic, a fully cleared multi-student draft is removed, the visible error title no longer leaks raw transport copy, and add-student presentation waits for the share sheet to leave. Only currently active real connections can be submitted; one successful recipient still opens the exact generated conversation, while multi-recipient success returns the count. Four API 37 tests cover populated selection/note/send behavior, expanded preview, offline failure preservation, and the no-student route; `coach-library-share-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `fb4c7efff0a839cd95184c8a9bed6d975e039deb494f7f393b097c4a53a1cc46`, and `coach-library-share-preview-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `5bc5773db48619437716ce58d5da43443351bf251c1877612fbb7fcc640f527f`.
- Teaching-material import now uses the current iOS `CoachLibraryAddSourceSheet` hierarchy rather than Android's generic alert: leaf Hero, exact camera/gallery rows, centered explanatory footer, rounded warm bottom sheet, and working close/camera/gallery actions are aligned. Imported items receive Swift's `教学视频 · M月d日 HH:mm` default title instead of Android's generic `教学素材`. Like iOS `CoachLibraryDrafts`, Android now copies the selected or captured video into an owner-scoped `coach_library_drafts` directory before queueing; reliable jobs no longer depend on a picker grant, Android's system-managed cache, or the clearable Memo `videos` directory. The existing reliable-delivery queue is surfaced inside the library as the iOS-equivalent active/recovered import card, with processing/uploading/saving phase copy, real progress, retained local cover/file size, stop, continue, and confirmed discard; discard uses the same bottom confirmation hierarchy and exact history-safe copy instead of a centered Material dialog. A queued large upload reuses the aligned cellular confirmation, survives tab changes, process recovery, and ordinary cache cleanup, and blocks creation of a second import. Successful delivery resolves the returned asset id, inserts the signed asset at the top immediately, and exposes the same contextual `添加学员/发给…` plus `编辑` actions for ten seconds. Six API 37 tests cover both source routes, active progress/stop, paused continue/discard, the discard sheet, app-owned owner isolation, and immediate post-import actions. Evidence is `coach-library-import-source-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `d141e3e9df97bc32109793179b5c8336a25f1db4c5b487a8b6103c932f98989e`) and `coach-library-import-discard-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `deedbe68aae874bfdbe211e927c1cbf4538ff0fc25aa59df4fdf018b8141f17c`).
- The private teaching-library root now follows the current iOS `CoachLibraryView` instead of Android's former scrolling artwork card and dense Material grid. `私有素材库` plus search/add actions and the PageBack2 overview remain fixed while only results scroll; the byte-mapped PageBack2 artwork supplies the fading fixed-header background, while the shared RoomBackground is rendered behind the scrolling content. Overview copy and left-aligned metrics, the 44dp filled search field, `全部 / 视频 / 图片 / 常用 / 未分类` capsules, and extra tag categories match Swift. Android-only sort and result-count rows are removed. Material cards use square black fit previews, rounded-rectangle duration badges, one-line fallback titles, the first tag or `未分类`, and independent send/continue-draft glyphs instead of 16:9 crops, use counts, edit links, and text send links. Their media now follows iOS' cover → generated local/remote video frame → deep-green golf fallback chain rather than Android's former black inventory tile; a visible card with no playable URL silently reloads its signed detail just like Swift's grid task. True empty-library/search states retain the bordered action card, while a non-empty library whose filter has no results now uses the lighter grid-owned `这个分类里还没有素材 / 查看全部` hierarchy instead of reusing the wrong full empty card. Pull-to-refresh, real offline-to-online recovery, invalid-category reset, and last-visible-card automatic pagination match the current iOS behavior; continuation shows only a progress indicator instead of the legacy manual button. A populated-list sync failure preserves the last usable cards behind the matching retry/dismiss warning, an empty failure offers both retry and add-new recovery, and a first queued upload no longer falls through to the ordinary empty state. Three API 37 tests verify the populated hierarchy/actions, unavailable recovery, true empty actions, and filtered-empty reset; evidence is `coach-library-surface-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `0f1ab5760918751b82f7ac1a35bf7b4e8cb0482c35b180cf6d8398da586fd044`), `coach-library-empty-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `1a5413c664b972f578b6362ba22da2dde3fc57f0b076a15a90288e280a246e2c`), `coach-library-filter-empty-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `1eb719e252ac0ccc0f4e52be539a4cdb7261bce53c3efb4cf3c94602dc940516`), and `coach-library-unavailable-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `9187bd958971e4885cc93c5cb341e8f213d559f2432637270b8762186ec53465`); all captures are 1440×3120.
- The current iOS page artwork is now part of the Android runtime rather than an audit-only reference: latest `PageBack2` provides the coach-home and teaching-library headers, `PageBack3` provides review/profile/shared-space headers, and `PageBack1` provides the learner-list and training-goal artwork. A shared Compose background component preserves the iOS right-aligned crop and warm-light, white-mist, dark-green, or bottom-green overlays. Android's three files are byte-identical to the iOS originals, and a SHA-256 regression test now guards the imported pixels rather than merely checking that similarly named files exist.
- The shared iOS `v2RoomPageBackground()` treatment now also has one Android wrapper instead of page-local crop copies. Home/customization, goals, Plaza, all Memo lists and editors, profile/settings, AI archive, credits reset, account security, storage, feedback, and legal surfaces layer their content over the same bottom-anchored 360dp fairway image with a 90dp warm fade. API 37 visual proofs are `memo-room-background-latest-ios.png`, `all-memo-room-background-latest-ios.png`, and the guided workspace capture above.
- The legacy coach student manager remains in source for compatibility and still supports lesson mode, training goal, age, search, `只看待处理`, local-draft priority, and collapsed history. It is no longer the primary tab because current iOS replaced that surface with `CoachStudentsInteractionView`.
- The live coach `学员` tab now matches the current iOS interaction page rather than retaining Android's former one-line student rows. `学员 / 和更好的人，一起打好球` and the `连接` action sheet lead active teaching-space cards with the gold avatar ring, `我的学员 · 简介`, red `有新消息`, latest space preview, `当前重点 / 当前目标`, and the same one-full/two-compact/three-plus snapping-carousel count rules used by Swift. The sheet includes explicit invite, add-friend, and cancel actions; whole-page pull-to-refresh waits for both workspace/dashboard and Tencent IM refresh, while a confirmed offline-to-online transition now performs the same immediate space/dashboard/IM recovery as SwiftUI without duplicating the initial online load. Locally persistent `学员顺序`, Tencent IM `最近对话`, incoming-request badges, direct C2C chat, RoomBackground artwork, and the privacy note remain live. Active students missing from Tencent's recent list are synthesized as `已连接，可以开始聊天`, student conversations stay ahead of friends in saved order, and the bottom-tab dot combines workspace, IM, and request updates. API 37 verifies the populated card, space route, focus/goal metrics, pull refresh, connection sheet/cancel, recent conversation, and full-screen drag order; `coach-students-interaction-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `23f99c6aabece1b4f7f373c8ad0aabf201fc286481345845cefc3f38166e5a67`.
- The student and coach `建立新的联系` flows now use one iOS-style bottom confirmation sheet. Android removed the student-side centered `AlertDialog` and coach-side expanded icon cards; both now render the visible title and exact relationship message, two 56dp fairway actions separated by border lines, and a separate 56dp cancel surface above the navigation safe area. The coach page's privacy note uses a true lock-in-shield composition, iOS' 12sp medium hierarchy, leading alignment, and the matching 28dp bottom scroll clearance instead of the former generic centered shield row. API 37 re-verifies the page in `coach-students-interaction-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `23f99c6aabece1b4f7f373c8ad0aabf201fc286481345845cefc3f38166e5a67`) and records the sheet in `coach-students-connection-sheet-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `3c888b9d4a97676348d63de8135ea7c71581605a28bc6f14c87fc1d4c03e7fad`).
- The coach `学员顺序` destination now matches current `V2InteractionCoachOrderView` instead of using an Android-only compact alert with visible up/down arrows. Android opens a full-screen light list with the centered title, only the trailing `完成` action, 40dp avatars, 16sp semibold names, separators, and a persistent drag handle on each row. Long-press dragging changes the actual saved order; system back dismisses without saving, while accessibility services receive equivalent row position plus `上移 / 下移` custom actions. API 37 performs a real first-to-second drag, verifies the saved connection-id order, and renders the resulting page in `coach-student-order-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a329fce387d07742cbfc2e41ba5d98baaef5e69e6b8c27833fe98cde77d48b9d`).
- The shared `联系人` route now follows the live iOS `TUIKitFriendsContentView(initialTab: 1)` hierarchy instead of rendering Android-only rounded action cards. Both student and coach entry points open the same warm-white full-screen page with the leading close action, left-aligned `联系人 / 好友和群聊` identity, trailing add-friend action, flat `新的联系人 / 黑名单` rows, real request badge, pinyin A–Z friend sections, public-profile avatars/names, retry/empty states, and direct C2C chat. `新的联系人` now owns a dedicated accept/refuse page with the iOS button order and handled states, while `黑名单` loads the real Tencent blocked-user list and uses iOS' exact centered empty artwork/copy. `添加好友` is a separate RoomBackground page matching `FriendsAddFriendView`: xmark navigation, QR scan card, username/phone hints, filled search field/action, search empty state, already-friend tag, and direct chat action; incoming requests no longer leak into this page. API 37 renders all four 1440×3120 surfaces in `interaction-contacts-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `1591c4883d87e7ae45d2a9309ec38e8a852026eb7d0c949fb579b7a33100e73e`), `add-friend-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `16326d02fd3e17c3a07725d112e15b21e51f9768e95507356d914dc5c774ae9e`), `friend-requests-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `e24c9e5870e8fa721498f941d4462028db8a34396252b502b41d4641d1b270f4`), and `blacklist-empty-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `274fc6d53a0af335f42a68d4d153515381e5a9fea527ae970e4caa27193cf3b6`).
- Direct C2C chat now exposes the current iOS `C2CChatSetting` route from both the centered identity title and circled overflow action. Its destination uses iOS' exact `详细资料 / 备注名` identity and action copy, alert messages, and grouped controls. Android loads and mutates the real Tencent friend/conversation state for remark, message do-not-disturb, pinning, blacklist, clearing history, friend deletion plus conversation deletion, and add-friend fallback. A non-friend now sees the full iOS-style `添加好友` page with editable, nickname-prefilled verification wording; that wording is passed through `V2TIMFriendAddApplication.setAddWording` rather than discarded. Reporting uses the authenticated `userAuth/reportUser` action on the matching full-screen black sheet with the six current iOS reasons, optional detail, submit, and report-plus-block. A saved remark updates the live chat title, and deleting a contact exits the chat like iOS. Three API 37 tests verify the surfaces and actions: `interaction-chat-settings-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `ad00f4e6aab328e6b81ae7228677598e75b0571823193cf1636326e48a07c8cf`), `interaction-add-friend-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `72cb9a1c3d4c5ec003ecf4f4cd53b6e60f7522f4db53265b8c97587d92a498f9`), and `interaction-report-user-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `6820fd1b8973e4f56d84ea1f412b81bcd9025c483d2c6656194723a400e2bd94`).
- The C2C message body now covers the current iOS rich-message, reaction, forwarding, multi-select, and text-translation surface. Eligible text menus use iOS' five-items-per-row wrapping and include `翻译`; Android calls Tencent's actual `translateText`, keeps translated state across message polling, renders the separate 14sp translation bubble with the exact Tencent support footer, and matches its long-press `隐藏 / 转发 / 复制` menu. Forwarding translated output sends a new text message to one or multiple selected recent conversations. Direct/original message forwarding still uses actual `createForwardMessage / createMergerMessage / sendMessage`, the 30-message separate limit, sender-order merged titles, at most four abstracts, multi-target chips, and the iPhone-style forward-type bottom group. Multi-select retains the 20dp leading circle, bubble-wide toggling, 56dp action bar, and real batch deletion. API 37 verified the complete eight-test chat class and captured `interaction-chat-actions-with-translation-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `818f3c2f54502107d32224dfccd588daf7b66f3b6b7288e7e702b1307015478a`), `interaction-chat-translation-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `ddce93c215c8cc0cc175bdcd56d58478e6153d828dc2bc4549db44c8330ece4d`), `interaction-chat-forward-type-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `b23a683791bcf375f4e62d52f82e7f4a2d2d73703682a96190783abbc65888ba`), `interaction-chat-multi-select-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `abaea492ee6252f2106c5046961ecb8b4817c328e25cef99fb00cfcf9da224ee`), and `interaction-chat-forward-target-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `bc44e3527def651b3f52fb8299dc4ee25ded086571aae526d27f76d2ca5c88d4`), all 1440x3120. Voice-to-text remains an SDK capability gap: iOS AtomicX exposes it, but Android Tencent IM SDK `9.1.7818` has no public ASR operation. Authenticated cross-device delivery remains launch QA because local instrumentation has no production IM credentials.
- Android now consumes the lightweight `getStudentUnreadCounts` versioned response on a 60-second foreground cadence. The central `挥呗` entry and each connected space show live unread badges, and the student list prioritizes spaces with unread activity; the coach dashboard performs the same one-minute version check and a full relationship refresh every fifth cycle. These polling loops stop below the lifecycle's visible `STARTED` state and re-check versions on return instead of spending network/media work in the background. As on iOS, the first snapshot only establishes a cold-start baseline; a newly increased coach unread, advanced coach reply, accepted invitation, or learner-initiated disconnect raises an eight-second in-app banner whose real `connectionId + itemId` route opens the exact discussion or relationship history instead of only switching tabs.
- Coach student cards now open a complete relationship-profile editor backed by `updateCoachConnectionPreferences`: required stage/goal/focus fields, lesson mode, training goals, age, weekly target, cadence, communication preference, follow-up schedule, private category/note, and pin state save to the real connection and refresh immediately. A new active connection whose timestamp or any of the three required fields is incomplete automatically opens this editor once; `稍后` is account/connection-scoped for 24 hours, while onboarding alone pre-fills the iOS defaults of two weekly practices, weekly cadence, and image/video communication.
- The relationship-profile editor now also carries the current iOS quick-entry vocabulary for training stage, primary goal, focus topics, and communication style. Tapping a preset updates the same real profile fields, focus topics remain capped at eight, and every text field stays editable so coaches can retain custom terminology rather than being forced into the presets.
- The relationship editor now also mirrors iOS' account-owned profile card: nickname, remote or `Avatar_*` preset avatar, handicap, golf age/start year, dominant hand, home course, and first equipment brand are synchronized from the student account and presented read-only, so coaches do not duplicate or overwrite learner-owned facts.
- Workspace video roots and video replies now use the authenticated `prepareAssetUpload` → private COS multipart upload → JPEG cover upload → `completeAssetUpload` contract. Failed deliveries retain the local video, cover, account owner, and stable request IDs in a 24-hour queue; pure-text roots and replies now enter that same queue without a fake media dependency and reuse the same request ID on retry. Both student and coach shells attempt foreground recovery after restart without duplicating already accepted posts or replies.
- Android spaces now mirror iOS' content hierarchy instead of mixing every root post into one feed: `在练` is backed by `student_practice`, `在看` is backed by `coach_post` / legacy coach content, old items fall back safely from sender role, and new text/video roots send the matching `contentMode`. Opening a root now separates `内容 / 交流 / 资料`; the resource surface deduplicates explicitly saved media and excludes voice assets and unsaved album conversation videos.
- The shared space overview presents relationship type, current goal, and current focus once in the PageBack3 relationship card. The single practice stream retains explicit newest/oldest ordering, comfortable/compact density, and iOS-compatible new/viewed/replied/student-viewed labels backed by `studentHasUnread`, `latestReplySenderRole`, and `reviewState`. Layout and sort choices persist by role and connection, and root ordering uses `latestActivityAt` before creation time so an older item with a new reply correctly rises to the top.
- New practice content has role-aware sources: both sides can record with the system camera or select an album video, learners can reuse an account-scoped personal record, and coaches can select an existing private teaching asset and publish it through `createWorkspacePost` as practice content rather than reviving the removed watching stream. Learner video confirmation defaults `需要教练点评` on for teaching spaces and forces it off for friend spaces. All prepared files still enter the same stable-ID delivery queue and retain retry state after failure. Root-post and thread-reply drafts remain private per account and space with the same 24-hour lifetime as iOS; an atomically accepted queue job owns its files and clears only its corresponding editor draft.
- Conversation media now uses capped, fit-scaled previews so portrait videos cannot expand to full-screen height, and playable covers open the signed video URL. Message indentation reflects the current role, opening a thread refreshes read state immediately, and coach-only save actions turn selected reply media into private material without silently adding ordinary album replies. Consequently, `资料` contains only explicitly saved/referenced reply assets, matching the iOS acceptance rule.
- An open discussion can now attach up to three related videos from a mixed set of local album selections and, for coaches, existing private teaching assets. The multi-select strip supports in-place add/remove and a visible `N/3` limit; cloud ids plus managed local paths/covers/stable upload ids survive the account/thread-scoped 24-hour draft window. Text, voice, or a newly prepared primary video can carry the related videos in one stable reliable-delivery job. Existing cloud asset ids are never reuploaded, local related media are uploaded sequentially, each resolved asset id is persisted for retry, the combined local payload participates in the 100MB metered-network guard, and draft/queue-owned copies are removed at the correct remove, expiry, disconnect, success, cancel, or logout boundary. Once persistence accepts a job, ownership transfers before network delivery so a transient failure cannot leave a second sendable editor copy; an OS scheduler failure cannot roll back the accepted payload. Publication forwards at most three `attachmentAssetIds` and four library-save ids, matching the iOS/backend contract.
- Local workspace video now follows the same preparation path for new roots and discussion replies: camera and album sources open a full-screen playback/trim surface, passthrough trimming preserves video and audio tracks, and the derived clip keeps the original stable upload/request identifiers before entering reliable delivery. Both the initial private copy and a nontrivial trim perform an explicit free-space preflight with source-size-aware reserve; an insufficient device fails early with actionable copy and cleanup instead of leaving a half-written draft.
- Newly uploaded coach video now carries the iOS private-library choice through preparation, trim, draft restoration, and reliable delivery without a second upload. Root posts offer it for camera and album sources; in-thread replies offer it for newly recorded coach video while ordinary album replies retain the iOS rule that they can be saved afterward from the message action. Legacy queue jobs and learner/friend uploads remain safely unsaved by default.
- Reliable video delivery now exposes its real preparation, byte-upload, and publication phases with monotonic percentage progress in the queue and above both app shells. An active transfer can be stopped by the user; OkHttp is cancelled, the retained local media and stable request id remain paused instead of being picked up by automatic recovery, and an explicit retry resumes the idempotent delivery from the retained job.
- Coach discussions now include the iOS professional-analysis return path for learner videos. Android downloads the signed source with visible cancellable progress, opens a dedicated slow-motion/frame-step/drawing workspace, records the screen with microphone audio through the system MediaProjection consent flow, supports pause/resume/cancel without modifying the original, and returns the completed recording through trim as a pending composer attachment so it can be sent with text and reply context through the reliable queue.
- The pending-review path now opens directly into an iOS-aligned `给学员反馈` composer instead of making the coach rediscover the discussion tab. It keeps the learner avatar/date, 168-point source-video summary and original note visible, adds the current `我的反馈` hierarchy with optional title and a 1,000-character counter, and presents equal `添加视频 / 口播分析 / 教学素材` tools backed by the existing real upload, analysis-recording, and private-library flows. The optional title and body are both account/item-scoped draft state, survive recreation, and become the actual queued reply title/copy across text, voice, primary-video, and metered-network continuation paths. Local root/reply/analysis videos now return to the composer before publication and expose the iOS `选择视频封面` flow: a debounced 4:3 frame preview, exact time slider, skip/confirm actions, and explicit non-destructive guidance. Confirmation replaces the real queued JPEG cover without altering the video; the primary media path, selected cover time, save-to-library choice, and stable upload/request ids survive the same 24-hour draft window.
- Space timelines now follow iOS' versioned live-refresh contract: the visible feed checks its lightweight `feedVersion` every 30 seconds, and an open discussion checks `threadCheckVersion` every 8 seconds during the first two minutes then every 20 seconds. Both loops pause outside the visible lifecycle and perform a version-based resume check rather than blindly reloading signed media. Unchanged responses carry no thread media, full signed playback URLs refresh hourly, remote deletion/receipt updates win, and optimistic local replies remain present until the server includes them. Feed history now follows `nextCursor`; loading older pages deduplicates overlap while a first-page refresh preserves already loaded history.
- Root content and individual replies now expose deletion only when the server marks them as owned by the signed-in user. Android confirms the destructive action, calls `deleteWorkspaceItem`, refreshes the authoritative thread, and follows the latest moving iOS behavior by removing deleted roots/replies from space feeds, review/home/student previews, resource aggregation, and incoming-activity notices. Server placeholder copy such as `内容已删除 / 原内容已删除` can no longer leak into learner-facing previews; an already open deleted thread becomes read-only and shows one neutral deleted state.
- Android thread replies now carry iOS' real `replyToItemId` and optional `replyAtSeconds` contract for both text and media, render the referenced sender/preview/timestamp, and preserve that context in reliable retries. The reply composer can also record a microphone-authorized AAC voice message, upload it as `voice_recording`, recover it through the same 24-hour queue, exclude it from video resources, and expose a playback chip in the conversation.
- Coach discussion composers now load the same private, server-backed `常用回复` collection as iOS. The four most recently used entries can be inserted into a draft without auto-sending; the management dialog supports create, edit, delete confirmation, the 30-entry limit, and usage-driven server reordering.
- The latest moving iOS quick-reply design is now mirrored: Android searches title and body case-insensitively, limits titles to 20 characters with a visible counter, previews the composed reply before saving, presents the manager/editor as nested medium sheets, and keeps the full-width `新建常用回复` action fixed above the navigation safe area. Its outgoing queue also separates iOS-style `进行中 / 失败` lists, keeps metered large uploads waiting for Wi-Fi in the working list rather than mislabeling them as failures, and scopes batch retry to genuine failed/paused jobs.
- The coach review workspace now mirrors iOS' operational queue rather than rendering generic feed cards: `全部 / 新互动 / 已查看 / 有草稿` focus filters use the same draft-first then new/viewed-oldest ordering, local draft-only roots are restored even when absent from the first server page, and cards separate the source content from the latest exchange while exposing student identity, pin state, training focus, relative time, cover, delivery/draft/overdue/follow-up/responded state, and state-appropriate actions.
- Open student spaces now provide the iOS-style direct workspace switcher, ordered current-first then pinned/recent, without returning through the overview. Drafts stay isolated by account and connection, and coaches can jump from the same header to the relationship-profile editor and return to that student.
- Active spaces now follow the latest iOS collapsed-first hierarchy: a PageBack3 relationship overview leads one `练习内容` stream, with count, time ordering, and density controls shown only when records exist. The bottom row always separates private IM from workspace publishing; the real composer appears only on demand or when a recoverable text/media draft exists, while `收起` remains available only for an empty composer. Header actions stay consolidated into one menu; coaches can edit the teaching profile, toggle `重点学员` without overwriting newer profile fields, or disconnect, and the returned connection immediately updates the current workspace, parent list, and switcher.
- The learner-facing empty teaching space now deliberately uses the latest iOS relationship explanation instead of retaining an obsolete intermediate first-swing campaign card. Publishing remains immediately reachable through the fixed `发内容` action and its two-choice sheet, so removing duplicate in-card actions does not remove capability.
- Root-content deletion now follows the latest iOS visibility boundary: the confirmation states that the root and its exchange will leave the space, successful deletion clears the thread draft, refreshes the parent feed, and dismisses the open conversation. Reply deletion retains its separate confirmation and refreshes only the current thread, so a reply removal cannot unexpectedly close the root discussion.
- The Android teaching-profile flow now mirrors the current iOS onboarding hierarchy and completion contract. `PageBack3` is confined to the 208dp student Hero while the page and all five editor destinations remain warm white; the main toolbar is the same `稍后/取消 — 新学员接入/教学档案 — 保存` hierarchy with no Android-only bottom save bar. Account facts use equal columns and separators, the empty guidance states that only `当前阶段` is required, and the four entry cards now appear in iOS order with semantic icons plus one `必填` and three `选填` badges. Stage alone marks both the local and cloud relationship profile complete; target, focus, and lesson settings remain optional and editable later. The complete summary keeps the artwork goal card, icon-bearing lesson row, editable focus card, and private coach-management entry. The `当前阶段 · 必填` destination now also uses the centered navigation title, gold-ring account card, cloud-sync cue, three-column account facts, exact required guidance, adaptive stage/age/communication choice grids, and no Android-only free-text stage field. The `训练目标 · 选填` destination now uses the exact PageBack1 Hero and 20%→80% deep-green overlay, three iOS-order 52dp-icon goal cards with full descriptions and selected styling, plus the separate bubble-labelled 100-character detail editor; selecting a card persists one enum and only auto-fills the title while the detail is blank. API 37 verifies onboarding dismissal, stage selection, stage-only completion, goal selection and 100-character clamping, centered subpage navigation, top save, and persisted completion in `coach-student-profile-onboarding-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `7584d4529987ea76acd5039f36dbfbd237295302f408f9afa17dafdcd7ecfa42`), `coach-student-profile-current-stage-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a0f12fdd5ae87ab012eb24eb5474558edbbc93a9d40c9be00b6e188b4d608fcb`), `coach-student-profile-stage-only-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `4b87ff5377dc6dc996036d973c9777fd7ad566cc13b6e8b32e53b72d59224b6c`), and `coach-student-profile-training-goal-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a74f05858489d8af7d6d45f3c2e18063519cb46db4b6d7b09b8b3d2c366fcaa8`). Focus, category, and private-note inputs retain the 120/30/300-character limits, while cadence, weekly target, communication preference, age, pin, follow-up, and category presets remain attached to their iOS sections.
- `授课方式 · 选填` and `训练重点 · 选填` now mirror their current SwiftUI render trees rather than generic Android form controls. Lesson mode has the exact three icon/description selection cards plus a bordered teaching-rhythm section with the iOS 2-session default, 1–14 step controls, toggle styling, cadence grid, and previously missing communication grid. Training focus has the icon heading, 150dp bordered editor with 120-character clamp, and adaptive-equivalent four-column muted-gold multi-select topics, including retained custom tags and the eight-tag ceiling. Instrumentation persists `灵活上课 / 每周3次 / 双周 / 语音为主` and a 120-character focus with two tags; API 37 evidence is `coach-student-profile-lesson-mode-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `c0feeb5d58836648e694cbf604bd73bfddadb75e5ac21e4ac079978bbf83de3e`) and `coach-student-profile-training-focus-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `7693fc515f8c70ec66d7dc9e6d36f7abdc2532ee94c230b6cbedb1ed09c6f33f`).
- `教练管理` now mirrors SwiftUI's private management section: one 38%-opacity mist-green card, five preset categories plus the active custom category, a 30-character custom field, pinned and follow-up switches, a localized date/time row, and an 82dp private-note editor with leading count and 300-character clamp. Android's former `不设置 / 明天 / 3天后 / 一周后` approximation is removed. The follow-up state now follows iOS semantics: saved values initialize the switch and picker, absent values initialize tomorrow, the picker permits the earlier of now/current through one year, and disabling the switch sends `null` without discarding the staged date. API 37 evidence is `coach-student-profile-management-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `8c99de421bd39c593f8134ed8c29a15773d558798e70d5641f0b120a4d5ca44d`).
- The in-space switcher now follows iOS' full-page workspace model instead of a generic alert: it shows the signed-in avatar/name and active `教练身份 / 学员身份`, separates teaching relationships from friends with live counts, keeps the current relationship checked, and uses already-loaded dashboard/unread/feed roots for each row's latest visible title plus relative activity time. Selecting another row keeps its account-scoped draft intact and opens that space directly; the coach-only `添加新空间` action closes the current workspace before opening the real invitation lifecycle.
- Coach and student workspace failure copy now follows iOS' explicit user-error boundary. Server-approved recovery messages such as expired invitations, invalid relationships, oversized videos, unavailable local media, and incomplete responses remain actionable, while unexpected HTTP, endpoint, token, user-path, or platform diagnostics are hidden in release builds and retained only in debuggable builds. The shared boundary also rethrows coroutine cancellation instead of turning a disposed screen into a stale error banner, and a source-level regression test prevents the coach/space surfaces from returning to direct `Throwable.message` rendering.
- Student spaces now expose the iOS `记录` path against Android's real account-scoped video Memo repository. Choosing a personal record fixes the current connection, copies the source into managed cache, generates a cover, publishes it as `在练`, and hands ownership to the reliable queue so success cannot delete the original Memo video and failure can resume after restart. The dedicated multi-space Memo share and the in-space record picker both default to an ordinary record rather than a forced review task; only the explicit review switch changes that behavior in a coach space.
- The coach library now matches iOS' management model with debounced server-backed title/tag search, dynamic `全部 / 常用 / <tag> / 未分类` categories, `最近使用 / 最新优先 / 复用最多 / 标题` sorting, title and five-tag editing, and server-backed removal that explicitly preserves already shared history. It follows the server cursor beyond the initial 50 assets while retaining already loaded rows across first-page refreshes. Assets with an unsubmitted share draft now rise above the selected sort in stable order, so `继续发送` cannot be buried while every non-draft asset retains the chosen ordering. Its result surface uses compact two-column 16:9 cards with two-line titles, one-line tags, usage metadata, edit, and quick-send actions. The current iOS first-use card, filtered-empty reset, and dashed `还想补充更多教学素材？` grid card are now present on Android with direct album/camera actions. Private video import transfers ownership to the same account-scoped reliable queue instead of deleting the prepared file after a transient failure. A successful one-student share consumes the returned item and opens that exact new discussion immediately, while multi-student sharing retains an explicit successful-recipient count.
- The latest iOS library copy and editor hierarchy is now reflected on Android: the add-source dialog leads with `记录你的教学灵感` and the same camera/album copy, editable titles expose and enforce the iOS 50-character limit, the primary save action reads `保存素材`, and recipient selection consistently uses `发送给学员 / 发送给 N 位学员`. Source inspection corrected the earlier still-image gap claim: the current iOS `VideoMemoVideoPicker` is explicitly configured with `config.filter = .videos`, Android uses `PickVisualMedia.VideoOnly`, and the shared `prepareAssetUpload` contract currently accepts only video/audio MIME types. Both clients therefore expose the same current video behavior even though the iOS-facing copy anticipates future image support; image import must be introduced as a coordinated backend-and-both-client feature, not treated as an Android-only omission.
- Online Download now imports the exact iOS `OnlineDownload_DouyinLogo` bytes and uses them in both the compact platform badge and the 58-point hero emblem. The hero also follows iOS' current golf watermark, gradient opacity, and `10 积分/次 / 保存相册 / 可建 Memo` metric hierarchy instead of Android's earlier text glyph and generic download chrome.
- Library list responses remain preview-only like iOS; tapping a card now requests `getCoachAsset` just in time, opens its signed playback URL, and avoids signing every private teaching video during first-page load. Playback from a shared conversation also records `recordAssetPlayback` against the real connection so coach efficiency metrics are no longer under-counted on Android.
- The coach shell now includes iOS' fifth `我` tab. It shows the signed-in coach, authorization state, live student/review/replied counts, real `getCoachMetrics` teaching/upload/playback efficiency data, settings and role switching, pending-delivery guards, and the latest branded logout confirmation that keeps cloud teaching content while clearing the local signed-in session.
- Reliable delivery is no longer foreground-only, workspace-only, or media-only. Video, voice, pure-text roots, pure-text replies, and private-library video imports all enter the account-scoped WorkManager queue with connected-network and storage constraints, a short foreground-first delay, exponential retry, stable request IDs, and account isolation. Old persisted jobs decode with the workspace default, while new library jobs retain their destination/source/tags and never expose a fake “open space” action. Text jobs bypass media validation/upload and publish directly with their persisted idempotency key; only genuinely failed jobs participate in the batch-retry action. When recovery still has pending work, it promotes itself to a `dataSync` foreground worker with a low-priority ongoing notification, matching iOS' OS-managed background-transfer posture for long non-chunkable uploads. Android exposes the account-filtered queue above both student and coach tab bars: each retained job shows its destination type, attempt count, active/paused state and last error, supports individual or batch retry, can reopen a real corresponding space/discussion when applicable, and confirms removal before deleting any retained local media for a non-active job. Android can therefore restart the process and resume server-idempotent delivery after connectivity returns without leaking another account's pending count; an interrupted single multipart file still restarts from byte zero because the current COS grant/Cloud Function protocol does not expose chunk IDs or uploaded-part state.
- Large-media network policy now matches iOS' 100MB safeguard. Space posts, personal-record shares into a space, video replies, and private teaching-material imports are stopped before their upload attempt when Android reports an active metered network; the retained queue job waits for Wi-Fi unless the user explicitly confirms mobile-data use. The approval belongs only to that stable job and survives process restart, while legacy queued jobs default to unapproved. The same confirmation is available from the originating composer and from the persistent delivery queue, so foreground retry and WorkManager recovery cannot bypass it.
- Android now uses Tencent Cloud Chat SDK `9.1.7818` as the real friend source for `挥呗空间`. Space refresh initializes/logs in with the account's existing `SDKAppID + userId + userSig`, pulls `getFriendList`, normalizes at most 200 IDs, and sends them to the same `syncFriendSpaces` action used by iOS. Expired UserSig is renewed once, logout also logs out Chat SDK, backend verification remains authoritative, and an unavailable friend service falls back to existing coach/friend spaces instead of hiding the overview.
- The coach library can now upload a private video once and batch-send it to selected active students through the idempotent `shareLibraryAsset` action. Personal video Memos expose a separate `分享到空间` flow that uploads once, creates isolated per-space roots, freezes recipients during delivery, and retries only failed targets. When the first frozen relationship becomes unavailable before upload authorization, Android now mirrors iOS by trying the remaining selected relationships before abandoning the single shared upload, so one stale target cannot prevent every valid recipient from receiving the record.
- The space-header `连接` action now matches iOS' two-way choice instead of being coach-only: Android can still accept a coach invitation, or search the authenticated public account directory by username/phone and submit a Tencent IM two-way friend request. The same surface lists incoming requests and supports accept/reject; pending-validation (`30539`) and already-friends (`30515`) results are treated explicitly, and accepted IM friends are reconciled through the existing backend-authorized friend-space sync.
- Android's add-friend surface now also restores the complete iOS QR path rather than stopping at text search: the shared `huibei://user/{userId}` payload is generated from profile settings, parsed with bounded scheme/host/path validation, recognized continuously from a CameraX luminance stream or from a selected gallery image through ZXing, resolved through the authenticated `getPublicUserInfo` action, and then handed to the same Tencent IM request flow. Camera denial leaves gallery recognition available, malformed/non-Huibei codes stay rejected, and decoder/parser regression tests use a real generated QR matrix.
- The private-library index now includes iOS' fixed `全部 / 常用 / 未分类 / 视频 / 图文` categories ahead of dynamic tags, plus the latest private-material title, add-source copy, visible result/total/category/reuse counts, and existing server-backed sorting/search behavior.
- Coach tab work indicators now follow the iOS semantics: the review badge combines server-pending review roots with additional local reply drafts without double counting, clamps at `99+`, and the library tab shows pending import/share work. The library recipient sheet persists selected students, note, and stable batch id per account/asset for 24 hours, restores with `继续发送`, and clears only after a successful share.
- Role switching is now blocked while that account still owns reliable-delivery jobs in both the student and coach shells, with a direct route to the queue. Logout is the stronger privacy boundary: it cancels the account's WorkManager recovery, deletes retained queue media, clears workspace and library-share drafts, logs out Tencent IM, and purges the separate professional-analysis video cache while preserving the user's personal videos and notes.
- Home quick tools now mirror the latest iOS discovery row: all six entries (`录挥杆 / 挥杆记录 / 回看分析 / 轨迹绘制 / 视频下载 / AI 教练`) appear in one horizontally scrollable strip before `更多`, with matching icon families, 52-point circular treatments, colors, and guest locks. The direct history entry opens the date archive instead of starting a recording, while `更多` retains the existing configuration sheet. Every locked shortcut now also matches the iOS auth boundary: logged-out trajectory, AI, and download taps enter login instead of opening the protected destination, then continue to the originally selected tool after successful authentication; trajectory carries a dedicated post-login intent so GolfCam opens automatically on return. API 37 UI smoke verification exercised `先看看 -> 轨迹绘制` and confirmed that the guest returns to the SMS/password login page without exposing GolfCam or crashing.
- Home `回看分析` follows the current SwiftUI `V2AnalysisSourceSheet` instead of Android's former centered three-choice alert. It is a full-width warm bottom sheet with a 42% scrim, drag handle, 58dp analysis mark, exact title/subtitle, the prominent album action first, live capture second, format guidance, sand cancel action, outside-tap and downward-drag dismissal, and a dismissal-locked import progress card. The Android-only `已有素材库` choice is removed because it is absent from the current iOS render tree; saved clips remain available through the separate `挥杆记录` tool. Album launch, unreadable media, import, trim, and local persistence failures now all clear the busy state, clean superseded temporary files, reopen the two-source sheet, and keep both retry and live recording available; coroutine cancellation stays silent and never becomes a false failure. Eight deterministic flow tests plus three API 37 sheet tests cover the complete recovery contract. Evidence is `home-analysis-source-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5046e194d0da451cf8bff43a6b81b2794b8c44d74656278b6110799cc8c2afed`), `home-analysis-source-processing-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `3923d81e899a039867ad0f7bcc2c1b1d33e594ef65d0eb02a4517c79a459dd05`), and `home-analysis-source-recovery-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `a18ce4d1585dc3047a03e0426ce3bee5666154178597545cfca4540d7d70c6ac`), all 1440×3120.
- Home discovery micro-chrome now follows the current dirty iOS sources rather than the older Android styling: tool glyphs and guest-lock glyphs use the same 18/7-point rhythm, the Home-only category rail uses 10-point spacing, 12-point labels, sand-beige unselected capsules, 3-point fairway selection shadow, and the `全部 -> 精选` label mapping. The continue-practice fallback uses the same deep-green/fairway gradient, center-to-bottom 45% scrim, and leading plus icon; portrait video-card play and favorite glyphs are also reduced to the iOS 9/11-point sizes. The side-by-side emulator verified the updated guest Home rail and live cloud cards without clipping.
- Home discovery cards no longer reuse the older Plaza card chrome. They now follow the latest iOS 3:4 portrait-cover layout with the play badge, bottom category tag, two-line title, compact coach row, and heart-style favorite count/action; guest favorite taps now enter login instead of failing silently.
- Daily Practice now requires both camera and microphone permission, enables CameraX audio recording, intercepts system back while the current clip is finalizing, and only dismisses after the last video has either been stored or failed explicitly. The archive count follows iOS' `N 条记录` wording.
- Swing replay now maps the scrubber's absolute position to a frame, uses tolerant/coalesced seeks during interaction, lands on the exact frame at release, keeps drawings visible after the editor closes, and exposes the iOS-matched confirmed clear action plus speed-change feedback. The professional-analysis player retains its separate persistent annotation posture, top-right quick actions, and speed notices.
- Coach professional analysis now also ports iOS' real video-layer utilities instead of stopping at matching copy: horizontal flip transforms the source video, detected skeleton, and drawing canvas together; the bundled on-device pose detector extracts local-video frames off the UI thread, rejects landmarks below iOS' `0.24` confidence threshold, updates at the same 160ms cadence, and renders the same 12 shoulder/arm/torso/leg connections with green alignment guides, yellow limbs, and gold joints. Student-video audio now starts muted like iOS and has an explicit original-audio toggle while microphone screen recording remains available. The detector uses Google's base ML Kit pose runtime in single-image mode and caps inference frames at 1280px on the longest side; it does not upload frames. A dedicated Android instrumentation check loaded the bundled model and processed an offline 480x360 frame successfully on the Pixel 7 Pro Android 17 emulator, while the release build passed R8/resource shrinking with the detector retained.
- Video Memo now uses the full iOS-style continuous inertial timeline rather than the former eight-frame substitute: `稳 / 细 / 帧` densities control a waveform time axis, dragging pauses and seeks continuously, decay carries the scrub with bounded velocity, and playback resumes only when it had been active before the gesture. Drawings remain visible in playback view with quick undo/clear actions.
- Android now requests `getAppUpdatePolicy` with `platform=android`, applies numeric version comparison, stable rollout buckets, forced/minimum-version rules, and reminder intervals, and opens the Google Play URL. The shared backend stores Android and iOS policies in separate documents; Android remains safely disabled until operations explicitly configures the Play release.
- Direct website packaging now has a validated, opt-in ABI filter instead of forcing every friend-testing APK to carry emulator and legacy native libraries. The default remains a four-ABI universal APK/AAB for Play and general verification; `MYGOLFSWING_ABI_FILTERS=arm64-v8a` produced a 55,278,682-byte unsigned release APK containing only arm64 native code, versus 122,724,113 bytes for the current restored universal artifact after the on-device pose and comparison-analysis runtime work. Unsupported or empty ABI selections fail Gradle configuration, and the default universal outputs were rebuilt after the size check so a special-purpose website build cannot silently replace the authoritative artifact.
- Runtime smoke testing against the currently deployed legacy `videoPlaza` function confirmed that it still ignores `platform=android` and returns the enabled iOS/App Store policy. Android now fails closed unless the response explicitly identifies `platform=android`, preventing an iOS `v2.6` prompt from appearing on fresh Android installs while the platform-isolated backend revision awaits deployment.
- The current debug build was freshly installed with no app data on the local Pixel 7 Pro Android 17/API 37 emulator and cold-started `MainActivity` in 1.188 seconds. The activity remained top-resumed, completed the iOS-matched short brand transition, entered the readable guest Home rather than forcing login, and emitted no `AndroidRuntime` crash log. UIAutomator independently observed `晚上好，挥呗游客`, discovery/search/tool content, and the four ordinary tabs; `guest-cold-start-home-ios-parity.png` is 1440x3120 with SHA-256 `a2174e6ae182e197f43993e9e8366dbd97103aaa8c0f169033ec6baafc9f7a62`. Protected actions still preserve intent and open the redesigned login flow. The opt-in `MYGOLFSWING_DEBUG_APPLICATION_ID_SUFFIX` property continues to support a side-by-side `.parity` package without changing normal debug/release ids. A fully formed `huibei://invite?inviteId=…&token=…&code=…` cold-start intent still resolves to the same activity and keeps the logged-out user at authentication for that protected acceptance flow. Authenticated R/S/C/X checks remain manual QA.
- Read-only production probes confirm `coachWorkspace` recognizes the new public invite-preview route (`INVITE_NOT_FOUND` for a synthetic id) and protects `getCoachMetrics` with `SESSION_REQUIRED`, so the current coach backend contract is present. The deployment mismatch is isolated to the older `videoPlaza` update-policy handler rather than the coach/student workspace service.
- Android now recognizes the same verified web invite (`https://www.mygolfai.com.cn/invite/...#token=...`) and `huibei://invite` links as iOS, preserves the invite through login, forces the safe student experience, previews the coach, and accepts by private token without exposing it in ordinary screen copy.
- Current `coachWorkspace` responses are now retained at field parity instead of being reduced to the first Android UI's needs: connection perspective/timestamps and account golf facts, historical coaching-focus snapshots, media dimensions/size, review requirement/waiting/follow-up/overdue state, view cursors, sender summaries, latest-reply media, asset metadata, per-connection dashboard authority, secure invite token/coach credentials, thread versions, and upload-grant expiry/file identifiers all survive tolerant parsing. Legacy missing video flags still fall back to persistent root metadata and cover art.
- Action-level comparison now finds Android counterparts for all 31 current user-visible iOS `coachWorkspace` actions. The four remaining iOS service-only names are intentional implementation substitutions: Android uses the current `prepareAssetUpload / completeAssetUpload + createWorkspacePost` reliable queue instead of the legacy `prepareVideoUpload / commitVideoUpload` path, uses role-authorized `createWorkspacePost` for the same coach-post semantics, and receives pending-library recovery through `listCoachLibrary`'s built-in server recovery rather than issuing the redundant explicit recovery call.
- The current iOS light-room visual treatment is now shared through Android's `V2RoomPage`/`V2RoomBackground` layer rather than copied page by page. Beyond Home, goals, Plaza, Memo, profile, settings, AI archive, credits, account security, storage, feedback, and legal, it now covers login, registration, password reset, role selection, AI chat/history/configuration, online download/history, all five coach tabs, coach student-profile editing, the learner-space landing and full-screen workspace switcher, Memo sharing, the video-comparison picker, the full-screen profile-location picker, and notification/privacy. Source-contract tests pin those mappings so a later refactor cannot silently restore an opaque page background.
- Account deletion now follows the complete current iOS `AccountDeletionView` render tree instead of stopping at the light background and initial warning card. Android keeps the centered `删除账号` navigation title in every state and shows `取消` only before deletion; the confirmation Hero uses the fairway artwork and exact four data categories, the final confirmation/error overlays use the branded 104dp gradient header and paired capsule actions instead of Material alerts, deletion progress sits in the 104dp ring/24dp white card hierarchy, and completion uses the fairway Hero plus exact `账号、本机登录状态及相关数据已完成清理，感谢你使用挥呗。 / 返回首页` handoff. The production repository flow and logged-out login continuation reuse the same components. - Remaining manual evidence is intentionally still open and owned by the project owner: authenticated two-device/backend execution of the R/S/C/X matrix—including friend-request acceptance, MediaProjection consent, microphone capture, stop/retry progress, and analysis-video return/send on representative devices—is required before release sign-off. Both iOS and Android currently retain a stable upload job but restart an interrupted single COS multipart request from byte zero; true byte-level continuation needs a shared resumable backend protocol and is therefore a common future enhancement, not an Android/iOS parity deficit.

### Tracking rule

- `scripts/android_check_ios_alignment.sh` now includes untracked Swift/config and visual asset files as well as modified tracked files. A new iOS screen or image can therefore no longer bypass the alignment gate merely because it has not yet been committed. The exact bytes of every dirty parity file are pinned in `docs/ios-parity-dirty-files.sha256`; a later edit to an already-documented filename now fails with `changed after its last Android review` until its new behavior is inspected, synchronized, and re-pinned. The checker also extracts the highest declared iOS `MARKETING_VERSION` and fails when Android's default `versionName` drifts behind it; the current check reports `2.6.1` on both platforms. It additionally extracts literal current coach-workspace actions from iOS and fails if Android lacks a counterpart. The four iOS-only actions for its retired pre-unified upload recovery path (`prepareVideoUpload`, `commitVideoUpload`, `createCoachPost`, and `recoverPendingLibraryUploads`) are explicitly excluded; all 31 current unified actions are present on Android.
- The same checker now discovers every iOS `PageBack*.imageset`, requires the corresponding Android `drawable-nodpi/page_back_N.png`, and performs a byte-for-byte comparison. It also reads `docs/ios-parity-assets.tsv` and byte-compares 26 current visual assets: the login-course background/logo, home Hero, analysis/guest preview, the Memo forest fallback, three goal scenes, all five preset avatars, both profile gender vectors, all four quick-add cards, the four custom-toolbar icons, GolfCam impact/landing markers, and the Douyin download icon. The Android toolbar's four early substitute illustrations and the hand-built landing vector have been replaced with the exact iOS source bytes. The checker resolves every Swift string literal under iOS V2 and the still-active legacy `JustSwing`, `SwingAnalysis`, and `GolfCam` source trees against the real asset catalog, including model/enum-selected imagery and SVGs, while excluding the explicitly disabled `CoachDemo*` design-preview pack; it fails when any resolved production asset has not been mapped for Android. It now also scans production Kotlin, Java, and XML references for the 23 mapped runtime resources plus the three PageBack backgrounds, using the exact Android resource type (`drawable`, `raw`, or `mipmap`): an image whose bytes were copied but whose real runtime reference is absent fails as unused instead of counting as parity. Three explicitly marked source-only files—the Memo forest original and two gender SVG originals—remain byte-guarded inputs for their lossless Android derivatives without pretending that the raw originals are rendered directly. A newly added or replaced mapped iOS visual therefore fails the live parity gate until the actual artwork—not just its filename or a visual approximation—is imported and wired into Android.
- This snapshot is a live synchronization checkpoint, not a claim of full parity. Full parity is reached only after the remaining items above are implemented and verified against the same iOS HEAD and dirty-file set.

## 2026-07-29 Tracking Snapshot

### iOS State

- iOS tracking source is `/Users/huxiaoran/Documents/xiaoranProjects/MyGolfSwingApp` at HEAD `9b99c57d` (`feat: GolfCam v8轨迹节奏重构 + 轨迹调参工具链 + 挥杆分析播放修复`), with `origin/main` still at `b97eef97`.
- Current iOS dirty parity files are `MyGolfSwingApp.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj`, `MyGolfSwingApp/Views/GolfCam/TrackAnimationLayer.swift`, `tools/trajectory-lab/core/algorithm.ts`, and `tools/trajectory-lab/core/algorithm.test.ts`.
- The iOS `TrackAnimationLayer.swift` / trajectory-lab edits define the v8 dynamic launch-speed cap as `(8 + 6 * activeDuration) * (0.55 + 0.45 * speedNorm)` instead of leaving `startSpeedLimit = 0.0`.
- The iOS project file now has `MARKETING_VERSION = 2.5` for both dev and release bundle identifiers; the remaining source-list movement for `SwingAnalysisVideoTrimView.swift` is an Xcode project ordering/structure change, not a new Android runtime behavior.
- `MyGolfSwingApp.xcodeproj/xcuserdata/huxiaoran.xcuserdatad/xcschemes/xcschememanagement.plist` is also dirty in the iOS checkout, but it is user-specific scheme ordering and is intentionally outside the Android parity gate.

### Android State

- `scripts/android_check_ios_alignment.sh` passes against the current iOS checkout: `iosHead=e51fe9c9`, `dirtyParityFiles=4`.
- Android GolfCam already ports the dynamic v8 start-speed cap through `GolfCamSpeedConfig.golfCamV8StartSpeedLimit(activeDuration, speedLevel)` and uses it when building the GolfCam-like head-progress mapping.
- The GolfCam v8 cache key mirrors iOS' exact unquantized renderer inputs—including hold/release/density values, start-speed limit, and `v8.depth-density.2` algorithm version—so small geometry or tuning changes cannot reuse a stale Android mapping.
- `TrajectoryOverlayFrameSampler` routes GolfCam-like preview/export frames through `GolfCamSpeedConfig.trajectoryHeadProgress(...)`, so rendered preview and exported frames share the same iOS-aligned v8 head progress.

## 2026-07-28 GolfCam v8 Follow-Up

### iOS Change Tracked

- iOS `main` is ahead of `origin/main` by commit `9b99c57d` (`feat: GolfCam v8轨迹节奏重构 + 轨迹调参工具链 + 挥杆分析播放修复`).
- The live iOS worktree also contains an uncommitted `TrackAnimationLayer.swift` correction that enables the v8 dynamic `startSpeedLimit` instead of leaving the launch speed uncapped at `0.0`.
- The matching trajectory lab edits are in `tools/trajectory-lab/core/algorithm.ts` and `tools/trajectory-lab/core/algorithm.test.ts`, where the v8 trace now exposes the dynamic start-speed limit and caps high-speed launch jumps.
- The iOS project file `MyGolfSwingApp.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` is also dirty, so Android parity tracking must treat any GolfCam v8 target membership, asset inclusion, or build-structure change as explicitly reviewed rather than only comparing Swift/TypeScript source behavior.

### Implemented in Android

- Android `GOLF_CAM_LIKE` trajectory head sampling now uses a v8-style depth-density mapping instead of the older gamma-only `GolfCamSpeedConfig.progress` path.
- The Android mapping ports iOS' dynamic start-speed cap, apex hold/release shaping, progress depth density, tail release, and geometry-aware duration/descent adjustments into the shared `TrajectoryOverlayFrameSampler`, so preview and export share the same head progress.
- Distance labels shown on GolfCam-like trajectory overlays now sample the v8 trajectory progress on the existing Android/iOS frame cadence while preserving the rounded final distance behavior.
- Android's v8 trajectory cache key now mirrors iOS' exact unquantized inputs, including the dynamic `startSpeedLimit`, progress-density strength/power, tail-release strength/start, hold/release parameters, and `v8.depth-density.2` version, so future GolfCam tuning changes cannot reuse a stale head-progress mapping after geometry or density changes.
- `GolfCamTrackStyleAlignmentTest` now locks the high-speed launch cap with the same 6s / max-speed / `apexProgress=0.70` / 60fps first-frame evidence used by the iOS trajectory lab, including the `>0.55` half-second progress, `<apex` cap, `<0.12` first-frame jump, and `<0.08` final-half-second release checks; it also directly pins the iOS v8 dynamic `startSpeedLimit = (8 + 6 * activeDuration) * (0.55 + 0.45 * speedNorm)` formula for min/default/max speed, and verifies the preview/export shared `TrajectoryOverlayFrameSampler` ignores the legacy progress callback for GolfCam-like styles so both rendered preview and exported frames use the v8 trajectory path.
- GolfCam renderer geometry now follows iOS' actual-video-space contract instead of measuring every curve in an implicit 1:1 square. The shared arc table scales normalized points by the displayed/exported video width and height, solves the cubic apex analytically, applies iOS' `0.915` pre-apex arc correction, and feeds the same aspect-aware table into v8 geometry metrics, legacy motion, ribbon positioning, preview, distance labels, and MP4 export. Landscape and portrait fixtures now produce intentionally different apex/head progress while repeated preview/export geometry remains deterministic. All 190 focused GolfCam style/renderer unit tests pass, and the real-MP4 Track Style plus Overview API 37 tests both pass after the change; same-source iPhone/Android exported-frame comparison remains the final cross-platform visual acceptance step.
- Local release verification now runs with Android Studio's bundled JBR and passes `testDebugUnitTest`, `lintRelease`, `assembleRelease`, and `bundleRelease`; the repeatable preflight entry is `scripts/android_release_preflight.sh`, the current release APK builds at `app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release-unsigned.apk`, and the Play-ready bundle builds at `app/build/outputs/bundle/release/app-release.aab`.
- The preflight now prints source `branch`, `commit`, `dirtyFiles`, targetSdk, and SHA-256 digests for the exact APK/AAB it verifies; it supports `--require-clean-worktree --require-signed --min-version-code=N --min-target-sdk=36` for release-candidate handoff, so Play upload and tester evidence can be tied back to a specific source state, Play API floor, and artifact.
- Earlier release lint cleanup removed the then-current Compose/KTX findings and left only dependency/SDK freshness warnings. The latest full lint reports `0 errors, 57 warnings, 5 hints`, including non-blocking Compose/style/KTX findings; the stricter preflight still allows only its dependency/SDK freshness ids, so that separate launch gate must be cleared before release.
- Release builds now accept injected version/signing inputs through Gradle properties or environment variables (`MYGOLFSWING_VERSION_CODE`, `MYGOLFSWING_VERSION_NAME`, and the four `MYGOLFSWING_RELEASE_*` signing values), keeping no-credential local unsigned builds available while making signed Play upload builds reproducible; invalid version codes, partial signing configs, and release keystore paths that are relative or inside the Android repository fail fast. See `docs/android-release-readiness.md`.
- Android's default release metadata now uses `versionCode = 2` and `versionName = 2.5`, matching the current iOS marketing version while still allowing release-candidate builds to override both values through `MYGOLFSWING_VERSION_CODE` and `MYGOLFSWING_VERSION_NAME`.
- Google Play Billing release-copy handling now has pure helper coverage: release builds hide raw Google Play/backend diagnostics, debug builds keep details for internal diagnosis, support order labels preserve an order id or token suffix, and the three Play product ids are locked by `V2BillingCopyTest`.
- Storage management cleanup now has local coverage for the current iOS six-category accounting and delete boundaries: `清理所有缓存` removes thumbnails, temporary files, avatar cache, and other cache while preserving downloaded videos and local AI models; individual video cleanup leaves thumbnail cache intact; confirmations and failures use the same stable product copy without raw paths or exception detail.
- Home replay-analysis import now follows the iOS 2GB source-size guard for album, camera, and remote-download ingestion; Android cleans up oversized partial copies, opens a post-import trim-confirm screen before saving album footage as a swing clip, uses passthrough MediaMuxer trimming to preserve source quality, advertises the same larger MP4/MOV allowance, and the Swing replay player explicitly uses fit/aspect display so local footage is not cropped in the analysis view.
- Camera/realtime swing-analysis imports now also follow the iOS move-first import optimization: Android archives FileProvider temp videos with a same-volume rename before falling back to copy, reducing post-recording wait time while keeping the copied-file fallback for devices where the move is unavailable.
- Android imported-video filenames now preserve iOS-supported source containers (`.mp4`, `.m4v`, `.mov`, and QuickTime `.qt` normalized to `.mov`) for album import, camera archive, and remote download instead of forcing every persisted source to `.mp4`; unsupported or unknown extensions still fall back to `.mp4`.
- Swing replay gallery saves now keep the same container signal for imported local footage: MOV/QT sources are saved as `.mov` with `video/quicktime`, M4V as `.m4v`, and unknown sources fall back to MP4 instead of every gallery export being labeled `video/mp4`.
- Video Memo gallery saves now use the same container/MIME normalization as share/export: MOV/QT, M4V, and WEBM keep matching extensions and MIME types, while unknown local extensions fall back to MP4 so MediaStore display names and MIME types stay consistent.
- Android now applies the same iOS `.resizeAspect`/full-frame playback rule to all local swing replay surfaces: clip detail, analysis trim, and day-detail playback all use Media3 `RESIZE_MODE_FIT`, with `V2SwingMediaTest` locking the three entry points so local analysis footage is not cropped.
- Home replay-analysis source QA is tracked as its own launch evidence item (`QA_HOME_ANALYSIS_SOURCE_EVIDENCE`), requiring the current iOS two-source paths—realtime recording and album trim confirmation—before release sign-off. The checker no longer requires the retired third `已有素材库` entry.

### Current Android Launch Focus

- Production signing and Play Console delivery still need real release evidence; local output is an unsigned APK.
- Google Play Billing remains the biggest external launch gate: product activation, real test purchases, grant/consume recovery, and backend receipt/token validation need evidence before payment-enabled release.
- Device QA should prioritize the paths that local tests cannot prove: camera/media picker permissions, GolfCam preview/export visual output, gallery save, plaza cloud interactions, login continuations, and release-copy failure states.
- Dependency freshness warnings (`compileSdk` 37 availability, AGP/Gradle/Kotlin/AndroidX/Media3 updates) should be reviewed in a separate compatibility pass after the current release candidate is stable; the current Play targetSdk floor is enforced by preflight and launch evidence.

## 2026-06-27 Fast-Track V2 Parity Pass

上线优先级按“用户是否点得到、点进去是否能完成主流程、失败时是否有出口”排序。

### Implemented in Android

- The iOS navigation atlas `APP-001`, `ROOT-001`, and `HOME-001` are represented by Android's splash/bootstrap root, V2 tab scaffold, and Home tab; startup chooses login or Home from session state, then Home provides goal cards, quick add, common tools, recent Memo, plaza recommendations, AI, Plaza, and Me tab exits.
- Root startup and post-auth background profile refresh now preserve coroutine cancellation while still degrading ordinary cloud refresh failures, so Android can finish opening the iOS-aligned app shell without turning disposed root work into a completed sync; `V2RootScreenTest` locks this boundary.
- Profile user-info and credits refresh now also use a cancellation-safe background boundary: ordinary refresh failures no longer escape the app-scope coroutine while the loading state still clears, and cancellation is preserved for disposed profile/root work; `V2ProfileStoreTest` locks this behavior.
- Home common tools now route all four primary actions: recording, replay analysis, trajectory creation, and online download.
- Account-gated actions now preserve their launch context through login/register: Android returns to the triggering page or tab after auth, keeps the business back stack before that page, and no longer always resets to Home, which keeps download, purchase, plaza, AI, and profile flows closer to iOS' contextual login handoff.
- Account-scoped creation actions now continue after login: tapping new Goal/Text Memo/Video Memo from Home, Memo lists, All Goals, or the Quick Add sheet while logged out signs in first, then opens the originally requested creation page instead of only returning to the previous tab.
- Profile account-surface actions now also continue after login: logged-out taps on edit profile, goal stats, video-Memo stats, swing stats, AI archive, credit center, and account security sign in first, then open the originally requested profile destination.
- Plaza account-surface actions now continue after login for favorites and the direct watch-history route: logged-out taps sign in first, then return to Plaza with the independent `我的收藏` sheet open or back into the watch-history page instead of stopping at the generic Plaza tab.
- Register and reset-password screens now match the login screen's keyboard/navigation-bar safe-area handling, so auth forms keep their bottom actions reachable on gesture, three-button, and small-screen Android devices.
- Android's main login surface now matches the current iOS `PhoneLoginPage` fallback paths: the imported golf-course Hero keeps the exact 360dp/100dp fade, 80dp rainbow logo footprint, 32sp title, 13sp English line, and 9dp three-value ribbon glyphs; the card width now evaluates exactly as `min(screen - 32dp, 420dp)` and uses the same tab/divider rhythm, 48dp cream inputs, 50dp country/SMS controls, 56dp primary action, error icon row, underlined guest/register links, and 16dp agreement control with gold legal links.
