SMS sending and login submission now have independent progress states, so requesting a code no longer locks or animates the main login action. Backend-safe mainland-phone and exact six-digit-code validation remain intact. API 37 verifies both fallback modes, empty password validation, guest continuation, and registration routing in `login-sms-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `dc8fa590af50169568064da9e2985e2af65afa51b2280fd57c2e2d3a7556bf0f`) and `login-password-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `816fe79a5a413c1755fcdc7e0aa3b1cf53b2dfeab8bb132024300457ffd32434`). iOS carrier one-click login remains a platform/SDK-only capability and is not represented by a fake Android action.
- Change-password / set-password now matches iOS' `passwordSetupAlert` instead of navigating to an Android-only auth page: it opens over the live account-settings page with the exact 40% dim layer, 360dp/24dp branded card, byte-identical `GolfCourseBackground` header, green overlay, key/title/subtitle hierarchy, 48dp cream fields, paired `取消 / 确认` actions, and success hold before dismissal. Existing-password and bound-phone SMS paths keep their real backend operations; the validation order and exact mismatch/code/current-password copy follow iOS, while release-safe transport errors remain localized. API 37 verifies both dialog variants and the first validation state in `account-password-dialog-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `93c78aac37d4f4e41adfcffb667b82f6bb402a065509634aab5fa7c1ff354492`).
- The iOS navigation atlas `AUTH-005 完善资料页` now uses a dedicated Android two-step flow instead of relabeling the ordinary profile editor. Step one renders the byte-identical golf-course background, two-step progress, glass `最后一步` intro, five preset avatars with a 120dp selected preview, generated/editable 15-character nickname, save-first note, and real preset-key update. Its nickname clear action now uses the same filled-circle `xmark.circle.fill` treatment, and saving runs the same case-insensitive substring/whitelist moderation before any profile request; Android bundles the current iOS `sensitive_words.json` byte-for-byte (SHA-256 `eab3aaa92faab4a4ff2507497284d8a341c13694b4874d1f7ce042ee033d21f3`) rather than maintaining a divergent short list. Step two mirrors `CompleteProfileOptionalDetailsView`: imported boy/girl artwork plus deselectable privacy choice, the four iOS learning buckets and suggested/adjustable handicap, deselectable dominant hand, ten-brand multiselect, real save, and a no-write skip path. Its handicap editor now also matches the SwiftUI medium sheet rather than using a centered Android dialog: `调整差点` uses leading cancel/trailing confirm navigation, integer and decimal values are real center-snapping draggable wheels, and `设为暂无` retains the exact hierarchy. Explicit optional-field clearing updates both cloud payload and local session rather than retaining stale values. Password registration always enters AUTH-005 and lands directly on step two after the authenticated session loads, matching iOS; login/reset still offer it only when the raw profile is incomplete and no business continuation is pending. The post-auth choice is now a deterministic tested boundary: blank raw nicknames enter AUTH-005 only without pending work, while every download/create/plaza/AI/QR/analysis/trajectory/shared-video/coach-invite continuation wins even when registration requested forced completion. Four refreshed API 37 tests verify persisted selections, skip behavior, pre-request rejection of a sensitive nickname, and the `20.0 → 21.0` wheel/confirm flow; evidence includes `complete-profile-step-one-content-filter-final.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `9290b601ecc56c7f8f014bb229e92492563fd9e27d1b7cc6603939cd6251972a`), the normal first step in `complete-profile-step-one-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `c83ccc5878f50cd9690f7cf5a830a4b51bd99f003099f0ece8731517632550e8`), `complete-profile-step-two-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `41d4067f49ecc8cfa91e20c75a0f5dc2a167a5f6b1024e25c0ccce68768a262a`), `complete-profile-step-two-actions.png` (SHA-256 `90086393d0d9b01623f472d146aff13902a737d088e55d62b465b103eb13cf7a`), and `complete-profile-handicap-sheet-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `ba63b130b1627e07aea0d8a59b8b7fd5c61eeaed287ec55bb5722dc003d08ee6`), all 1440×3120.
- Android's `AUTH-001` login surface now mirrors the current iOS hierarchy and visual tokens: the byte-identical golf-course image and rainbow-ring logo drive a 360dp Hero, the `优质视频 / 练球心得 / AI 助手` ribbon sits above a white card pulled upward by 40dp, and the agreement row lives outside that card. SMS and password states retain iOS-sized fields, full account placeholder, unified `登录 / 注册` CTA, and mode-specific `先看看 / 现在注册` footer actions; `先看看` enters the existing logged-out Home experience without creating account state.
- The iOS navigation atlas `AUTH-002 注册页` is represented by Android's current registration surface and follows `RegisterPage`: the golf-course Hero uses the same title, subtitle, three-value ribbon, 40dp translucent back action, and pure `chevron.left` glyph rather than Android's former shafted arrow; the first preset avatar and generated nickname are selected by default; five preset avatars plus an adaptive-width `自定义` photo-library entry share the same row; a selected custom image is previewed locally and uploaded only after account creation; upload failure keeps the authenticated account and exposes the iOS-matched `重试上传头像` recovery path instead of registering twice. The card now evaluates exactly as `min(screen - 32dp, 420dp)` and uses the exact 24dp radius, 20×18dp padding, 12dp section rhythm, avatar divider, four 48dp cream input rows, username/nickname leading glyphs, `已帮你生成昵称，可直接注册 / 已使用你的自定义昵称，换头像不会覆盖` state, and live `换一个` nickname action. Agreement uses the 16dp checkbox and gold links; the 52dp create action, underlined inline login, and 44dp guest action remain outside the card. API 37 verifies the full top and bottom hierarchy without creating an account in `register-ios-parity-chevron-final.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `84fb39cd4f1ea0307294d96504b3b66521779e79f796dad47d213fe854e9322a`) and `register-ios-parity-actions.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `7a2a51040dd3e1c9a395ea1e78d150e1586bb7e9e0bbbfec4cd94bddbf051640`).
- The iOS navigation atlas `AUTH-003 忘记密码页` is covered on Android: password-login "忘记密码" opens `V2ResetPasswordScreen`, SMS reset can auto-login with the new password, and the shared post-auth refresh can still route to `AUTH-005 完善资料页` when no business continuation is pending. The complete page now mirrors current `ForgotPasswordPage`: the 260dp golf-course Hero has the 36dp dark back circle, pure `chevron.left` glyph rather than Android's former shafted arrow, and title-only hierarchy; the card evaluates exactly as `min(screen - 32dp, 420dp)` and uses four 48dp cream input containers, `+86` and the 76dp verification action stay inline, SMS sending and password submission use independent progress states, validation follows the iOS field order, and the 54dp action, translucent support card, and 300dp success overlay use matching metrics. API 37 verifies the initial hierarchy, empty-submit error, and back action in `reset-password-chevron-final.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5222288d13614cebb9540e713a8dea55df076d7db94bacd686158f051ecfef00`).
- The iOS navigation atlas `LEGAL-001 用户服务协议` and `LEGAL-002 隐私政策` are covered by Android's shared legal document screen from login, register, profile, and account-security surfaces, with asset-backed content and safe bottom scrolling.
- `LEGAL-001/002` now also match the current iOS `LegalDocumentView` presentation instead of rendering the full Markdown source inside one oversized Android card. Android uses the same 44dp warm toolbar and close target, 180dp accent Hero, 200dp offset decoration, 64dp document/privacy icon, 24dp title, 13dp update line, 20dp content inset, 24dp section rhythm, red warning panel, chapter-number capsules, independent 14dp white body cards, contact divider/mail row, copyright footer, RoomBackground, and 40dp bottom clearance. The visible and bundled versions match iOS—Terms `2026年8月31日`, Privacy `2026年8月28日`. The parser preserves tables, bullets, subsections, emphasis, and local long-form scrolling while intentionally retaining Android's Google Play and Tencent service disclosures rather than copying incorrect App Store text. Three parser/source tests and two API 37 long-page tests cover both assets, contact scrolling, and dismissal; screenshots are `legal-terms-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `eefeb1721ad14858a05a5583f2cd54c7cbb12562cbb0f2563767c5eeca7be2e7`) and `legal-privacy-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `94565743f35335b6f8a7425b305febee6f5f345e0c4b6e779ce63642644d8e6e`).
- SMS authentication now matches both the current iOS request and the deployed `userAuth` contract: ordinary code requests send only `action + phoneNumber`, phone login sends `action + phoneNumber + six-digit code`, and every session-issuing or refresh request carries one installation-scoped random `deviceId` that survives logout without relying on a hardware identifier. Android validates the backend's exact mainland-mobile shape (`1[3-9]` plus nine digits) and the six-digit code before network submission, so unsupported `10x` / `12x` prefixes no longer reach the service and collapse into a generic failure path. Read-only live probes against the deployed endpoint also confirmed its current explicit responses for an invalid phone (`请输入正确的手机号`) and an invalid/expired code (`验证码错误或已过期`). Release copy still hides arbitrary backend/exception/path detail, but a curated allowlist preserves those actionable server responses plus cooldown limits; common timeout, DNS/connectivity, TLS/certificate, HTTP 5xx, and JSON-decoding diagnostics are now converted into distinct safe recovery messages instead of misleading every non-credential failure as `请检查账号信息`, while debug builds retain the raw diagnostic for unknown failures. Repository and UI tests lock the payload metadata, exact phone/code validation, deployed response contract, safe backend/transport recovery mapping, and cancellation behavior.
- Auth login/register/reset-password flows now also clear loading states through `finally` when SMS, login, registration, reset, or post-reset auto-login calls throw before returning a handled `Result`, keeping the iOS atlas `AUTH-001/002/003` entry paths recoverable.
- The learner bottom tabs now match the current iOS `V2BottomTabBar` contract end to end: Home, Memo, Tools, and Me retain the same 19dp glyph while selection changes only tint and the 4dp dot; the former visible `广场` label is absent. The center `挥呗` control keeps the 54dp fairway button, 4dp white border, complete 58dp selected gold ring, connection-aware `连接教练 / 教练互动` semantics, and the red count capped at `99+`. Its outer layout now reserves the raised ring instead of constraining it to the 54dp button. A focused Pixel 7 Pro API 37 test exercises every tab, the center callback, both connection states, and badge capping; evidence is `student-bottom-tabs-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `6d98119f1e854e338f32aab66af6d05d0989a419506772d0c089459fbeca70c7`).
- Home header controls follow the currently rendered `V2HomeHeader`: the 48dp avatar keeps the fairway-tinted shadow and 2.5dp role-aware ring, while the 36dp `记录` capsule uses the ordinary light add glyph. Current SwiftUI has no avatar gesture or developer sheet, so Android's former hidden 1.5-second long-press route and its non-functional force-empty preference have been removed from both debug and release source.
- Shared V2 avatar fallback now mirrors iOS' `V2Avatar` behavior rather than stopping at a generic account glyph: when a name is available, Android renders its uppercased first character on the same sand-beige fallback across Home feedback/header, role selection, profile settings, invites, space switchers, teaching discussions, coach student cards, review cards, and profile editing. Every live call site sizes the glyph at roughly iOS' `avatarSize × 0.38` instead of using one oversized default. HTTP(S), iOS-style `file://`, and Android `content://` image references are all loadable; loading failures retain the name initial instead of leaving an empty circle, while preset avatars remain authoritative. Unit tests lock Chinese/Latin/blank-name resolution plus accepted/rejected URI schemes, and two API 37 Compose device tests verify both missing-avatar and unavailable-remote-avatar initials.
- Reset-password support tips now match iOS' informational boundary: the email and phone rows use the same muted 11.5sp copy inside the translucent bordered tip card and no longer add Android-only mail/dialer actions.
- Account security and account deletion flows keep destructive/sign-out actions above Android system navigation, and the deletion-complete return action now uses iOS' stable 50dp tap target.
- Account deletion's logged-out state now has a working login-first path instead of a disabled terminal button; after auth, Android returns to the deletion confirmation context.
- Profile edit avatar upload, account-security dialogs, and account deletion now preserve coroutine cancellation in their thrown-error helpers, keeping dismissed profile/account surfaces from reporting false release failures while ordinary backend/local exceptions still show stable localized copy.
- Feedback now keeps the cloud-backed submit form reachable above keyboard/system navigation and shows inline 500-character validation instead of only disabling the submit action.
- Feedback submit failures now use a cancellation-aware release-copy helper, keeping cloud/legacy-channel diagnostics hidden in release builds while preventing cancelled feedback work from surfacing a stale submit error.
- Legal document loading failures no longer expose asset paths in release builds; Android now shows stable localized recovery copy while debug builds can keep the document path for diagnosis.
- Legal document top chrome now mirrors iOS' modal viewer more closely: Android removes the duplicate centered navigation title above the hero and uses a right-aligned 24dp muted close-circle action while keeping the shared legal content renderer and back behavior.
- The iOS navigation atlas `GOAL-001`, `GOAL-002`, `GOAL-003`, `GOAL-004`, `GOAL-005`, and `GOAL-006` is represented on Android: users can create goals, attach existing Memo through `V2GoalMemoPickerSheet`, open all goals, inspect detail, edit existing goals in the same full-screen form, and delete from either all-goals swipe actions or the edit screen with confirmation.
- Profile and profile-settings scroll surfaces now also respect Android navigation-bar insets, including the logout Snackbar, bringing the Me tab's bottom spacing closer to iOS safe-area behavior.
- Full-screen Memo list surfaces now respect Android navigation-bar insets for all-Memo, video-Memo, and text-Memo lists, so empty-state actions and final rows keep the same bottom breathing room as iOS list pages.
- Text Memo and Goal create/edit forms now match the video-Memo form's keyboard/navigation-bar handling, keeping long-form input and bottom save actions reachable across Android keyboard and system-navigation modes.
- Goal list and detail pages now keep their floating create and bottom check-in/pause actions above Android system navigation, matching iOS' bottom-action safe-area behavior for the goal workflow.
- Swing recording and swing-library screens now respect Android navigation-bar insets for capture controls, saved-clip feedback, selection delete actions, and the replay-analysis material grid.
- The iOS navigation atlas `HOME-002 快速添加` is covered by Android's shared `V2QuickAddSheet`: Home shows the 2x2 new-goal / video-Memo / text-Memo / swing-record grid, Memo shows the video/text/swing grid, each card uses the iOS-style photo background, bottom scrim, and layered translucent icon badge, and each account-scoped creation path preserves the login continuation.
- The historical atlas-only `HOME-004 V2 DevSwitcher` is not part of the current iOS render tree or `V2HomeHeader`. Android therefore does not expose a private avatar long-press page; a source regression test rejects the removed callback, timer, copy, and preference entry.
- The iOS `HOME-003 自定义工具栏` sheet remains reachable from `更多`: Android matches its centered title, subtitles, switches, long-press drag handles, and local persistence. The saved visibility and order now feed the compact discovery row immediately and survive leaving/re-entering the page, closing the previous write-only setting defect.
- Home common-tool customization now opens as a bottom sheet instead of a centered dialog, matching iOS `V2HomeToolBar`'s `.sheet` presentation more closely while keeping Android's local arrow reorder controls.
- Home common-tool customization rows now mirror iOS' row metrics and disabled-state hierarchy more closely: Android uses the same 14dp row spacing, 4dp vertical row padding, iOS `tool_*` image assets at the 22dp row size, 50% row opacity when a tool is hidden, and per-tool checked switch tint matching iOS `.tint(tool.color)`, while retaining the local arrow-based reorder controls.
- Home common-tool cards now match iOS' custom icon treatment more closely: Android keeps the 46dp accent circle and renders the same `tool_swing` / `tool_analyze` / `tool_path` / `tool_download` image assets at the iOS 28pt-equivalent size instead of Material fallback glyphs.
- Home common-tool cards now mirror iOS' `V2HomeToolCard` shadow token more explicitly: Android sets both ambient and spot shadow colors to deep-green 7% on the 16dp / 18dp card shadow instead of leaving part of the platform shadow at its default color.
- Home common tool cards now shrink long titles before ellipsizing, matching iOS' minimum-scale behavior and reducing clipped labels on small Android screens or larger font settings.
- Home common-tool empty hint now mirrors iOS' centered empty state: when all quick tools are hidden, Android centers `点击「自定义」添加常用工具` across the row instead of leaving the full-width text start-aligned.
- Home common-tool toolbar now uses the iOS component-owned 20dp horizontal inset for its header, empty hint, and scroll content instead of inheriting the page's 16dp section padding, matching `V2HomeToolBar`'s `.padding(.horizontal, 20)`.
- Home focused-goal deck now matches iOS' Hero card rhythm more closely: Android uses the 220dp card height, 16dp inner padding, 10dp vertical content spacing, 236dp pager/deck height, and 10dp stacked-card Y offset from `V2GoalHeroCard` / `V2GoalCardDeck`.
- Home focused-goal deck now mirrors iOS' cyclic pager behavior: Android uses a 3-copy virtual pager, starts from the middle copy, maps stacked cards and dots through the real goal index, and silently resets back to the middle copy after boundary swipes so the deck loops continuously.
- Home focused-goal Hero card shadow now follows iOS' style-colored shadow more closely: Android applies `style.shadowColor` to both ambient and spot shadow channels for the 24dp card elevation instead of leaving the ambient shadow as the neutral platform default.
- Home focused-goal Hero card header chip now mirrors iOS' `今日聚焦` glyph metrics more closely: Android keeps the 28dp gold bordered tile but reduces the play glyph from 14dp to the iOS 10pt-equivalent size.
- Home focused-goal Hero card footer now mirrors iOS' `footerRow` spacing more closely: Android uses a real weighted spacer between the Memo/video meta and check-in button instead of a fixed 10dp gap, and matches the separator dot's 5sp tracking.
- Home focused-goal Hero card check-in button now matches iOS' internal glyph metrics: Android uses the 11dp state icon inside the 16x8dp rounded action capsule, matching the SwiftUI 11pt bold symbol size while keeping the existing state/background behavior.
- Home recent-Memo header now matches iOS' information hierarchy: Android removes the extra inline count next to `最近 Memo`, uses the larger section title style, and switches the right-side `全部` affordance to the same fairway-colored forward-arrow treatment.
- Home recent-Memo header right affordance now matches iOS' `HStack(spacing: 4)`: Android uses a 4dp gap between `全部` and the forward arrow instead of the tighter 2dp spacing.
- Home recent-Memo video and mixed cards now match iOS' play affordance more closely: Android uses the white `play.circle` treatment positioned near the left side of the thumbnail instead of a centered white button with a green triangle.
- Home recent-Memo mini-card chips now mirror iOS' shared `V2Chip` token mapping more closely: Android uses the white 18% / white 25% bordered dark chip for video cards, mist-green / deep-green neutral-light chip for text cards, 10dp icons, and the same 10x5dp capsule padding.
- Home recent-Memo video and mixed card internals now match iOS' `V2HomeMemoCard` behavior more closely: Android always renders the duration badge with a `0:00` fallback like `memo.durationSec ?? 0`, and uses the iOS 6dp video / 4dp mixed title-meta spacing split.
- Home recent-Memo video and mixed card title fallback now mirrors iOS' `displayTitle` helper: Android no longer injects the Android-only raw `title` fallback before the body first-line fallback.
- Home recent-Memo video and mixed card relative-time labels now follow iOS' single-line caption behavior: Android applies ellipsis overflow so long localized time copy stays within the 200dp mini card.
- Home recent-Memo empty list handling now mirrors the latest iOS section behavior: Android keeps the header, shows skeleton cards only during startup loading, and otherwise leaves the horizontal row quiet instead of adding an Android-only empty prompt card.
- Home recent-Memo video and mixed placeholder thumbnails now mirror iOS' `V2HeroGrassBG` fallback: Android keeps the dark-green linear base, overlays the shared hero grass image at 55% opacity, and scopes that richer placeholder to Home mini cards so other Memo thumbnail surfaces keep their existing topic gradients.
- Home recent-Memo text mini cards now mirror iOS' `textCard` structure more closely: Android uses the 12dp `DSSpacing.md` card padding, removes the extra Android-only accent chip from the top row, and renders the body from `memo.text` without falling back to the title.
- Home recent-Memo text mini card footer now follows iOS' single-line bottom row behavior: Android applies ellipsis overflow to both the relative date and word-count labels, preventing long metadata from crowding the 200dp card.
- Home recent-Memo and plaza recommendation sections now share iOS' tighter horizontal-section rhythm: Android uses `sm` spacing between headers and rows, 11dp forward arrows on `全部`, and the smaller plaza play marker in the header.
- Home plaza recommendation cards now reuse the same iOS `V2Chip(.neutralDark)` token treatment for the top category chip: Android uses the white 18% fill, white 25% hairline border, 11sp medium label, and 10x5dp capsule padding instead of the older black overlay pill.
- Home plaza recommendation top category chips now keep the same compact one-line behavior as the iOS card: Android applies ellipsis overflow so long category names stay inside the 200dp card rather than expanding the header row.
- Home plaza recommendation card typography now mirrors iOS' `V2HomePlazaCard` tokens more closely: Android uses `DSTypography.caption` for the duration badge and bottom meta row and `DSTypography.cardTitle` for the two-line title instead of smaller local 10/11/13sp overrides.
- Home plaza recommendation bottom metadata now follows iOS' one-line truncation behavior: Android applies ellipsis overflow to the coach/category text row so long names do not hard-clip inside the 200x140 card.
- Home plaza recommendation cards and loading placeholders now match iOS' card treatment more closely: Android uses the shared `Radius.md` corner radius, renders the skeleton with top chip/duration placeholders plus bottom title/subtitle blocks instead of a plain gradient tile, and adds the same full-card 0/18/0 white diagonal shimmer overlay while keeping placeholder opacities fixed.
- Home plaza recommendation fallback thumbnails now use the iOS Home-specific two-color gradient mapping instead of the Plaza feed's heavier three-color thumbnail gradients, so no-cover cards match `V2HomePlazaCard.gradientPlaceholder`.
- Home recent Memo now shows animated loading skeletons while cloud sync is in progress, then falls back to the true empty state only after sync ends.
- Home plaza recommendations now match the latest iOS empty-list behavior: Android shows the same three 200x140 skeleton cards whenever the recommendation list is empty, instead of switching to an Android-only `暂无广场推荐` empty hint after loading.
- Home decorative footer now mirrors iOS' `flag.checkered` cue more closely: Android uses the Material `SportsScore` outline icon at the same 18dp scale and deep-green 55% tint instead of the plainer flag outline.
- Home common tools match the current iOS quick-tool dispatch: record opens swing capture, analysis opens the two-source live-recording/album sheet, trajectory opens GolfCam, and download opens the V2 download page; existing clips remain in the neighboring `挥杆记录` archive.
- Home common tools now render outside the active-goal block like iOS, so first-run or no-active-goal users still see recording, replay analysis, trajectory creation, and online download after the empty-goal card.
- Home empty-goal card now mirrors iOS' `V2HomeEmptyCard` polish more closely: Android adds the same `DSSpacing.sm` top offset before the 84dp target circle, uses the 36dp target glyph size instead of the oversized 40dp icon, applies the iOS card-shadow treatment with an 8dp black 6% shadow, removes the extra zero-height spacer that doubled the primary/secondary action gap, and gives the secondary plaza action the same bottom `DSSpacing.sm` padding.
- Replay analysis opens the usable Android swing material flow and can continue into the existing frame-step swing detail page.
- The separate `挥杆记录` archive defaults to the latest date with clips when today is empty, so saved material remains useful without restoring the retired third source inside `回看分析`.
- Replay-analysis album imports now preserve a source label on the created swing clip and show it in the swing library card, clip detail, and generated review Memo, making imported analysis material easier to identify later like iOS' analysis-import notes.
- The iOS navigation atlas `TRAJ-001`, `TRAJ-003`, `TRAJ-004`, `TRAJ-005`, and `SYSTEM-002` is covered by the Android GolfCam flow: Home opens the trajectory source sheet, system video selection enters or restores the six-step workflow, completed cached work opens the overlay preview, and export/save writes the rendered MP4 to the gallery with release-safe failure recovery.
- The iOS navigation atlas `DOWNLOAD-001` and `DOWNLOAD-002` is covered by Android's V2 online download page and confirmation state, including URL recognition, platform metadata, credit cost, confirmation/cancel paths, and login/credit recovery.
- The credit-backed video-information confirmation now uses iOS' full-screen dialog shell instead of Android's former inline card/action block: it shows the seal cue, title fallback, platform, one-decimal MB size, `m:ss` duration, credit cost, and the exact `确认下载 / 异步下载 / 取消` hierarchy. Foreground confirmation proceeds directly without a redundant second large-file choice, asynchronous confirmation enters the persistent queue, and cancel still calls the server refund path. API 37 verifies all three actions and the complete hierarchy in `online-download-confirm-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `012c65cdb78b2c0bd599e2ae82cda4688f115fdeb42aa210182f1d6b0f19bf93`).
- Video-information fetching now uses iOS' blocking `V2DialogShell` state instead of leaving only an inline status card in the scroll view. The 28% scrim, green progress indicator, current extraction message, source URL with a three-line limit, 24dp rounded card, border, and elevated shadow are aligned; outside taps and system dismissal cannot accidentally interrupt or hide the active request. API 37 evidence is `online-download-fetching-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `35026aeff67a7fa67c7770b9c007e759ada946db43389b94eab9680cb228f0c7`).
- Download completion now uses the matching iOS dialog shell instead of a Material alert: the 64dp leaf-green completion cue, `下载完成` title, source/credit sentence, and capsule actions are aligned. Home/tool downloads expose `创建练球 Memo / 继续下载`; plaza-origin downloads expose only `完成` and return to the calling player instead of leaving the user on a reset downloader. API 37 verifies both source variants and actions in `online-download-success-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `4348fe2e97d70b1a1278712943ce409663028f2794c5d6300b4cea77c0b5071e`).
- Online download has a V2 page with login guard, credit refresh, Douyin start/confirm/cancel, foreground and asynchronous progress, cancel refund messaging, failure recovery, pending-confirmation restore, persistent background-task management, post-download Memo creation for the Home/tool foreground path, and a separate iOS-matching Plaza direct route. Plaza playback URLs bypass the Douyin metadata/start/confirm APIs, download immediately to a private staging file, publish to MediaStore, remove the staging video/thumbnail, and create no Android-only Memo. The 10-credit local balance update and success handoff occur only after gallery publication succeeds; publication failure returns to a visible retry state without deducting credits.
- Online download statistics now use the same persistent global model as iOS instead of recomputing counts from Memo rows. Successful normal foreground downloads increment the daily, inclusive 30-day, total-count, and cumulative-byte records; older Android download Memos are migrated once. The visible summary is now exactly `今日下载 / 近 30 天 / 累计大小`, including iOS' one-decimal MB/GB formatting. API 37 verifies persistence plus `online-download-summary-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `ef3aa1b49623cf0e9027329c9827854777869158d3c31e7f1a22fadfb7c9d39c`).
- Normal foreground downloads now also publish their downloaded container to the system gallery before completing, while retaining the private source needed by the created Memo; a gallery failure does not destroy the usable Memo, matching iOS' best-effort Photos save behavior.
- Online download hero now mirrors the iOS V2 information hierarchy more closely: grass-course background, Douyin/download badge, launch copy, and compact capability pills for cost, confirmation, and Memo creation.
- Online download navigation now follows iOS' source-aware chrome instead of the generic Android `视频下载` title. The 36dp mist back action, centered title/subtitle, bordered white async-manager action, warm-white surface, and bottom divider are aligned; normal entry renders `抖音视频下载 / 粘贴链接，下载练球素材`, while Plaza entry renders `保存广场视频 / 下载到本地并保存相册`. Both actions and both variants pass on API 37; evidence is `online-download-topbar-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `1b1360e6ca1cac801c429e115aba8c85f4497d4b7902ec0397672c58d312e08c`).
- Online download's idle body now follows the current SwiftUI render order, design tokens, and action placement instead of the retired Android composition. The 20dp/shadowed Hero, translucent metric pills, 14dp white cards, exact 116dp muted input and placeholder, credit-card/music/bag icon roles, sparkles idle cue, centered footer, `提示 / 知道了` error alert, persistent `充值` label, silent empty clipboard, and active displayed-URL subtitle are aligned. The white `下载积分` card replaces the black three-metric panel; `抖音链接 / 视频地址` and the live service badge lead the input card; `粘贴` and `开始下载 / 保存到本地` are the only input actions; and the bottom capability card remains exactly `可接触页面`. API 37 verifies the refined hierarchy and callbacks in `online-download-idle-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `4479e9ca9228e0c5339b892cee5b2c1add7990e79a26ee49a96196c090d0965e`).
- The download connection badge no longer claims `服务在线` unconditionally. Android now runs the same one-shot HTTP-200 check as iOS against `https://download.mygolfai.com.cn/`, uses cancellable OkHttp execution, preserves coroutine cancellation, and renders the exact gold `等待检测` versus green `服务在线` states. The production root returned HTTP 200 during this checkpoint, and API 37 verifies all three internal probe states without exposing transport diagnostics.
- Online download completion now matches iOS' explicit success handoff: Home/tool sources show a success dialog after local or online Memo creation with direct exits to the new Memo or another download, while Plaza sources publish the downloaded video to MediaStore, remove private staging artifacts, create no extra Memo, show only `完成`, and return to the caller. API 37 publishes, queries, and cleans up a real non-empty foreground Plaza gallery item.
- The iOS navigation atlas `DOWNLOAD-003 -> MEMO-003 创建练球 Memo` is now represented after Android downloads: the completed-state CTA and success dialog open the generated video Memo in edit mode first, so users can review title, notes, tags, and linked goals before saving into the player.
- Online download active states now show elapsed time and live phase copy during fetch/confirm/download/cancel, matching iOS' progress-card feedback more closely and making slow download jobs easier to diagnose.
- Online download release failures and cancel notices now avoid exposing raw exception/backend messages during fetch, cancel, and confirm; Android shows stable recovery copy, keeps cancel/refund guidance visible for confirmed jobs, and only appends low-level details in debug builds. `V2OnlineDownloadCopyTest` locks release hiding of URL, token, job id, backend endpoint, user-path, and local-file diagnostics while preserving debug troubleshooting detail.
- Online download now also recovers from thrown start/cancel/confirm/download/Memo-creation exceptions, not only backend `Result` failures, so failed jobs return to an actionable idle/ready state instead of leaving the iOS-parity progress UI stuck in fetching, confirming, cancelling, or downloading.
- Online download task lifecycle calls, repository Result helper, and failure-result rendering now preserve coroutine cancellation instead of wrapping it as a backend failure, so fetch/confirm/cancel cancellations do not show false failure copy or incorrectly roll the task into retry guidance. `V2VideoDownloadRepositoryTest` covers the repository boundary directly.
- Online download's input CTA now stays actionable for launch-critical account/credit states: valid links route logged-out users to login and low-credit users to purchase, while unsupported non-Douyin sources use iOS' blocking guidance instead of creating an Android-only online Memo.
- Online download now synchronously preserves an unstarted pasted URL before every login, purchase, and download-history handoff instead of relying on a later Compose effect that might lose an immediate tap. Anonymous drafts migrate once into the signed-in account, normal and Plaza-direct drafts use separate scopes, and active/pending jobs cannot be overwritten by a stale idle draft. The existing pending-job restore path remains authoritative once a real task starts; all 18 API 37 download-page/manager tests pass, including the new handoff/migration/isolation boundary.
- Online download pending-task restore parsing is now covered by `V2OnlineDownloadCopyTest`: malformed or incomplete payloads are ignored, saved job id/status/message/cost/video info are restored, and missing cost falls back to the launch credit cost instead of corrupting the resumed confirmation state.
- The iOS navigation atlas `DOWNLOAD-004 异步下载管理` now uses both the same manager hierarchy and a real Android background engine instead of the former `下载记录` substitute. Confirmed direct URLs enter an account-scoped persistent multi-task queue backed by constrained foreground WorkManager jobs; the queue survives navigation/process recreation, restores interrupted running work, reports progress, supports pause/resume/failure retry/removal/clear-completed, publishes successful files to `Movies/挥呗` on modern Android, and posts a completion notification when authorized. Logout cancels and removes only the departing account's queue records while preserving downloaded personal video files. The page keeps iOS' centered `异步下载` chrome, `关闭 / 清空完成`, three-column `任务概览`, exact empty copy, file-size/status rows, progress bar, and status-specific controls. API 37 verifies real persistence/owner isolation plus populated and empty UI states and all visible actions in `async-download-manager-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `43ffe6423ce659bd7b8c98dbe890bdba909dce7df02fbd6393855c64ee242cab`).
- The iOS navigation atlas `DOWNLOAD-005 异步下载建议` now has the same visual and functional Android decision path: recognized files at the iOS 10MB threshold show the 28% dialog scrim, 64dp fairway download cue, `检测到大文件`, exact centered file-size guidance, and capsule `异步下载 / 继续当前下载 / 取消` hierarchy instead of a Material alert. Explicit cancel is required, matching iOS' non-dismissible overlay. The confirmation page also exposes `异步下载` directly. Selecting it confirms the credit-backed server job once, extracts the returned direct URL, persists and starts the background task, requests notification permission only when needed, and leaves the foreground page free for another task; foreground confirmation/download and cancel/refund remain independent alternatives. API 37 verifies all three actions in `online-download-large-file-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `e21f59b58343e0821bb0adfe7bf8d6976f54e0351cc7969d0c257a1f8d1ba032`).
- Online download now turns supported non-Douyin links into a direct "添加在线视频 Memo" primary action instead of leaving the main button disabled, while preserving the login guard before creating the Memo.
- Online download's supported non-Douyin "添加在线视频 Memo" path now continues after login: logged-out users sign in first, then Android opens the video-Memo creation flow and preloads the original URL in the online-video picker instead of stopping back on the download page.
- Online download's platform support UI now distinguishes "可下载" from "在线 Memo" instead of presenting supported non-Douyin platforms as purely coming soon.
- Async-download manager empty state now matches iOS literally: first-time users see the outlined tray cue, `暂无异步任务`, and `大文件视频可在确认时加入异步下载。`, while `清空完成` is disabled until a completed task exists. The previous filled Android Inbox substitute is removed and source-guarded; API 37 whole-page evidence is `async-download-manager-empty-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `9ef4411c6e4eb3c985de363a279cbea07aab2951e6c20b188c4b0aa351e46add`).
- Async-download task and empty states respect Android navigation-bar insets, keeping the final task row and pause/continue/delete controls reachable like iOS' safe-area manager view.
- Async-download manager has an account-scoped login state: logged-out or session-expired users see `登录后查看异步下载` instead of another account's queue, and both the download-page history action and the boundary card preserve `Destination.DownloadHistory` as the post-auth continuation. The nineteen-test API 37 class verifies the boundary copy plus close/login actions in `async-download-manager-login-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `73f6a66b0761c58f8003f913aacb37e0a1276f3c1c41847923854dd5ab99f105`).
- Shared text links from Android system share are routed into the V2 flow: Douyin links open the download page, while other supported platforms open the online video Memo picker.
- Clipboard behavior now follows each iOS source: online download silently keeps the current draft when the clipboard is empty, while the online-video selector retains its localized manual-paste guidance; URL copy only reports success after `setPrimaryClip` succeeds.
- Online video picker now matches the iOS top-bar and helper-copy details more closely: Android balances the back and confirm sides with the same 60dp minimum rhythm, and uses the iOS invalid-link/help wording for supported platform recovery.
- Online video picker now mirrors the iOS elevation hierarchy more closely: Android adds the fairway glow behind the empty-state link orb, the 1dp soft card shadows on the URL input / supported-platform surfaces and platform tiles, the 6dp preview-card shadow, and the stronger fairway shadow behind `添加到 Memo`.
- Online video picker icons now mirror iOS' visual scale more closely: Android uses the 28dp empty-state link glyph and renders the preview play affordance as a 48dp translucent circle with a 16dp play glyph nudged right, matching `V2OnlineVideoSelectorView`.
- The iOS navigation atlas `MEMO-001`, `MEMO-002`, `MEMO-004`, `MEMO-005`, `MEMO-006`, `MEMO-007`, `MEMO-008`, `MEMO-009`, `MEMO-010`, and `MEMO-012` is covered on Android by the Memo tab, shared quick-add sheet, text create/detail/edit flow, video/text secondary lists, video Memo player, swing recording/library/detail entries, and online-video selector.
- Plaza player no longer uses local-only interaction placeholders for the core actions: like, save/favorite, comments, report, claim, coach profile, saved list, watch history, and replay from saved/history cache are connected.
- The iOS navigation atlas `PLAZA-001` and `PLAZA-002` is covered by Android's plaza feed and immersive player, including Hero/category/search/list states, Home recommendation entry, playback controls, coach entry, like/save/comment/download/Memo/feedback actions, and watch-history persistence.
- Plaza grid, coach-profile favorite buttons, and the plaza header favorites entry now use the same login-first behavior as the player instead of relying only on store-level save failure fallback or showing a false empty account sheet.
- Plaza list, interaction, and coach-profile release failures no longer expose raw backend exception text; Android keeps localized retry copy for refresh/load-more/like/save/coach loading while debug builds can append low-level detail. Store-level lastError rendering now uses the shared throwable helper for refresh/load-more/like/save rollback paths, `V2PlazaCopyTest` locks release hiding of backend, endpoint, token, user-path, video-id, and coach-id diagnostics for these paths while preserving debug troubleshooting detail and coroutine cancellation, and `V2PlazaRepositoryTest` covers the repository Result helper's cancellation boundary.
- Plaza list refresh/load-more and coach-profile loading now also preserve coroutine cancellation and clear loading states through `finally`, so thrown cloud/category/coach failures cannot leave the plaza feed or coach page stuck on a spinner or write a false localized error after disposal.
- Plaza header now mirrors iOS' title treatment more closely: Android uses a fairway-green `Eco` leaf-style icon matching iOS `leaf.fill` instead of the older generic plant glyph/plain-dot treatment, and aligns the subtitle indent to the 28dp title-icon width.
- Plaza header now mirrors iOS' collapsed search posture and action order: the 44dp filled-bookmark `我的收藏` action precedes `搜索视频`, and the full search field expands only after the user opens search or a query exists.
- Plaza hero card now matches iOS' badge, decorative glow, and play-control details more closely: Android adds the sparkles icon before `今日精选`, restores the top-right trophy-gold radial glow from `V2PlazaHeroCard`, and uses the smaller 14dp play triangle inside the 44dp white circle.
- Plaza hero card shadow now matches iOS' fairway-tinted elevation: Android applies fairway 12% to both ambient and spot shadow channels for the 24dp rounded Hero card instead of relying on the neutral platform shadow.
- Plaza hero play button now matches iOS' optical centering: Android offsets the 14dp play triangle 1dp to the right inside the 44dp white circle, mirroring SwiftUI's `offset(x: 1)`.
- Plaza hero play button now also mirrors iOS' local button depth: Android adds the black 15% / 4dp circular shadow behind the 44dp white play control from `V2PlazaHeroCard`.
- Plaza hero coach chip now mirrors iOS' bottom-info treatment more closely: Android uses the same fairway-to-muted-green gradient behind the 16dp coach initial avatar instead of a flat fairway fill.
- Plaza grid cards now match the latest iOS thumbnail composition more closely: Android removes the extra centered play-circle overlay from grid thumbnails while keeping the full-card tap target, top category/NEW chips, bottom save button, and duration chip.
- Plaza category chips and grid-card elevation now track iOS' exact micro-chrome more closely: Android uses the 10dp category-chip spacing, fairway-tinted selected-chip shadow, and the same light 8dp / 4% black card shadow used by `V2PlazaVideoGridCard`.
- Plaza discovery ordering and grid metadata now follow the current iOS contract: the feed defaults to `最新`, exposes the fixed-width `最新 / 高分` switch beside the category rail, sends the selected sort to refresh and pagination, limits `NEW` to the three newest videos inside the 48-hour window, and uses category/view count plus saved/favorite/relative-date metadata instead of Android-only generic labels. Guest save controls also carry the iOS-style lock marker. The side-by-side emulator exercised both sort modes against the live feed without clipping or a runtime crash.
- Plaza category empty state now mirrors iOS' `V2PlazaView` copy hierarchy: Android names the selected category in `「分类」暂时没有视频`, uses the shorter `换一个分类试试` guidance, and switches the regular empty icon/title treatment away from the search-style state.
- Plaza coach profile now matches the iOS black immersive hierarchy end to end: a 220-point banner with purple/navy fallback and bottom scrim carries the 72-point gradient-ring avatar, name, badges, and certification; bio expansion, colored platform links with account/link copying, IP region, four threshold-aware public totals, `作品` count, tall dark video cards, and explicit loading/error/empty states follow below. The Android light room card, generic navigation bar, three-stat row, embedded save buttons, and `教学视频` section title have been removed from this legacy dark page. The top floating Home action still resets to the V2 Home tab, cards open the plaza player, and back returns to the previous page.
- Plaza player account actions now preserve logged-out intent for launch-critical non-mutating flows: comment opens the comments sheet after login, download opens the existing V2 download page with the current video URL, and report/claim opens the feedback dialog after login instead of stopping back on the player.
- Plaza player now exposes a visible "下载" action that routes the current plaza video URL into the V2 download page in a plaza-source mode, matching iOS' "保存广场视频" entry path while reusing Android's credit confirmation, progress, and Memo creation flow.
- Plaza-source download now has a launch-safe fallback when the backend rejects or cannot parse a real VOD URL: Android keeps the failure inline on the download page and exposes a no-credit "save as online video Memo" path so users can still play, review, and link the plaza video instead of being stranded behind a failed download dialog.
- Plaza player can create a replay Memo directly from the current plaza video, giving Android a launch-safe equivalent for iOS' "保存广场视频" review workflow without requiring local download first; repeat taps reopen the existing plaza Memo instead of creating duplicates, and local write failures recover the button state with a visible toast.
- The iOS navigation atlas `PLAZA-003 评论 Sheet` and `PLAZA-005 视频反馈 Sheet` are represented on Android: the plaza player loads/sends cloud comments, reports comments, preserves logged-out intent into comments/feedback, and submits video report or content-claim requests through `V2PlazaRepository`.
- The iOS navigation atlas `PLAZA-004 保存到视频备忘录` now has an Android confirmation sheet: tapping the player Memo action opens `保存到视频备忘录`, shows source context, lets users keep/remove the default category / 广场视频 / 在线视频 tags, and then creates or reopens the plaza-backed video Memo.
- Plaza player playback failure/no-source states now match the iOS review-safe pattern more closely: they preserve video context, explain expired/blocked stream scenarios, offer retry/return, and surface Memo creation when an online source still exists.
- Plaza player bottom progress controls now respect Android navigation-bar insets, giving the full-screen player the same safe-area breathing room as iOS and preventing the scrubber from sitting against system navigation.
- Plaza player progress controls now show current time and total duration above the scrubber, and the current label follows the drag target while seeking, matching iOS' clearer playback-position feedback.
- The iOS navigation atlas `MEMO-014 视频对比` is represented by the current live SwiftUI route rather than its former expanded design: the more-tools `对比` action first opens the black source/empty stage, then the Room-backed candidate picker, then a full-screen two-pane player with independent/synchronized seeking, shared mute, drawing overlay, and comparison-only `稳 / 细 / 帧` scrub feel. Android-only VS overlay, replacement toolbar, frame capture, and save-review actions were removed because the current iOS render tree no longer exposes them.
- Video Memo local-video export feedback preserves coroutine cancellation, so leaving the player cannot turn disposed MediaStore work into a stale gallery-failure capsule; `V2MemoMediaTest` locks this helper boundary.
- Video Memo comparison sharing now mirrors iOS' `分享对比视频` affordance: Android pauses both players, shares one or two local videos through FileProvider-backed `video/*` intents when available, and falls back to comparison text plus online playable links for remote sources.
- Video Memo comparison now mirrors iOS' top-right mute affordance: Android keeps a comparison-scoped mute state, applies it to both ExoPlayer instances, and preserves the selected sound state when replacing the comparison video.
- Video Memo comparison synchronized scrubbing now follows iOS' merged-drag-bar behavior: enabling `同步` hides the per-pane scrubbers, shows one shared bottom scrubber with both timestamps, and clamps the seek target separately for each video duration.
- Video Memo comparison selection now matches iOS' full-screen picker posture more closely: Android uses a warm-white selection surface with a close nav bar, selectable video cards, explicit `确认选择`, and a full-screen empty state instead of an immediate-action alert dialog.
- Video Memo comparison picker cards now mirror iOS' visual video cards more closely: Android shows the Memo thumbnail or first-frame fallback in a 96x64 rounded preview with a play overlay, while retaining selected-state checkmarks and metadata.
- Video Memo comparison feedback follows iOS' in-screen notice pattern: Android shows share failures and unavailable-sync guidance in a top capsule inside the comparison player instead of sending those actions to system Toasts.
- Video Memo comparison time labels now match iOS' precision: Android displays visible comparison timestamps and saved `视频对比复盘` time markers as `M:SS.mmm`, while keeping the main player on the lighter tenth-second format.
- Video Memo creation/editing now mirrors the current iOS local-video preview: the preview stays muted, uses the platform video controller, keeps the duration badge on the parent card, and no longer adds Android-only `手感 · 稳/细/帧` or mini-timeline controls before saving.
- Video Memo creation preview now mirrors iOS' `videoPreview` play affordance more closely: Android keeps the 202dp preview height, uses the shared 52dp translucent play circle with 2dp white stroke and 18dp `play.fill`-style glyph for local/online preview overlays, and uses the iOS-style rectangular video glyph at the 28dp empty-preview scale instead of Android's circular play marker.
- Video Memo creation note toggle now mirrors iOS' `noteSection` detail more closely: Android uses a 13dp muted chevron for the collapsed/expanded note row instead of the heavier default Material arrow size.
- Video Memo creation expanded note field now mirrors iOS' joined-capsule treatment: Android switches the note header row to top-only 12dp corners when expanded, letting the 100dp editor panel keep the bottom-only corners instead of rendering two separately rounded cards.
- Video Memo creation expanded note editor now matches the iOS `TextEditor` field rhythm more closely: Android gives the editable area its own 80dp minimum height and nudges the empty placeholder by the same inner top/leading padding used in `V2MemoCreateVideoView`.
- Video Memo creation title input now mirrors iOS' `titleSection` focus feedback: Android switches the 1dp underline from border gray to fairway green while the title field is focused, matching the SwiftUI `focusedField == .title` treatment.
- Video Memo creation no longer renders the older linked-goal chips: the helper still exists in the Swift file but is absent from the current iOS body, so Android now follows the actual visible hierarchy and silently preserves pre-existing links during edit saves.
- Video Memo creation source controls now match iOS' card density more closely: Android uses the 64dp source-chip height, 12dp corners, 18dp icons, fixed medium-weight 12sp chip labels, and iOS-style `保存中…` / `更新中…` progress copy for the bottom save button.
- Video Memo creation source section now matches iOS' lighter hierarchy more closely: Android removed the extra white 24dp wrapper card and 8dp shadow around `视频来源`, leaving the title, source chips, preview, and downloaded-source summary as the same unframed section structure used by `V2MemoCreateVideoView`.
- Video Memo creation top bar now follows iOS' single-save hierarchy: Android removes the duplicate top-right save action, keeps the bottom fixed save button as the primary commit path, and uses the warm-white nav bar with a 32dp close button, balanced right spacer, and bottom divider.
- Video Memo creation bottom save bar now mirrors iOS' `bottomBar` structure more closely: Android adds the top 1dp divider and uses the 20dp horizontal / 12dp top / 32dp bottom padding around the destination copy and 52dp fairway save capsule.
- Video Memo creation's selected online source now mirrors iOS' complete `videoPreview` layering: Android preserves the recognized source title separately from the editable Memo title, replaces the former long-URL fallback with the 36dp circular link treatment and two-line source title, adds the 35% top vignette for remote covers, restores the 12dp bottom-trailing expand glyph, and overlaps the expanded note borders into one joined capsule seam. Its complete draft is now saved-state aware: camera/album/online source identity, adopted local paths, thumbnail, duration, platform, recognized source title, title, note expansion, selected tags, hidden goal links, pending camera path, permission state, online-picker visibility, and discard confirmation survive Android activity/process recreation instead of collapsing back to an empty form. The online-source device test consumes and clears the parent handoff first, selects `上杆`, recreates the page, and only then verifies the recovered source metadata and tag, so recovery is not being faked by the original navigation parameter. API 37 verifies both the empty and restored selected forms in `memo-create-video-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `8ba0bc314f0d80c784027506d865e197bfb0f81e947badb40a7f9815c187ea8e`) and `memo-create-video-online-selected-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `f4b87bace93a4e826bf1fe6ff5f36e49088bf95da84cc1b148277bbc60acfdb6`).
- Video Memo dismissal and edit-source behavior now follow iOS' `confirmationDialog` and immutable-source contracts instead of Android's centered alert and dormant recovery branch. Closing a dirty draft first clears focus/keyboard, then shows a bottom action sheet with grouped title/message, destructive `放弃`, separated `继续编辑`, navigation-safe spacing, and 34% scrim; both continue and discard paths are exercised. Edit mode now has only the lock row and preview—`更换视频`, recovery copy, and the impossible replacement callback were removed. API 37 evidence is `memo-create-video-discard-sheet-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `288fa6c4a6ef2d32da207995df7cf9ec0110b56ed3287fe5bb008c8cacf7a5c7`) and `memo-video-edit-source-lock-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a7d4e7073eb6eb5f1505ad35c2424e8bfb75681fc8fb77011c26c8fde7fb4eed`).
- Video Memo edit source locking now matches iOS' cleaner edit-state hierarchy: Android normal edit mode shows the lock explanation plus preview without an extra source chip, while the recovery source-replacement path still keeps its explicit source label.
- Video Memo playback now covers iOS' `导出` more-tools action for local Android videos: the player menu writes the current Memo's local video file into `Movies/MyGolfSwing` through MediaStore, keeps online/remote videos on a clear localized unavailable path, and cleans up incomplete gallery entries on write failure.
- Video Memo playback sharing now matches iOS more closely: Android shares local Memo videos as real `video/*` streams through the app FileProvider, while remote/online videos still fall back to sharing the playable link and Memo context text.
- Video Memo playback feedback now mirrors iOS' in-player notice pattern: Android shows share/export progress and failure/success copy in a top black capsule inside the full-screen player instead of relying on system Toasts for those core actions.
- Video Memo playback drawing/playback state now follows iOS' guardrails: Android closes the drawing panel on playback errors, disables the drawing entry when playback is blocked, and dims/locks the central play button while drawing tools are active.
- Video Memo playback bottom progress now matches iOS' composite-control structure more closely: Android replaces the Material slider with a thin blue progress bar and 16dp white thumb that supports absolute tap/drag seeking above the frame-level timeline strip.
- Video Memo playback top chip now matches iOS' visual treatment more closely: Android renders the `视频Memo` pill with the same dark-green fill plus fairway-green outline instead of a flat dark pill.
- Plaza feed loading/error/empty states now match the iOS hierarchy more closely: loading shows spinner plus copy, failures show an error icon/title/explanation/retry action, and search/category empty states split title and guidance instead of collapsing into one muted line; favorites owns its corresponding states inside the separate iOS-style sheet.
- Plaza feed bottom footer now mirrors iOS' `V2PlazaFooter`: when the list has no more videos, Android shows the `已经到底了` divider row plus a deep-green `回到顶部` affordance that animates the grid back to the first item instead of only showing a passive `已加载全部视频` label.
- Plaza feed bottom footer now also matches iOS' `arrow.up` affordance more closely: Android uses an 11dp upward arrow glyph instead of the heavier 14dp chevron for `回到顶部`.
- Plaza grid card text clipping now follows iOS' `lineLimit` behavior more closely: Android applies ellipsis overflow to the top category chip, two-line title, and single-line coach name so long labels truncate like `V2PlazaVideoGridCard` instead of hard-clipping.
- Plaza grid card top category chip shadow now matches iOS' explicit black 18% / 3pt micro-shadow instead of relying on the platform default shadow color.
- Plaza grid card bottom stats now mirror iOS' `play.circle` semantics for public view counts, using a circular play glyph instead of reusing the solid duration/play triangle.
- Plaza grid card titles now follow iOS' `lineLimit(2)` behavior without forcing a two-line minimum, so short titles keep the same compact title-to-coach rhythm as `V2PlazaVideoGridCard`.
- Plaza filter chips now mirror iOS' single-line chip behavior: Android applies one-line ellipsis to the main `CategoryPill` rail when category names are long.
- Plaza home cards, grid cards, player stats, and coach profile stats now share the iOS V2 public-display count rules: low-view videos show category/new/featured copy instead of cold-start `0` counts, low favorites show `收藏`, and 10,000+ values use the Chinese `万` compact style.
- Plaza favorites no longer use the former Android-only saved/search/filter replacement mode. The separate sheet now uses the same server-backed account list and simple empty guidance as current iOS, so closing it always returns to the untouched discovery category/search/pagination state.
- Plaza like/favorite/comment/report/claim/download/create-Memo actions now reject logged-out users before optimistic UI mutation, navigation, local write, or form submission and route to login from the player, preventing false interaction states and wasted input when account-scoped actions cannot run.
- Plaza like/favorite optimistic sync now preserves coroutine cancellation and only rolls back on real cloud/local failures, so cancelled player/list lifecycles no longer surface false localized errors or undo settled UI state.
- Plaza comments now match iOS' in-sheet retry posture more closely: comment load/send failures show inside the comments dialog, typed text is kept for retry, and editing the draft clears the error instead of relying on a transient top toast.
- Plaza comments now preserve review context on failures: load/send/report errors render as an inline banner while any already-loaded comment rows remain visible, matching iOS' sheet behavior better than replacing the whole list with an error state.
- Plaza comment load/send now also recovers from thrown repository/cloud wrapper failures: Android clears loading/submitting through `finally`, preserves coroutine cancellation, and keeps the comments sheet actionable with localized inline retry copy.
- Plaza player release errors now avoid exposing raw exception/backend messages for Memo creation, plaza refresh, comment load/send/report, and video report/claim submission; Android shows stable localized recovery copy, with low-level details appended only in debug builds. The shared Plaza throwable helper now preserves coroutine cancellation for these player/comment/report surfaces, and `V2PlazaCopyTest` covers comment-id/video-id diagnostic hiding plus cancellation behavior.
- Plaza comment-report and video report/claim submissions now also recover from thrown repository/cloud wrapper failures: Android clears the submitting state through `finally`, preserves coroutine cancellation, and keeps the relevant dialog/sheet actionable with localized retry copy.
- Plaza comments dialog now also matches more of iOS' visible information hierarchy: the title includes the comment count, each row shows the commenter avatar or fallback initial, and comments include relative time copy such as `刚刚` / `N分钟前` / `N小时前`.
- Plaza comment and report/claim dialogs now avoid Android keyboard/system-navigation overlap, keeping send/submit/cancel actions reachable while typing like iOS' safe-area sheets.
- Plaza comment moderation now matches iOS' comment sheet capability: Android can report another user's comment through the same `reportComment` cloud action, shows a confirmation dialog, keeps logged-out users behind login, and surfaces success/failure without closing the comments context unexpectedly.
- Plaza report/claim submission feedback now matches iOS' sheet-result posture more closely: Android shows success/error inside the feedback dialog, keeps failed submissions editable for retry, and briefly displays success before closing instead of relying only on transient toasts.
- Plaza watch history is account-scoped and local like iOS: Android no longer records anonymous history while logged out, keeps up to the same 200 records per account, and direct history entry shows the exact no-write/login boundary instead of claiming cross-device sync.
- Plaza watch history now matches the iOS dark sheet hierarchy: the top-left close action is a bare purple down-chevron, real covers no longer receive Android's extra center play overlay, fallback covers retain the muted video glyph, dividers begin at the iOS 92-point inset, zero-progress rows omit status copy, and list/empty/login states reserve navigation-safe breathing room. Current V2 Plaza also omits the Android-only `最近看过` rail that had no counterpart in `V2PlazaView`.
- Plaza saved/video-history cache restore now has direct parser coverage in `V2PlazaCopyTest`: malformed payloads fall back to empty lists, bad rows are skipped, legacy saved-video rows still recover to a playable saved-card placeholder, and watch-history progress clamps back into the valid range instead of corrupting the account-scoped history entry.
- Video Memo playback failure/no-source states now use the iOS-style review overlay with device/error iconography, centered guidance, clearer cross-device sync copy, and retry/return button hierarchy.
- Video Memo edit mirrors iOS' current locked-source policy: opening edit from the standard player actions hides the camera/album/online replacement controls, shows the same lock guidance, and limits changes to title, note, tags, and linked goals. Missing-source/playback-error overlays are intentionally read-only like the live SwiftUI tree and no longer expose the obsolete Android-only replacement route.
- Video Memo edit missing-record recovery now uses the same Android/iOS-style missing-entity page as the player/detail surfaces, with a real back action, instead of leaving users on a bare "视频 Memo 不存在" text state.
- Video Memo player now covers the iOS launch-critical controls in a bottom playback bar: slow playback, 5-second skip back/forward, play/pause, more actions, drawing, compare, category edit, delete, and system sharing of video Memo information without leaking unusable local file paths; Android also recovers with a localized toast if the OS share sheet cannot be opened.
- Video Memo player top chrome mirrors iOS' `视频Memo` identity chip: Android keeps the dark-green bordered capsule, video-library glyph, 12dp icon, `返回` close action, and stateful drawing-tool accessibility copy matching the `play.rectangle.on.rectangle` intent from `V2VideoMemoPlayerView`.
- Video Memo player delete now uses the same save-safe destructive posture as text Memo detail: Android waits for `deleteAndWait` before leaving the player, shows `删除中…`, blocks duplicate delete/cancel taps while in flight, and stays on the player with a localized `删除失败` dialog if persistence throws.
- Video Memo player deletion now also mirrors SwiftUI's compact system-alert geometry instead of Android's oversized Material card: a centered 270dp light-material panel uses the iOS 17sp/13sp title-message hierarchy, gray separators, equal 44dp blue-cancel/red-delete actions, and the exact current copy while retaining the safe `删除中…` state. Its persistence-failure branch now uses the same 270dp system-alert geometry and 44dp iOS-blue `好` action instead of falling back to a large Material dialog. Player state is recreation-safe: play/pause intent, current position, 1x/0.5x/0.25x rate, drawing/more-panel visibility, delete confirmation, comparison route, and share-to-space route survive Android activity/process recreation; transient loading failures, notices, and in-flight delete work deliberately do not. API 37 pauses a real MP4 at 0.5x, opens deletion, recreates the page, and verifies the restored controls/alert in `video-memo-player-restoration-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `bdb9192421a6cee9042db0e8c649d6d91ca013f6dc43da1ea97809de92c26941`). A second deterministic device flow suspends the first request, proves cancel/confirm are disabled and no duplicate request starts, injects persistence failure while retaining the player and record, then retries successfully; its failure surface is `video-memo-delete-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `6e3ac15c602f55fb87c6993223ff2ca846a7e9dacfe31016fc698ab7613b45dd`).
- Video Memo player bottom controls, drawing tools, and compare picker now respect Android navigation-bar insets, matching iOS' full-screen player safe-area behavior.
- Video Memo player now has the full continuous iOS-style scrub timeline: a waveform axis supports `稳 / 细 / 帧` density modes, absolute drag-to-time seeking, bounded inertial decay, pause-on-interaction, and conditional resume only when playback was active before the gesture; the center marker and precise slider remain available for orientation and direct seeking.
- Text Memo creation and detail now match the iOS safety and editing behavior more closely: new text Memos can be linked to active goals, closing a draft text Memo prompts before discarding title/body/tag/goal changes, detail edits can adjust linked goals, destructive delete requires confirmation, and backing out with unsaved edits prompts before discarding changes.
- Memo create/update/delete now uses iOS-style optimistic list updates on Android: new async saves appear immediately, edits and pin changes refresh before Room emits, deletes are tombstoned briefly to avoid flicker, and the optimistic cache is bounded to pending upserts so truly deleted records do not linger.
- Text Memo creation now matches iOS' form validation more closely: Android requires both title and body before save, caps titles at 24 characters and body text at 240 characters, and trims saved content to avoid empty/whitespace-only Memo records.
- Text and Video Memo creation now match iOS' save-safety behavior more closely: Android waits for the local save before closing, disables duplicate submits while saving, blocks accidental close during saving, and keeps the form open with an explicit failure dialog instead of silently dismissing it. Video creation uses the current iOS `操作失败` dialog title while retaining actionable localized save-failure detail.
- Video Memo create/edit now takes the save lock synchronously before launching persistence, eliminating the former pre-coroutine double-tap race. API 37 suspends the first create request, verifies `保存中…` plus disabled save and inert close behavior, injects failure while retaining the draft, then confirms one successful retry. Edit mode separately proves a failed update keeps the changed title, original stored record, locked source, and same compact recovery alert before returning exactly once on retry. Evidence is `memo-video-create-save-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5d0785cd19ff6265555a9062ec5b8d0653a6b87eb0732fdf25ffc02d0faf1c3d`) and `memo-video-edit-save-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `20df28ea0f3d6457048731e1d62bbc47339adf3021469ccf543f4c444a284c62`).
- Text Memo creation now takes the same synchronous pre-coroutine save lock, snapshots the submitted fields, blocks close/back and duplicate submission while suspended, and preserves the full draft after injected persistence failure. Its former Material alert is replaced by the shared 270dp iOS system geometry while retaining SwiftUI's Text-specific `保存失败` title. API 37 verifies failure, dismissal, one successful retry, and exact draft retention in `memo-text-create-save-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5ec08a978ab98e9944b989169d3d1167c923b1b2fd85041e133761e8c53a1b28`).
- Text and Video Memo creation release failures no longer expose raw local/cloud save exception text; Android keeps the draft open with stable localized retry copy, while debug builds can append low-level detail for internal diagnosis. `V2TextMemoCopyTest` locks Text Memo release hiding of database, backend endpoint/status, memo-id, tag-id, user-path, and local-path diagnostics across create-save, detail-save, and delete failures; `V2VideoMemoCopyTest` locks Video Memo release hiding of activity, content-URI, local-file, database, memo-id, and user-path diagnostics across camera launch/adopt, album import, and save failures while preserving debug troubleshooting detail.
- Text/Video Memo and Goal save flows now preserve coroutine cancellation instead of treating navigation/disposal cancellation as a user-visible `保存失败`, while real local/cloud persistence failures still keep the draft form open with localized retry guidance.
- Memo tab cloud sync now also preserves coroutine cancellation at the store boundary: Android can still best-effort through local demo cleanup, cloud pull, and dirty push failures, but login/list disposal cancellation is no longer swallowed by bare `runCatching`; `V2MemoStoreTest` and `V2MemoRepositoryTest` lock this behavior.
- The iOS navigation atlas `MEMO-013 标签选择 / 新建标签` is represented by Android's current Memo tag flow and follows the current Swift render tree instead of the older navigation-atlas interpretation: text Memo create and video Memo create/edit render the grouped `V2MemoTagPicker` directly in the form, while `+ 新建` alone opens the new-tag sheet and preserves the selected group/name/keyboard-safe actions.
- The shared Memo new-tag sheet now also preserves its presentation, typed tag name, and selected category group through Android activity/process recreation, so either text- or video-Memo creation cannot silently discard an in-progress custom tag. The category chips expose their selected state to accessibility and device assertions. API 37 recreates the open sheet after entering `节奏训练` and selecting `身体部位` in `memo-new-tag-restoration-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `1978f72df259aae0dfb4ec7c0a25fcfe2074df067cb1387a687df7fc31ae8d5c`).
- Memo home footer now mirrors iOS' AI-FAB clearance: Android keeps the `查看全部 Memo` footer structure and adds the same 32dp trailing space after the footer so the bottom-tab center AI button does not visually crowd the final training-count copy.
- Memo home AI insight card now mirrors iOS' bottom-row anchoring more closely: Android separates the top chip/title/body content from the source/continue row, pins that bottom row to the card's lower edge, and reserves space under the body so longer insight copy cannot overlap the footer controls.
- Text Memo detail tags now mirror the latest iOS detail layout exactly: Android renders the full grouped tag list inline with 15sp `标签`, the static fairway `选择下方标签` guidance, 11sp group names, and selected fairway capsule chips. The Android-only `+ 添加标签` action and extra `标签选择` intermediary sheet were removed from detail editing; tags toggle immediately on the page like Swift. API 37 verifies the grouped inline hierarchy in `memo-detail-tags-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `c4b5503bf5fe5e5226016ace34b3f96433a487e7dca444ad82076e30ebcd1147`).
- Text Memo detail linked goals now match the same iOS lightweight section rhythm: Android removes the nested white editor card, uses the 15sp deep-green header, keeps the empty copy inline, and renders goal chips as white capsules with priority dots, subtle shadow, and fairway selected borders.
- Text Memo detail header chips now mirror iOS' `headerSection`: Android removes the enclosing priority card and `优先级` label, leaving the compact priority capsule plus parent-category capsule above the 30sp title.
- Text Memo detail background now matches iOS' `DSColors.warmWhite` page base instead of the older Android green-to-cream gradient, so the edited detail sections read as one consistent iOS-style surface.
- Text Memo detail inline tag and linked-goal sections now share the same 16dp content rail as the title/body/attachments, removing the extra nested Android inset so the section rhythm matches iOS' `.padding(.horizontal, 16)` layout.
- Text Memo detail linked-goal priority dots now match iOS' `goalDotColor`: critical red, high orange, normal fairway, and low gray instead of Android's older yellow/bright-green mapping.
- Text Memo detail linked-goal chips now use the same light black 6% / 3dp capsule shadow as iOS' `goalChip`, rather than relying on the platform default shadow color.
- Memo home section header icons now match the latest iOS row semantics more closely: video notes use the document/article icon, text notes use a chat bubble, and swing records use a rectangular play-display icon instead of the older Android circular play treatment.
- Memo home section header count spacing now matches iOS' single 4dp/pt rhythm: Android removes the extra literal space before `N / total` and relies on the same leading padding used by `V2MemoSectionHeader`.
- Memo home AI insight card copy now follows the iOS `V2MemoAIInsightBridge` rules more closely: empty state uses `还没有洞察内容` / `记录你的第一条 Memo 后...` / `📊 暂无数据`, profile-only states use `📊 暂无 Memo 样本`, sampled states show `📊 基于最近 N 条 Memo` capped at 7, and the disabled `记录后解锁` affordance keeps the fairway color like iOS.
- Memo home AI insight body now mirrors iOS' segment-rich text treatment: Android tokenizes the status summary or generated coach sentence and renders focus/pain/goal keywords in fairway semibold while keeping ordinary body copy muted gray.
- Memo home header now mirrors iOS' title/action spacing more closely: Android keeps a 12dp minimum gap between the left title block and the `新建 Memo` capsule, matching `V2MemoHeader`'s `Spacer(minLength: 12)` behavior on tight widths.
- Text and video Memo secondary list nav actions now follow the latest iOS placeholder model: Android keeps the single 44dp search affordance visible but disabled, and `V2MemoSecondaryListFilterTest` covers the still-active category/source chip filters for those lists.
- Text Memo secondary-list large cards now match the iOS bottom row hierarchy more closely: Android removes the redundant meta/date line from the card footer and places word count on the left with tag chips on the right in the same row, relying on the iOS-style left date block for timing context.
- Quick Add sheet visual details now match iOS more closely: Android uses the same black 50% dim behind the bottom sheet, removes the extra white stroke around the 36dp image-card icon badge, adds the iOS-style black 45% / 2px card text and chevron shadow, keeps the title/subtitle stack at the same 4dp spacing over the image cards, and now renders the same deep-green fallback gradient under each quick-add image resource.
- Quick Add sheet now matches iOS' split horizontal insets more closely: Android keeps the image-card grid at 16dp while insetting the beige `取消` button to 20dp, matching `V2MemoCreateSheet`'s separate grid and cancel-button padding.
- Quick Add guest authentication now matches iOS' custom prompt instead of using Android's narrow platform dialog: it uses the centered 360dp-capped white card, 22dp corners, subtle border and shadow, 40dp lock badge, exact action-specific copy, and equal 46dp `返回` / `登录/注册` actions. The 16% overlay dismisses on outside tap just like iOS. API 37 verifies both the Memo 2+1 grid and Home guest prompt in `quick-add-memo-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5ef058a12fe261c437d8fb2fc74bf61efcd2326cb4dc064fd1f350a047183791`) and `quick-add-guest-prompt-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `34d601a24fd403f467d18498533068ee055c0d5134ba8e27669e9d2228e4db04`).
- Text Memo creation now follows the current iOS render tree exactly: although `V2MemoCreateTextView` still contains a dormant `linkedGoalsSection` helper, it is not inserted in the active form, so Android no longer exposes an extra goal-association section during creation. Goal linking remains available from the dedicated detail/goal flows.
- Text Memo creation title entry now follows iOS' lightweight `titleSection`: Android removes the nested `标题` label/outlined field from the form card and renders a bare 22sp bold title input with the muted `标题` placeholder and 1dp divider above the body card.
