- Text Memo creation body and following sections now match iOS' page-level rhythm: Android removes the large all-in-one form card, renders the body as its own 16dp white rounded card with a light 6dp/4% shadow, `开始写今天的感悟…` placeholder, 200dp input area, counter, and floating mic affordance, then places the disabled video-reference affordance and grouped tags as separate page sections.
- Text and Video Memo creation tag sections now match iOS' form picker behavior: Android renders grouped tags inline with `标签` + `+ 新建`, maps known group SF Symbol names such as `figure.golf`, `figure.stand`, `flag.fill`, `timer`, and `list.bullet` to matching muted Material glyphs, and shows selectable chips directly in the page instead of a summary card that opens a separate selection sheet.
- Memo tag creation now matches iOS' sheet posture more closely: Android replaces the Material alert dialog with a warm-white bottom sheet, custom grabber, V2-styled tag-name input, horizontally scrolling category chips, centered 48dp cancel/create capsules, and category icons in the picker group rows.
- Memo tag creation, online-video selection, and video-Memo fallback URL entry now keep their input fields and confirm actions above Android keyboard/system navigation, closing another iOS safe-area gap in the Memo creation flow.
- Text Memo's create-page `挂个视频参考？` row is the same intentionally unavailable, 70%-opacity locked affordance as iOS, including the `挂视频参考暂未接入` accessibility boundary and no click action. Detail now follows the current Swift contract too: `暂不支持添加` is static, an empty card says `暂无附件`, and no Android-only explanation dialog is inserted.
- Video Memo camera permission recovery now has a release-safe system-settings fallback: if Android cannot open the app settings page after permanent camera denial, the flow shows localized retry guidance instead of silently failing or crashing.
- Video Memo camera permission requests now also have a launch-safe fallback: if Android cannot open the runtime permission prompt, the form stays open and guides users to retry or use the album path. API 37 now injects this failure and verifies the entered title survives, the compact iOS-style alert dismisses cleanly, and album recovery remains available.
- Video Memo camera capture launch now has the same release-safe fallback as profile avatar capture: if Android cannot open the system camera after permission is granted, the temporary file is cleaned up, the draft stays open, and users get localized guidance to retry or use the album path. API 37 verifies the failed `capture_` output is absent and records the compact alert in `memo-video-camera-launch-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `8dc357ac384de429a2aceab3d04efde15c29d31dc2ccfbb510efb54a641263b5`).
- Video Memo album selection now has a launch-safe picker fallback: if Android cannot open the system media picker, the create/edit page stays open and shows localized retry copy instead of dropping the user into a dead tap.
- Video Memo camera/adopt and album/import file processing now also clears the importing state on thrown media-store/file-copy/thumbnail failures, cleans up failed camera output, and keeps the create/edit form recoverable with localized retry guidance. API 37 injects both exceptions after a delivered picker result, verifies the title/form remain editable and all source routes recover, and records `memo-video-camera-processing-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `f00cfcb0b3b2709b7b28c0f97fbef00f4d12f173ec0a0e37e8da1ebbf7fd835f`) plus `memo-video-album-import-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b6351d03a3afe392fe8e31e051b32b7ee7317c378d6ccb1d0f32f37e12657afe`).
- Text Memo creation now includes the same iOS-style muted "挂个视频参考？" row after the body field, preserving the V2 creation information hierarchy without pretending attachment picking is already wired.
- Text Memo creation now also mirrors iOS' muted voice-input affordance: Android shows a disabled mic circle inside the body field so the future voice-entry slot is visible without advertising active recording.
- Text Memo creation voice placeholder now also mirrors iOS' accessibility semantics: Android exposes the disabled mic affordance as `语音输入暂未接入`, matching `V2MemoCreateTextView`'s accessibility label.
- Text Memo creation body controls now mirror iOS' `V2MemoCreateTextView` details more closely: Android uses a 200dp body input minimum height, floats the disabled 48dp mic affordance outward with a black 12% / 6dp shadow, and renders the video-reference row at 70% opacity with a 14dp muted video icon plus the 11dp trailing lock.
- Text Memo creation type copy now mirrors the current iOS source: Android uses `记一个发现` in the nav title, shows `先保存。没有字数目标，也不会自动分享` above the primary action, uses `保存` for the button label, and keeps the saving state as text-only `保存中…` without an Android-only spinner.
- Text Memo creation now follows iOS' single-save hierarchy: Android removes the duplicate top-right save action, keeps the warm-white close-only nav bar, and pins the destination copy plus primary save action above the keyboard/system navigation area.
- Text Memo creation bottom save bar now mirrors iOS' `bottomBar` styling more closely: Android adds the top 1dp divider, uses the 20dp horizontal / 12dp top / 32dp bottom padding, renders the save button as a solid fairway capsule with 40% disabled opacity, removes the Android-only check icon/gradient/shadow, and uses the iOS 17sp bold button label.
- Text Memo creation page rhythm now mirrors iOS' outer spacing more closely: Android uses 20dp horizontal padding for the top bar and scroll content, 28dp top padding before the form content, and keeps the 16dp vertical section rhythm from `V2MemoCreateTextView`.
- Text Memo creation top bar now mirrors iOS' `topBar` metrics more closely: Android keeps the 20dp horizontal padding, adds the extra 4dp top breathing room over the 12dp vertical rhythm, and uses the 13dp close glyph inside the 32dp circle button.
- Memo home header now mirrors iOS' create CTA sizing more closely: Android gives the `新建 Memo` capsule the same 132dp minimum width and uses the 11dp plus glyph from `V2MemoHeader`.
- Memo home header now mirrors iOS' top safe-area rhythm more closely: Android uses the same 52dp top padding and 12dp bottom padding from `V2MemoHeader`, instead of the tighter generic page spacing.
- Memo home header subtitle now mirrors iOS' structured spacing more closely: Android separates `我的训练记录 ·` and `N条` with a real 4dp row gap instead of baking the gap into the label string.
- Memo home section headers now mirror iOS' icon rhythm more closely: Android uses the 16x14dp leading section icon frame, the 4dp `更多` text/chevron gap, and the smaller 9dp chevron from `V2MemoSectionHeader`.
- Memo home horizontal video/text cards now mirror iOS' subtle card shadow more closely: Android uses the `V2MemoMixedCard` / `V2MemoTextCard` 4dp black 4% shadow treatment instead of the heavier platform-default 2dp shadow.
- Memo home horizontal video/text card chip rows now mirror iOS' fixed 16dp row height from `V2MemoMixedCard` and `V2MemoTextCard`, keeping the tiny category/accent capsules vertically stable.
- Memo home horizontal video/text card titles now mirror iOS' source fields more closely: Android no longer injects Android-only `title` / `无标题` fallbacks, using `memo.text` for text cards and `displayTitle` for mixed cards like SwiftUI.
- Memo home text-card internal padding now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoTextCard`: Android uses 14dp horizontal, 12dp top, and 14dp bottom padding instead of symmetric vertical padding.
- Memo home text-card quote accent now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoTextCard`: Android lets the 2dp gold rule follow the secondary quote text height instead of using a fixed 28dp bar with extra top offset.
- Memo home horizontal video/text rows now mirror iOS' `LazyHStack(spacing: 12)` row height more closely: Android removes the extra 4dp bottom padding from individual preview cards, leaving spacing to the section rhythm like `V2MemoView`.
- Memo home mixed-card play affordance now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoMixedCard`: Android keeps the 28dp translucent white play circle but uses the smaller 11dp play glyph with the same 1dp right optical offset.
- Memo home horizontal video/text cards now mirror iOS' `newBadge` treatment more closely: Android renders the white sparkles glyph inside the 18dp gold new badge for both video+note and text cards instead of showing a plain dot.
- Memo home text-card bottom metadata now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoTextCard` fallback behavior: Android shows the memo's relative `createdAt` label when `metaText` is missing instead of the Android-only fixed `最近` copy.
- Memo home row-empty cards now mirror iOS' `V2MemoSectionEmptyCard` polish more closely: Android uses the fairway 7% / 14dp shadow, the 28dp icon frame with a 26dp muted center glyph, and the same 6dp vertical stack rhythm around the icon, title, subtitle, and CTA.
- Memo home now follows the latest iOS empty-state structure: Android no longer replaces an all-empty Memo tab with a single full-page empty view, and instead always keeps the AI insight card plus the separate `视频笔记` / `随手记` / `挥杆记录` sections so each row exposes its own iOS-style empty card and creation entry.
- Memo video/text secondary lists now match the iOS `pinnedViews: [.sectionHeaders]` behavior: Android's date group headers use Compose sticky headers instead of scrolling away as normal rows, keeping the current time group visible while browsing long Memo lists.
- Memo video/text secondary lists now mirror iOS' stats/filter/list spacing more closely: Android keeps the 12dp gap between the filter chips and the scroll content from `V2VideoMemoListView` / `V2TextMemoListView`, so the first empty state or first date header no longer crowds the chip row.
- Memo text secondary list cards now mirror iOS' `V2TextMemoLargeCard` quote block: Android renders `memo.subText` as a two-line 12sp gray pull quote with a 3dp gold vertical bar between the body copy and bottom metadata.
- Memo all-list now reuses the same iOS-style time grouping rhythm as the video/text lists: filtered results are grouped by `V2MemoListGrouping`, date headers use full-width sticky muted strips, and cards keep the 16dp page inset with the shared `没有更多了` footer instead of a purely flat Android-only list.
- Memo home no longer retains the obsolete Android first-run guide panel path in code: the unused `V2MemoEmptyStateView`/guide-card branch was removed so the implementation cannot drift back from the current iOS `V2MemoView` structure of AI insight plus separate row empty cards.
- Memo home AI insight now unlocks from existing AI profile/goal context even before new Memo samples exist, matching iOS' profile-aware empty-state rule instead of treating every zero-Memo profile as locked.
- Memo home AI insight card now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoAIInsightCard` polish more closely: Android uses the 190dp minimum height, fairway 8% / 16dp card shadow, top-right gold radial glow, and 9dp trailing chevron treatment from the SwiftUI card.
- Memo home footer now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoFooter` metrics more closely: Android uses the 24dp top / 8dp bottom padding, 12dp spacing between the view-all capsule and decorative progress line, 13dp stack icon, and explicit fairway 15% / 6dp capsule shadow.
- AI chat now sends richer context before streaming: user golf profile, account-level golf settings, active-goal progress/cadence, recent Memo titles/tags/text/source metadata, and swing activity recency/growth-pool summary.
- AI service config now matches the iOS provider-picker hierarchy more closely: Android shows providers in a compact three-column card group with provider icon tiles, active `使用中` badge, saved-provider `已验证` badge, 12dp white-backed focused-circle indicator, and the same saved-provider/model restore behavior.
- AI service config provider tiles now mirror iOS' per-provider glyph treatment more closely: Android maps built-in/DashScope/DeepSeek/Moonshot/MiniMax/OpenAI to distinct icons instead of rendering every provider with the same sparkle glyph.
- AI service config provider tiles now also use the same per-provider gradient pairs as iOS `V2AIBridge.providerStyle`, including emerald/fairway for built-in/OpenAI, blue for DashScope, indigo for DeepSeek, purple/indigo for Moonshot, and orange for MiniMax.
- AI service config provider header now matches the latest iOS `V2AIProviderGrid` copy, using `点击查看` because tapping a provider only focuses the card/form until the primary action commits it as active.
- AI service config top chrome now mirrors iOS' sheet presentation more closely: Android replaces the centered navigation title bar with a 32dp floating white circular close button, 14dp gray close icon, 4dp black 6% shadow, and status-bar-aware 16dp top/leading inset.
- AI service config scroll content now mirrors iOS' page padding more closely: Android uses the same 20dp horizontal, 16dp top, and 32dp bottom content insets from `V2AIConfigView` instead of the generic 16dp page padding.
- AI service config hero now mirrors iOS' `V2AIConfigHero` tokens more closely: Android uses the 16dp top padding, 16dp vertical rhythm, 44dp CPU tile glyph, 22sp bold title, and fairway-highlighted `内置模型每天30条免费` subtitle segment.
- AI service config model list now mirrors iOS' `V2AIModelList` metrics more closely: Android uses the 14dp card radius, fairway 8% card border, 16dp card padding, 8dp header-bottom spacing, 44dp model rows, checked/empty-circle 14dp selection icons, 16dp row horizontal padding, and `#D8E2DC` 30% selected background.
- AI service config now uses the same fixed provider endpoints as current iOS and removes Android's extra editable `API Base URL` row and built-in hint card. Provider focus remains local until the primary action commits it; saved provider keys/models are restored without exposing an endpoint editor that has no iOS counterpart.
- AI service config now implements iOS' complete focus-versus-active button state machine: `当前模型`, `测试并应用`, `切换到该模型`, `测试并切换`, `请填写 API Key`, `测试连接`, `切换到挥呗 AI`, `测试中…`, and red `重试` states use the same enabled/tone rules. Unverified or edited external configurations temporarily apply the focused provider, perform a real two-message connection test, keep the tested configuration only on a non-empty response, and restore the full prior provider/model/key/verification snapshot on failure or cancellation; previously verified unchanged providers switch directly.
- AI service config save/test now has explicit progress, success, failure, and rollback behavior: Android disables duplicate actions, preserves coroutine cancellation, and shows release-safe localized failure copy if connection testing or provider/model/API-key persistence throws; `V2AIServiceConfigCopyTest` locks the state machine and hides provider, model, API-key, endpoint, backend, and local-path diagnostics in release while preserving debug troubleshooting detail.
- AI service config API Key editing now mirrors iOS' `V2AIApiKeyField` exactly: Android shows the `输入 API Key` / `安全加密存储` header, mist-green 44dp key field, `sk-...` placeholder, eye visibility toggle, fixed prefix/eight-dot/suffix saved-key mask, provider-scoped `已输入/未输入 API Key` status row, and draft-only key replacement semantics.
- AI service config status feedback now mirrors iOS' success/failure banner treatment: Android uses a check/error icon, 8dp icon-text spacing, 10dp rounded tinted background, and 12sp medium message text instead of a plain text-only banner.
- AI service config status micro-icons now match iOS' `V2AIConfigView` details more closely: Android uses a filled cancel-circle style glyph for failure banners and the same single outlined-circle treatment for the empty API Key state.
- AI service config primary action now mirrors iOS' button chrome and behavior: Android uses the 48dp rounded height, enabled-state 10dp same-tone shadow, trophy-gold edited-config tone, red retry tone, and inline test spinner. The final hierarchy also matches iOS' compact 172dp two-row provider grid, inset `使用中` / `已验证` badges, 15sp bold section labels, 11sp support labels, 12sp API-key status, and 300dp hero-subtitle measure. API 37 verifies the current external-provider form in `ai-service-config-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `c613348cdaf3737b8d15f8a6bb8c5dd00dc60bbd6f708bf326f8b312bc55787c`).
- AI service config bottom actions now mirror iOS' sheet footer more closely: Android adds the centered 13sp medium `关闭` text action under the primary button with 8dp vertical padding and 16dp trailing breathing room.
- AI service config now keeps its provider/model/API-key form above Android keyboard and system navigation, so switching external providers mirrors iOS' safe-area form behavior on smaller devices.
- AI chat now mirrors iOS' logged-out preview state: guests see three image-backed capability tiles and the same three sample questions, can tap or type to create an editable unsaved draft, and see an explicit `问题已放入草稿` boundary. Sending, history, provider, and config remain login-gated, so no account-scoped conversation/profile/quota mutation starts before authentication.
- AI chat context hierarchy now matches iOS more closely: Android no longer renders the Android-only "已接入档案、近期 Memo 与训练记录" ready pill in either logged-out or logged-in states; those account-scoped signals stay inside the hidden prompt/context layer.
- AI chat top bar now matches the iOS visible hierarchy more closely: an empty model label falls back to `实时对话`, the header uses the same warm-white surface and bottom divider, and history/settings actions use the quieter secondary tint.
- AI chat top bar now mirrors iOS' toolbar button treatment more closely: Android uses transparent 24dp hit frames with 16dp close and 18dp history/config glyphs instead of the older 36dp gray circular Material buttons.
- AI chat top/status hairline details now mirror iOS more closely: Android uses the 0.5dp top-bar divider from `V2AIChatTopBar` and the lighter `goldBright` 8% status-bubble background from `V2AIChatStatusBar`.
- AI chat history action and history empty state now mirror iOS' line-weight double-bubble glyph more closely: Android uses the outlined Material `Forum` icon for `对话记录` and the `还没有对话记录` empty state instead of the Android-only history/education glyph or heavier filled bubbles, while keeping the same transparent-button treatment and accessibility label.
- AI chat top-bar icon actions now expose stable semantic labels for return-home, history, and config, making the iOS atlas exits easier to verify in Android accessibility and UI-automation passes.
- AI chat input bar now mirrors iOS' composer boundary: Android adds the same top hairline divider above the warm-white input surface while preserving the existing IME and navigation-bar safe-area handling.
- AI chat input bar now mirrors iOS' composer chrome more closely: Android keeps the input capsule white in the login-locked state, uses the iOS fairway 40% disabled send color, applies the same fairway-tinted 4dp send shadow, and always sizes the send glyph to 16dp. The former Android-only red stop-generation action has been removed from this visible composer state.
- AI chat input now respects Android keyboard and navigation-bar insets, keeping the full-screen chat composer reachable while typing on gesture/three-button navigation devices like the iOS safe-area composer.
- AI chat now preserves the typed prompt until the message is actually accepted into the conversation, and the draft is stored in the chat store so missing external-provider configuration can route to settings and back without losing the user's input.
- AI chat now refreshes the built-in quota display after each completed reply, matching iOS' post-stream quota refresh so the top model label can immediately move from `qwen-plus (N/30)` toward `今日额度已用完`.
- AI chat now has the same iOS-style stream timeout guard: if a reply does not finish within 75 seconds, Android clears the streaming state, preserves any partial answer, and shows a `请求超时` status instead of leaving the user stuck on `正在思考中…`.
- AI chat status banners now match iOS' transient-feedback behavior directly: missing configuration, request failure, and timeout auto-hide after their respective four/four/three-second windows, while `正在思考中…` remains until reply completion, failure, timeout, or cancellation.
- AI chat active status bar now mirrors iOS' `V2AIChatStatusBar` feedback details: Android uses the gold chat-bubble glyph, 70% ink text, and the animated three-dot wave with SwiftUI's 70% base alpha, producing the same effective 21%-70% opacity range instead of a static leading dot or Android's former 30%-100% dots.
- AI chat/archive release failures no longer expose raw provider/backend exception text in assistant failure bubbles or manual archive-sync toasts; Android shows stable localized recovery copy, while debug builds can append low-level detail for internal diagnosis.
- AI chat status release-copy handling is covered by `V2AIChatCopyTest`, locking release builds to localized guidance without provider endpoint, model, API-key, session id, user-path, local-file, or reflection diagnostics while debug builds keep troubleshooting detail.
- AI chat launch-source safety now also guards the mock provider boundary: `ReleaseEndpointAuditTest` allows `V2AIMockProvider` only at its declaration plus the `V2AIChatStore` explicit debuggable helper gate, so Android release chat traffic cannot silently drift back to demo replies while tracking iOS' real AI service path.
- AI archive manual-sync release-copy handling is now covered by `V2AIArchiveCopyTest`, locking release builds to localized retry guidance without provider/backend endpoint, session id, API-key, local database path, or exception diagnostics while debug builds keep troubleshooting detail.
- AI profile reflection now converts parse/apply exceptions into the same handled failure result as provider errors, while malformed or unusable JSON retains current iOS' successful-but-no-change result. Manual archive sync clears its syncing state in a `finally` path for every result and thrown failure; local persistence remains best-effort like iOS' `try? context.save()` path.
- AI automatic profile reflection preserves coroutine cancellation and remains silent like current iOS; leaving/stopping or a best-effort reflection failure cannot replace the active chat status with an Android-only archive banner. The focused archive/history run now covers every manual result, thrown failure, logout cancellation, ordinary automatic failure, and automatic cancellation across 18 API 37 tests. Debug evidence is `ai-archive-sync-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `fb0409a1f645dc2a45b14b40a899abc8118ad985e5a81a4c69b2349b3c278c1f`); release-copy unit coverage separately proves that its diagnostic suffix is omitted from production.
- AI chat send-before-context refresh now preserves coroutine cancellation while still degrading ordinary local profile reload failures, so leaving the chat or cancelling a send cannot turn a disposed context refresh into a completed prompt-prep step; `V2AIChatCopyTest` locks this boundary.
- Golf profile local persist and best-effort cloud upload now preserve coroutine cancellation in both `V2GolfProfileStore` and `V2GolfProfileRepository`, so iOS-aligned archive/profile jobs can still degrade ordinary sync failures without turning disposed work into stale background results; `V2GolfProfileStoreTest` and `V2GolfProfileRepositoryTest` lock this boundary.
- AI chat message rendering now supports the iOS V2 attachment-card model: assistant messages can carry video references, saved-Memo cards, and plaza recommendation cards, with Android Compose cards matching the iOS information hierarchy for title, duration/status, tags, and action affordance.
- AI attachment cards no longer show false navigation affordances when the attachment payload lacks a stable Memo/video/plaza id; Android keeps them as informational recommendation cards until the model carries real destinations.
- AI chat attachments now persist across app restarts: Android stores the iOS-style attachment array as `attachmentsJson` on local AI messages with a Room 4->5 migration, so future video/Memo/plaza cards are not lost when sessions are saved and reloaded.
- AI chat now mirrors iOS' complete exit handoff: leaving the page, switching history, or creating a new conversation checks the original session before changing ids, requires one current-session round for an empty profile or three for an existing profile in default-standard mode, then resets the destination session's counter.
- AI chat profile reflection follows iOS' default-standard cadence: only each fifth successfully completed reply triggers periodic best-effort work; timeout, request failure, and cancellation do not. Results remain intentionally silent because current `V2AIChatView` never enables `showRecordedBanner` or presents reflection progress.
- AI chat provider pill now mirrors iOS' `V2AIChatProviderPill` metrics more closely: Android uses the 8dp fairway status dot, 12x6dp capsule padding, semibold provider label with `Provider: ` spacing, muted model text, and a 9dp down chevron instead of a plain text-only pill.
- AI chat provider pill now also matches iOS' message-scroll placement: Android renders it as the first LazyColumn item with 16dp horizontal / 12dp top / 8dp bottom padding, so it scrolls with the conversation instead of staying pinned below the top bar.
- AI chat message top area now matches iOS' cleaner structure more closely: Android removes the extra logged-in `已接入档案、近期 Memo 与训练记录` UI pill while keeping those profile/Memo/training signals in the hidden AI context prompt.
- AI chat assistant avatar now mirrors iOS' `V2AIAvatar(.idle)` base more closely: Android replaces the plain `AI` text badge with the mist-green-to-fairway circular avatar, fairway shadow, and white robot-face mark used beside assistant bubbles.
- AI history now matches the current iOS conversation model rather than hiding its first local session: a newly opened chat is persisted immediately as `新对话 MM-dd HH:mm`, carries zero messages without Android's former synthetic welcome bubble, and appears as the `当前 / 0条` card. The first real user prompt replaces that title with the same 20-character-plus-ellipsis rule. Current and historical sessions share one updated-time-descending list, switching preserves even a zero-message prior session, and tapping the current card closes the sheet like iOS. API 37 verifies the fresh current-card hierarchy in `ai-history-current-session-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b76972f0a3c5b2666a2e5258c7fdf84666518edeb0d5dceaf356c4a3c2b59250`).
- AI chat's guest shell now follows the current iOS invocation details rather than only matching the preview card: the top bar receives the real guest state and switches away from the signed-in `你的专属高尔夫小高` subtitle, the provider/model pill has no Android-only configuration tap target, and streaming does not replace the paper-plane control with a red stop button. The store still guards duplicate sends while a reply is active. API 37 verifies the resulting first-run hierarchy and accessibility action set in `ai-chat-guest-chrome-ios-parity.png`.
- AI history delete now covers current and historical sessions with a launch-safe rollback path: Android exposes the same two-step long-press context action as iOS instead of its former centered confirmation dialog, removes the target optimistically, restores it if local deletion throws, preserves coroutine cancellation, and shows localized retry copy inside the still-open history sheet instead of hiding the warning behind it. Deleting the current session closes the sheet and creates/persists a clean replacement conversation. API 37 renders the contextual action in `ai-history-context-menu-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `f435f780abbae7a6394f26a31b96374c53346ab162b7b417d4661244f44550f7`), then injects both an ordinary delete failure and coroutine cancellation: the failure restores the exact historical row and renders `删除对话失败，请稍后重试` in `ai-history-delete-recovery-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `513b748bfff9c64dca0a1e192c08d938e10c5bb4498b16a21ad11895148ac433`), while cancellation produces no false banner.
- AI history previews now understand attachment-only assistant messages, so sessions whose latest visible content is a video reference, saved-Memo card, or plaza recommendation still show a meaningful preview line instead of an empty card body.
- AI history cards now also match the iOS V2 card details more closely: current-session previews show the green leading dot, message counts use compact `N条` copy, and date/time are split into the fairway day label plus muted clock text.
- AI history current-session cards now mirror iOS' selected-card treatment more closely: Android uses the `#D8E2DC` 30% current background and fairway 10% / 4dp selected shadow from `V2AISessionCard`, in addition to the existing fairway border and `当前` badge.
- AI history sheet now has the same explicit top-left close affordance as iOS, while keeping the centered title and right-side new-session action.
- AI history no longer manufactures an Android-only welcome message when an empty local session is loaded, so the chat body and its `0条` history count match iOS exactly.
- AI history now preserves the logged-out user's intent: tapping history while logged out signs in first, then returns to the AI tab and opens the history sheet instead of stopping on the main chat surface.
- AI history's defensive no-session state now mirrors iOS' lightweight centered view in the actual medium detent: it shows only the muted 32dp conversation icon and `还没有对话记录`, relying on the header edit-note button instead of an Android-only CTA. Android previously centered that branch against the hidden full-sheet height, placing it outside the visible half sheet; the empty content now follows the active detent while normal signed-in first run continues to show the persisted zero-message current card. The nine-test class verifies both navigation modes in `ai-history-empty-gesture-ios-parity.png` and `ai-history-empty-three-button-ios-parity.png`.
- AI history sheet now reserves Android navigation-bar space for both the empty state and session list, matching iOS' bottom sheet safe-area spacing. The complete eight-test page class passes under both gesture and three-button navigation on API 37, with the last card and recovery banner remaining fully above system controls.
- AI history sheet header now mirrors iOS' top-bar treatment more closely: Android uses the 44dp header row, 17sp centered title, pale 0.5dp bottom divider, and a transparent 24dp button with a custom 16dp `square.and.pencil`-equivalent glyph for new sessions instead of the older green `新建` pill or Material list-and-pencil mismatch.
- AI history sheet drag handle now mirrors iOS' 36x4 black 20% capsule with 8dp top/bottom spacing instead of using the Material default handle.
- AI history sheet now mirrors iOS' medium/large presentation detents more closely: Android gives the bottom sheet a full-height layout with partial expansion enabled, so the empty state and short lists open as a medium-style sheet instead of collapsing to content height while still allowing expansion.
- AI history list now mirrors iOS' quiet card list treatment more closely: Android adds the 16dp top / 24dp bottom scroll padding, uses the exact 6dp title/badge/count rhythm, and moves delete off the always-visible red trailing icon into an iOS-equivalent long-press context menu without an Android-only confirmation dialog. A populated API 37 run verifies current/ordinary card color hierarchy, current-dot preview, two compact message counts, `今天 / 昨天` plus clock splitting, the square-pencil action, and ordinary-session switching in `ai-history-populated-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `6b00cfbedb31ea40eb1ab096c6e805981bea61cee5969dd5e139cfcd7fec60ac`). The same suite verifies that header-new preserves the zero-message prior session and leaves a clean current conversation.
- AI history load failures no longer masquerade as an empty first-run state: Android preserves the current in-memory conversation/list, keeps coroutine cancellation intact, and shows a release-safe localized retry banner that auto-hides after four seconds. Direct API 37 injection verifies both ordinary failure and cancellation; gesture and three-button evidence is recorded in `ai-history-load-failure-gesture-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `8c581d1aa93c5e9dcc99ddc0fa8e6030ba35e472e7fab89963f10b888d6c7f71`) and `ai-history-load-failure-three-button-ios-parity.png` (`133fa7917018ace7efae5aa68e79f0ebfe9c02fcf875118c656746c0db158424`).
- AI training archive now follows the current iOS capability boundary for its lower sections: `训练目标` and `教练指导` are the same semi-transparent dashed `待开放` cards with the exact `AI 训练目标功能待开放 / 教练协作功能待开放` guidance, rather than exposing Android-only live goal summaries or editable coach notes. Existing cross-platform `trainingGoals` and `coachNotes` data remains preserved in the profile model and AI context; it is simply no longer presented as a capability that current iOS does not expose.
- AI training archive empty/sync chrome now mirrors iOS' `V2AIArchiveView` more closely: Android replaces the emoji empty-state marker with a muted document/search icon treatment and uses the iOS 14x8 sync-button padding, 12dp refresh glyph, and 6dp icon/text gap. Its real section-plus route now also opens the iOS-equivalent 50% medium add-item sheet rather than an undersized content-height panel, retains `取消 / 添加`, the 3...6-line input and source chips, and imports the shared RoomBackground artwork inside the sheet. API 37 evidence is `ai-archive-add-sheet-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `e0ca1091e2db0549cff9ac3c07e13577ae10e83ff66d54d578aed03974496a88`).
- AI training archive row/status micro-chrome now matches iOS more closely: Android uses the smaller 14dp CPU-style status-summary glyph, the 11dp last-sync check mark, filled circle-x delete affordances, and white disabled sync-button content while a manual sync is running.
- AI training archive add-item sheet now mirrors iOS' source selector chips more closely: Android renders the 11dp source icon plus 12sp semibold label inside the 12x6dp capsule for manual and coach-advice sources, sharing the same icon mapping as saved archive rows.
- AI training archive section headers now mirror iOS' icon semantics more closely: Android uses a target-style icon for `训练重点` instead of a refresh glyph, and section add affordances use the iOS-like 22dp filled add-circle treatment instead of a heavier plain plus icon.
- AI training archive add-item input now mirrors iOS' plain field constraints more closely: Android uses 15sp ink-green text, a matching 15sp muted placeholder, and the same 3-to-6-line vertical limit inside the mist-green 12dp field.
- AI training archive add-item sheet now mirrors iOS' medium presentation more closely: Android allows the Material bottom sheet to open in a partially expanded state instead of forcing the add form directly into a full-height sheet.
- AI training archive now also has its own logged-out guard, so direct navigation or logout-while-open shows a login card instead of an empty/stale archive.
- AI training archive now respects Android navigation-bar/keyboard insets for both the archive list and manual-add bottom sheet, keeping profile-context editing reachable like iOS' safe-area sheet.
- AI training archive now also matches the iOS modal navigation chrome: the title is centered, `完成` is the only visible dismissal action, and the former Android-only leading back circle is removed while Android system back remains available. The archive-specific empty-state calculation follows iOS by treating a summary-only profile as still needing the empty guidance, while preserving the stricter model-level emptiness rule used by cloud sync. API 37 verifies both lower `待开放` cards, the absence of the visible back affordance, and the shared room background in `ai-archive-coming-soon-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `73a50a8db55e9f26afdc3728027ee183c67ef39b90fe3a1519ccb5117a633494`).
- AI training archive now closes its full account-session boundary rather than only changing the visible card: logout or session expiry immediately dismisses the add-item sheet, clears its draft, cancels any in-flight manual reflection, removes its toast/loading state, and prevents a stale confirm callback from writing profile data. Direct logged-out entry exposes only the login card, and `去登录` explicitly returns to `AI 训练档案` after authentication. API 37 verifies initial logged-out entry plus logout with the editor open; `ai-archive-login-boundary-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `d8d0a7119777c9c176a8d6eb95a1d51b0d8ee9f065d6a3ecff46731a940fdd6b`.
- AI training archive manual sync now distinguishes real message activity from a newly-created blank conversation, so the iOS `上次同步…暂无新对话` hint is no longer hidden by an empty Android session timestamp. Injected API 37 runs prove the exact `已更新 2 项 + 状态总结` success copy, automatic 2.5-second dismissal, thrown-failure localization, and immediate re-enabling of `立即同步`.
- AI training archive add-item state now survives Android saved-state recreation: the open medium sheet, target section, multiline draft, and selected `手动填写 / 教练建议` source are restored before committing the exact item. Keyboard-open actions pass on gesture and three-button navigation; evidence is `ai-archive-add-sheet-keyboard-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `3331d4fb884626ba38449afda3179d2478924d8b041de5ed5bdfd200edbc6d06`) and `ai-archive-add-sheet-keyboard-three-button-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `5c9bba4651cc92e1a158fa6f0a1e0b3a786a63f0489bbf660501b6e8aa2384f1`). The disabled `添加` action now also uses the muted iOS visual state rather than remaining fairway green.
- Swing clip detail now exposes a replay-analysis panel with create-review-Memo, drawing/frame analysis, and runtime technical metadata.
- Swing clip detail top navigation now mirrors iOS' inline navigation bar more closely: Android replaces the circular back/help chrome with a lightweight `chevron + 返回` action, centered `挥杆详情` title, and a 20dp muted help icon on the black bar.
- Swing clip detail now uses Android's existing audio impact detector to find likely strike frames on entry, shows iOS-style impact candidate chips over the video, and lets the user jump directly to the strongest candidate before drawing or creating a review Memo.
- Swing clip detail impact candidate overlay now mirrors iOS' lightweight video badges more closely: Android uses the compact bolt + `击球1/2/3` black 60% pills with 6dp corners, 8x4dp padding, and 10sp gold labels instead of the heavier frame/time badge with a gold border.
- Swing clip detail now mirrors the latest iOS replay scrub-feel affordance: Android exposes `稳 / 细 / 帧` modes above the video like iOS and converts horizontal scrub gestures into frame deltas using the selected points-per-frame density, so slow-motion replay and key-frame inspection no longer rely only on absolute percentage seeking.
- Swing clip detail technical readouts now mirror iOS' monospaced player typography: Android renders time, frame count, speed chips, and frame-step labels with monospaced numerals for steadier replay scanning.
- Swing clip detail frame-step controls now match iOS' two-line control shape more closely: Android renders the six `-10/-3/-1/+1/+3/+10` controls as 44dp icon-plus-label buttons instead of plain numeric text targets.
- Swing clip detail center play/pause control now mirrors iOS' `play.circle.fill` / `pause.circle.fill` treatment: Android uses a 44dp white circular media glyph instead of an Android-only white filled button with a black icon.
- Swing clip detail main playback row now mirrors iOS' `HStack(spacing: 0)` rhythm more closely: Android removes the extra 6dp gaps between the 44dp frame-step buttons and uses the same 44dp play/pause button slot.
- Swing clip detail frame scrubber now follows iOS' control order and height more closely: Android places the draggable frame progress bar after the frame-step and speed/impact rows instead of above the main playback controls, uses the iOS 24dp/pt scrubber band height, and matches the 15% track / 60% progress / shadowed 16dp thumb treatment.
- Swing clip detail frame scrubber now also mirrors iOS' impact-marker affordance: when `击球帧` markers are shown, Android renders 6dp trophy-gold dots at each candidate's timeline position on the scrubber, matching the iOS yellow candidate dots while keeping the overlay chips tappable.
- Swing clip detail speed chips now follow iOS' compact dark-player treatment more closely: Android formats fractional rates as `.1x` / `.25x` / `.5x`, keeps 11sp semibold white text, uses the same 6dp row spacing, and uses a fairway 50% active capsule instead of switching the active chip to a white button.
- Swing clip detail impact-frame chip now matches iOS' dark-player treatment more closely: Android renders the bolt + `击球帧` pill with a 14dp icon, 12sp label, white inactive text, trophy-gold active text, and 15% translucent backgrounds while keeping the Android `跳转` shortcut.
- Swing clip detail impact detection loading state now mirrors iOS' compact controls-row placeholder: Android shows only a yellow spinner centered in a 24dp slot instead of a wider Android-only `检测击球帧` text capsule.
- Swing clip detail bottom action row now mirrors iOS' 14dp icon rhythm more closely: Android applies explicit 14dp spacing between the 36dp action targets instead of relying on per-button padding that made the row feel tighter.
- Swing clip detail bottom bar structure now mirrors iOS' separated `bottomBar` more closely: Android inserts the same white 10% divider above the bottom actions and gives the action row 10dp vertical padding instead of letting it sit directly in the replay controls stack.
- Swing clip detail bottom action hierarchy now mirrors iOS more closely: Android keeps favorite/growth-pool/analysis as 36dp framed actions, uses 16dp framed icons with 40% inactive opacity and 1.5dp active borders, and renders save/info/delete as lighter 32dp plain icons with 17dp glyphs.
- Swing clip detail growth-pool action now mirrors iOS' state icon semantics: Android uses an arrow-up-circle glyph before promotion and switches to a check-circle glyph once the clip is in the growth pool, while keeping the green active treatment and existing confirmation flows.
- Swing clip detail growth-pool confirmation copy now mirrors iOS: Android asks whether to sync the swing video to `成长池（视频备忘录）` and uses the same concise demotion copy that the video remains in favorites.
- Swing clip detail details panel now mirrors iOS' lightweight `LabeledContent` stack more closely: Android removes the extra rounded translucent card, uses 16dp horizontal / 8dp bottom padding, 4dp row spacing, and 12sp white 70% text for both labels and values while retaining Android's extra source/frame-count rows.
- Swing clip detail details panel now also matches iOS' field set and concise values: Android shows only `时间/时长/帧率/大小/状态`, formats duration as `%.1f 秒`, and uses short `普通/收藏/成长池` status labels instead of Android-only retention explanations.
- Swing clip detail drawing entry now mirrors iOS' controls-row placement: Android removes the duplicate top-bar drawing icon and adds the `绘制` chip beside the impact-frame controls with the same 14dp icon, 12sp label, and active green translucent capsule treatment.
- Swing recording now matches more of iOS' launch ergonomics: the camera screen has a direct clip-library button, the top recording pill uses the same concise `录制中` state, and the saved-video toast can jump straight to the swing calendar for review.
- Swing recording top bar controls now mirror iOS' compact camera chrome more closely: Android uses 36dp black 50% circular controls with 16dp glyphs and switches the clip-library entry from a video-library glyph to the iOS-like list-bullet affordance.
- Swing recording primary record button now mirrors iOS' camera-control shape: Android uses an 80dp target with a 76dp white ring, a 60dp red idle circle, and a 32dp red rounded stop square while preserving the existing tap-to-start/stop behavior.
- Swing recording bottom controls now mirror iOS' phone-mode hierarchy more closely: Android removes the extra `点击录制挥杆` / `点击停止` instructional line under the primary camera button, leaving the button shape and state feedback to carry start/stop semantics like iOS.
- Swing recording save toast now mirrors iOS' visual treatment and timing more closely: Android uses the `check-circle + 视频已保存` content only, 20x10dp padding, 20dp radius, black 70% background, 14sp medium text, shows for 2 seconds, and keeps the existing tap-to-open-list behavior without showing an extra `查看` label.
- Swing recording now mirrors iOS' saved-clip thumbnail reveal: after a successful save Android shows the latest 56x72 thumbnail at the lower right for 4 seconds with a white 2dp border, red clip-count badge, close affordance, tap-to-open-list behavior, a gray golf fallback if the thumbnail cannot load, and hides it when a new recording starts.
- Swing recording no longer inserts the Android-only `手机录制模式 · 支持逐帧回看` idle pill. The signed-in camera surface now keeps the same uncluttered lower hierarchy as current iOS while retaining the real local-phone readiness badge.
- Swing recording mirrors iOS' mode switcher placement more closely without overstating Android capabilities: Android shows a top-centered black 55% segmented capsule with a selected white `手机` segment and phone glyph and drops to 45% opacity while recording; the unavailable Watch segment is not presented as functional.
- Swing recording camera startup and pinch feedback now follow current `JustSwingMainView`: the control chrome is withheld until CameraX is actually bound, startup shows only the centered spinner plus `正在启动相机...`, pinch drives the camera's reported zoom range capped at 6x, and the compact `%.1fx` black 58% capsule disappears after 1.1 seconds. Switching lenses resets/hides the indicator. The camera controls reserve the physical Android status-bar height even when the camera privacy layer redraws over an immersive window, preventing time/privacy indicators from covering close or camera actions. API 37 verifies the live camera hierarchy in `swing-camera-live-ios-parity.png` (1440x3120, SHA-256 `d6070fd7ee7ed915b31e80eff63494cbb55b58336e6ac258d3de15a648ac9d5b`); the virtual lens reports no zoom range, so live pinch-to-CameraControl remains a physical-device QA item while zoom math/source and the 2.3x indicator visual are automated locally.
- Swing recording now provides an honest Android counterpart to iOS' lower-left Watch badge: Android shows a lower-left black 50% local-phone badge with a phone glyph, 6dp status dot, and `手机就绪` / `本机录制中` text instead of implying unavailable Watch connectivity.
- Swing recording camera-bind and save release failures now show localized permission/retry guidance instead of exposing CameraX, device id, local-file, or clip diagnostics, while debug builds can still include the low-level cause; the shared throwable helper preserves coroutine cancellation for camera/save failures. `V2SwingCopyTest` locks release hiding, debug preservation, and cancellation behavior for these camera/save paths.
- Swing recording permission recovery now catches failed Android app-settings launches and shows localized retry copy from the camera permission state, keeping the main record path from ending in a dead tap on restricted devices.
- Swing recording runtime permission requests now recover if Android cannot open the permission prompt, showing localized retry guidance from both first entry and the manual retry action.
- Swing recording save feedback now waits for the captured file to be adopted and written to the swing-session store before showing success; thrown file/thumbnail/session-save failures clear the saving state, clean up orphaned media where possible, and use the same tested release-safe retry guidance instead of leaving the UI stuck or falsely reporting a saved clip.
- Swing recording and its clip-library entry now share login-first account scoping with other persisted V2 content: Home "挥杆记录", growth-pool/calendar entry, the in-recording library button, and session-expired record taps route to login and return to the intended recording or calendar destination before any account-scoped clip save/list action runs.
- Swing recording now also matches iOS' logged-out `JustSwingMainView` instead of starting Android camera permission work behind a guest gate. A logged-out or newly expired session renders the full black `挥杆记录预览` page with the exact hero, three capability cards, three-step flow, fixed `登录/注册后开始记录` action, and `继续浏览`; it reuses the byte-identical iOS `v2_quickadd_swing`, `v2_quickadd_video`, and `HomepageAnalysis` artwork already guarded by the asset audit. Camera and microphone permission requests plus camera binding are gated behind live authentication, and the full-screen page hides/restores the status bar like iOS. API 37 verifies both actions and the full rendered hierarchy in `swing-guest-preview-ios-parity.png` (1440x3120, SHA-256 `c5c5ea8f30908a643ee65cd77a86279859c5f9ce147a97759cbdf252fad5c3e3`).
- Swing calendar now mirrors iOS' `JustSwingCalendarView` info affordance: Android shows a top-right `info` action outside selection mode and opens a dark bottom sheet covering local storage, 14-day cleanup, remote/phone recording retention guidance, and gallery-save behavior with the same 36dp colored icon rows.
- Swing calendar selection chrome now mirrors iOS' toolbar hierarchy more closely: Android uses a centered title with left `xmark` / selection-mode `取消`, moves `选择/全选` into the top-right navigation actions beside `info`, and trims the bottom selection bar to `已选 N 项 + 删除` instead of duplicating all-select there.
- Swing calendar empty-day state now mirrors iOS' `ContentUnavailableView` posture more closely: Android removes the extra white card and record CTA from the no-filter empty state, centers a muted golf glyph with `暂无挥杆记录 / 这一天还没有拍摄记录`, and only keeps `查看当天全部` as a filter-clear escape when a retention filter is active.
- Swing calendar grid retention badges now mirror iOS' thumbnail overlay treatment more closely: Android uses white `star` / `arrow-circle-up` glyphs on yellow/green 85% circular badges at the top right instead of colored glyphs on a dark pill.
- Swing calendar grid selection and missing-media states now mirror iOS' thumbnail cards more closely: Android shows an empty circle for unselected selection mode, adds the 3dp fairway border on selected clips, and replaces the dark `文件缺失` fallback with a light gray golf-glyph placeholder.
- Swing calendar expanded month view now mirrors iOS' `displayedMonth` behavior more closely: Android keeps a separate displayed-month state, initializes it from the selected date when expanded, adds left/right chevron month navigation, and disables the forward chevron once the next month would be in the future.
- Swing calendar retention banner now mirrors iOS' row logic more closely: Android no longer shows `普通 ×0`, renders clock/star/arrow icons before each active retention row, uses the same `即将清理 / N天后清理 / 永久保留` value split, and moves the banner to the lighter 10dp rounded white-6% treatment.
- Swing calendar expanded month grid now mirrors iOS' flexible 7-column layout more closely: Android calculates day-cell width from available content width instead of using fixed 44dp cells, preventing weekday/day columns from crowding or overflowing on narrow devices.
- Swing calendar expanded month day cells now mirror iOS typography more closely: Android uses 14sp compact day numbers, bolds only today in the expanded grid, and matches the 36dp blank leading-cell height.
- Memo swing-session cards now mirror iOS' metadata hierarchy more closely: Android adds the small muted location, clock, and stack icons before place, time range, and multi-session rows while keeping the existing date block and overlapped thumbnail stack.
- Memo swing-session thumbnail fallback now mirrors iOS' `V2MemoSwingSessionCard` treatment: Android lays a fairway-to-leaf green gradient with a white play glyph under each 44dp thumbnail so loading or missing preview images no longer show as a plain dark block.
- Memo swing-session date/location text now follows iOS' single-line card constraints more closely: Android caps the 80dp date-block labels to one line and lets long location names ellipsize before the `+N` venue chip instead of crowding it out.
- Swing calendar date chrome now tracks iOS' compact/expanded calendar metrics more closely: the horizontal date strip uses 42x54 cells, 4dp spacing, 10dp corners, 16sp day numbers with 12sp `今天/昨天` labels, and the expanded month grid uses 36dp day cells with 8dp selected/today rounding.
- Swing calendar now follows the current `JustSwingCalendarView` hierarchy end to end rather than mixing the separate filtered clip-grid page into the default archive. Normal entry shows only date header, 90-day strip/month calendar, divider, and the day grid or exact `暂无挥杆记录 / 这一天还没有拍摄记录` state; retention filters remain only for the explicit growth-pool compatibility entry. The strip starts at today's trailing edge on its first frame, while a library containing no clip today automatically selects the newest recorded day; the growth-pool route independently selects the newest promoted day. Clip cards keep only iOS' `HH:mm` and duration overlay, and the retention summary preserves iOS' dark-card result on Android's fixed warm-white page so its white `普通/收藏/成长池` labels and guidance remain readable. Batch deletion uses the exact destructive bottom action sheet, and the information sheet keeps its fixed centered `关于挥杆记录` / trailing `完成` navigation row above separately scrolling content. Five API 37 tests verify the empty archive, information and delete sheets, ordinary latest-day selection, and growth-pool latest-day selection. New populated evidence is `swing-calendar-latest-recorded-day-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `e46e7342c1f799c894e753ab1318e321f1fe4359589517ac92aaf2799ddb9d73`) and `swing-calendar-growth-pool-latest-day-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `b0406099563e5812271ac175601c9b2619f18fe57c0a22738016d61aad50acd2`). Existing empty/info/delete evidence remains current.
- Swing clip detail gallery-save feedback is now release-safe: saving to Android's system album returns localized causes for missing local files, unwritable MediaStore, write failures, and publish failures, and cleans up incomplete gallery entries instead of leaving users with a generic `保存失败`.
- Home "回看分析" now follows iOS' two-source contract: album import or live recording. Existing clips remain reachable through the neighboring `挥杆记录` archive rather than appearing as an Android-only third source inside this sheet.
- Home replay-analysis live recording now covers the iOS navigation atlas `TOOL-001 -> SYSTEM-001 -> MEMO-011` path on Android: choosing `回看分析 / 实时拍摄` opens the phone recording surface and automatically opens the newly saved swing Clip detail with full-screen close chrome after recording stops, while the normal `挥杆记录` entry remains in continuous-recording mode with `视频已保存`. Two real-camera API 37 tests record and persist five-second clips through both routes. Evidence is `home-analysis-live-recording-detail-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `ff44f6293e142559b0910c078c5d751b31302bcd7149859cda6a019293ee22ef`) and `swing-camera-save-stays-recording-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `4d47e21bfbd21e3af8372e978fba17f35ba03558973f8487afea669fdef46164`).
- Home replay-analysis album import catches failed Android media-picker launches, restores the source sheet, and keeps live recording available instead of leaving the analysis entry in a hidden loading state.
- Home replay-analysis retains the persisted swing-material login-first guard and import safety: logged-out analysis taps sign in and reopen the source sheet, live recording routes through an account-scoped destination, and album-import file/session failures clear the importing state, clean up orphaned imported media, and return to the source sheet with retry alternatives.
- Swing clip detail help now uses the standard help icon instead of a text `?`, and the help copy explains the launch-safe key-frame workflow: audio impact candidates when available, plus frame stepping/slow motion as the fallback for muted or noisy clips.
- Swing clip detail help now mirrors iOS' sheet presentation more closely: Android replaces the alert dialog with a medium/large-style bottom sheet, adds the `功能说明` header with a `完成` action, and renders 28dp colored icon rows with 14sp semibold titles and 13sp secondary descriptions.
- Swing clip detail help copy now mirrors iOS' action semantics more closely: Android uses the arrow-up-circle growth-pool cue and explains syncing to `成长池（视频备忘录）`, while the album-save row uses the same download/export affordance as the bottom action.
- Swing clip detail no longer leaves posture detection / Watch linkage as dead disabled rows; both Android-only substitute rows are tappable, use launch-ready `姿态参考` / `穿戴联动` labels instead of `规划中`, and explain the current frame-step/drawing/phone-recording alternative path.
- Swing clip favorite/growth-pool/delete actions now use optimistic store updates like iOS: detail controls, library filters, retention counts, and day cards update immediately while Room file/deletion work completes in the background.
- Swing clip favorite/growth-pool retention updates now roll back if Room persistence throws and surface localized retry feedback in the detail player, preventing optimistic UI from permanently claiming a failed save.
- Swing clip deletion now commits the Room removal before best-effort media-file cleanup and rolls back optimistic UI if persistence throws, avoiding a split state where files are removed before the library record can be deleted.
- Swing calendar/library now also consumes swing-store rollback errors and shows a transient inline warning, so failed multi-delete recovery is visible after clips return to the grid.
- Swing library/detail rollback release-copy handling is now covered by `V2SwingStoreCopyTest`, locking release builds to localized retry guidance without Room operation, clip id, user-path, local-video, or thumbnail diagnostics while debug builds keep troubleshooting detail; the shared throwable helper rethrows coroutine cancellation so disposed Swing jobs do not publish false rollback errors.
- All Goals page background now mirrors iOS' current four-stop gradient exactly: Android uses `#F0F8F5 -> #E8F5EF -> #F5F5F0 -> #FAF8F5`, matching `V2AllGoalsView` instead of the older greener/w warmer wash.
- All Goals list rhythm now mirrors iOS' outer `VStack(spacing: DSSpacing.sm)` more closely: priority headers and goal cards no longer add their own vertical row padding, so header-to-card and card-to-card spacing is controlled by the shared 8dp list spacing like SwiftUI.
- All Goals stats Bento now uses the same card-shadow token family as iOS `V2AllGoalsStatsBento`: Android applies a black 6% / 8dp shadow behind the 78% white card instead of the lighter platform-default 4dp elevation.
- All Goals now exposes the iOS-style list delete affordance on Android: active and completed goal cards can be swiped left, then deleted only after a confirmation dialog.
- All Goals cards now match iOS' card and check-in control treatment more closely: Android renders the 48dp circular action as an icon+label state (`打卡`, `已打`, `完成`, `暂停`) with the active green gradient, plain active checkmark, 12dp glyph, 2dp icon-label gap, and fairway-tinted shadow instead of text-only, and the card surface now uses the same 78% white fill, 90% white stroke, and black 6% / 8dp card shadow family as iOS `V2AllGoalsCard`.
- All Goals card completion text now mirrors iOS' rounded percentage display, so bottom-trailing `完成度 XX%` no longer truncates fractional progress before rendering.
- All Goals cards now also use the latest iOS title-chip semantics: the chip follows `V2AllGoalsCard`'s local `iconType`/title-keyword mapping (`木杆` / `铁杆` when detected, otherwise topic name), and the Memo-count chip includes the same document affordance before `N条Memo` / `暂无Memo`.
- All Goals completed-row chrome now mirrors iOS' lighter finish treatment: Android uses the outlined 14dp check-circle, the gray 15% icon well, 50% green-gray stroke, no extra shadow, 8dp title/count spacing, 1.5dp count-pill vertical padding, and 11dp chevron.
- All Goals priority headers now mirror iOS' micro-depth on the section dot: Android adds the same 10dp colored dot with a black 5% / 1dp shadow before the priority label and divider.
- New/Edit Goal now surfaces linked Memo records like iOS: selected Memos render as horizontal thumbnail cards, can be removed one by one before saving, and are saved in the current Memo-pool order instead of as an opaque count-only entry.
- Goal Memo linking picker now mirrors the iOS V2 picker semantics and visual hierarchy more closely: Android filters out pure text notes, searches title/body/topic, shows thumbnail rows with topic/date metadata, keeps a selected-count footer, and confirms only the currently allowed video/mixed Memo ids.
- Goal Memo linking picker now follows the current `V2NewGoalMemoPickerSheet` render tree and behavior instead of retaining a Material interpretation: it opens at an iOS-large 92% screen-height detent rather than the former Android-only 520dp cap, places `取消` leading and `选择 Memo` centered, uses the 44dp white 85% search row, filters only iOS' display-title/body/raw-topic fields, and keeps pure text Memos out of both the list and returned selection. Rows use the complete 110×68 thumbnail card with its internal title/`MM.dd` footer, separate two-line title plus topic/`yyyy.MM.dd HH:mm`, exact selected surface/stroke/check circle, and no Android-only source/body rows. The bottom `已选 N 条 / 完成` bar is overlaid like SwiftUI's bottom-aligned ZStack, stays visible above keyboard/navigation, and no longer changes the button title to `完成 (N)`. API 37 now also asserts the sheet consumes at least 80% of the device height, then verifies filtering, clear, selection sanitation, confirmation, and the fully visible footer in `goal-memo-picker-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `dbc8fe5e1a59dffb094ebf8b9176b9eb9576324f807bd3d8bf0d1882a895767c`).
- Goal Memo linking picker now keeps its selected-count footer and confirm action above Android keyboard/system navigation, matching iOS' bottom sheet safe-area behavior while searching and selecting records.
- Goal linked-Memo thumbnails now share source labeling across create/edit and detail surfaces, while Goal Detail follows the latest iOS detail-card action: a plus capsule that always reads `选择已有 Memo`.
- Goal focus and Memo-link mutations now use optimistic state updates like iOS: switching the focused goal, linking/unlinking Memo records, picker confirmation, and Memo-form goal selection all refresh Android goal cards immediately before Room persistence catches up.
- Goal creation/editing now matches iOS' save-safety behavior more closely: Android waits for local persistence before closing, disables duplicate submits while saving, ignores top-bar close during save, shows `保存中...`, and keeps the form open with a `保存失败` dialog when persistence fails.
- Goal creation/editing release failures no longer expose raw save/delete exception text; Android keeps the form open with stable localized retry copy, while debug builds can append low-level detail for internal diagnosis. The shared throwable helper preserves coroutine cancellation so leaving the goal form cannot surface a false save/delete error, and `V2GoalCopyTest` locks release hiding of local database, backend endpoint/status, user-path, goal-id diagnostics, and cancellation behavior.
- Goal create/edit top navigation now mirrors iOS' `V2NewGoalNavBar` treatment more closely: Android uses the 36dp white translucent close circle with a subtle white stroke/shadow, 18sp centered title, and a white-text fairway -> exact `#40916B` gradient `保存` capsule with the same 6dp fairway 32% shadow and disabled opacity instead of a plain green text action on a heavier top surface.
- Goal create/edit main form card now matches iOS' `formCard` surface more closely: Android uses the same 24dp rounded card, 75% white stroke, 88% white fill, and black 8% / 14dp card shadow instead of the heavier 92% white surface and platform-default elevation.
- Goal creation/editing basic fields now mirror iOS' `V2NewGoalSectionLabel`, `V2NewGoalNameField`, and `V2NewGoalClubChipsField` more closely: Android uses the same 11sp gray tracked section labels, `描述你想练习的内容…` placeholder, 60% white name field with 8% black stroke, and wrapped club/type chips with selected fairway gradient/shadow instead of a single horizontal scroller.
- Goal creation/editing field chrome now also mirrors iOS' neutral input hierarchy and selected control surfaces: Android renders entered goal names and the large target number in deep ink instead of green, and selected completion-method cards, cadence cards, chips, and stepper buttons now share the same fairway -> exact `#40916B` gradient family plus fairway 32% button shadow instead of mixing approximate greens.
- Goal creation/editing form structure now matches iOS' V2NewGoalView/V2EditGoalView more closely: Android removed the extra `目标总次数` field and derives `targetSessions` from the cadence choice (`按次数` value, `按时间段` months × 4, or nil for permanent goals), with the edit CTA helper copy matching iOS' "已完成的打卡记录不会变化" reassurance.
- Goal create/edit validation and legacy continuous-goal handling now match both iOS view models: non-continuous goals require a finite positive target, permanent goals may submit without one, existing numeric targets enter the integer UI at a minimum of 1, and saving a continuous goal always rebuilds it as a free metric with target `0`, no unit, and no `targetSessions`. Android removed its former free-goal-only explanatory row so edit mode retains the same numeric target control rendered by iOS. API 37 evidence is `edit-goal-continuous-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `89137a1444991029a7b1f1f38da5cd4aefcc669beb3ac4768d956fe4f5c8e24f`).
- Goal target quantity controls now match iOS' upgraded stepper more closely: Android shows the same preset chips for times, balls, duration, and distance-backed records, uses the iOS value ranges (`次数 1...200`, `球数 1...500`, `时长 1...600`, `距离 1...1000`), mirrors the 44pt fairway-gradient +/- buttons with a large 36sp center number plus smaller muted unit, and now follows iOS' short-press / long-press acceleration model (`±1`, then repeated `±1`, then `±5` after a longer hold). Users can also tap the number to open an iOS-style bottom value picker with single-column numeric selection or dual hour/minute duration selection positioned near the current value. The picker has the iOS keyboard fallback: users can switch from the scroll list to direct numeric entry, with the typed value clamped into the same range on confirm.
- The Goal value picker now follows the current `V2GoalWheelPickerSheet` instead of presenting ordinary selectable rows: Android uses a center-snapping 180dp wheel with faded neighboring values and a fixed selection lane, keeps the unit outside the numeric wheel, provides separate hour/minute wheels for duration, and preserves the draft until `确定`. Its 360pt-style warm sheet now has the leading/header `取消`, centered title, 18dp keyboard/grid icon toggle, exact en-dash range copy, 48dp gray/green-gradient bottom actions, keyboard/navigation-safe layout, and the same clamp semantics as iOS. API 37 verified wheel scrolling writes the centered value and direct `999` input clamps to `600`; evidence is `goal-wheel-picker-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `8e51a3133043c63902356b92a5da8a68cdce06c5944c02b4c2c3b0fd0249642d`).
- Goal metric selection now matches iOS' card hierarchy more closely: Android renders `次数 / 球数 / 时长` as three equal rounded option cards with selected icon circles and green emphasis instead of plain pill chips, while keeping the same count/balls/duration model mapping.
- Edit Goal metric selection now mirrors iOS' read-only card treatment: Android reuses the same `次数 / 球数 / 时长` card group at 55% opacity with taps disabled instead of collapsing the locked completion method into a one-line summary.
- Goal cadence selection now mirrors iOS' stacked option-card hierarchy: Android renders `按次数`, `按时间段`, and `永久练习` as rounded radio cards, keeps the per-count stepper inside the selected card, supports the same 1...30 count picker by tapping the count value, uses the iOS fairway-gradient 36dp +/- buttons with long-press acceleration, and shows the iOS-style `1个月 / 3个月 / 自定义` range chips on the time-range row.
- Goal cadence selection now also mirrors iOS' selected-row finish more closely: selected frequency rows use the same translucent fairway-to-leaf gradient, the inline count number uses deep ink, and selected radio dots show a plain white checkmark instead of the heavier check-circle glyph.
- Edit Goal now mirrors iOS' cadence lock behavior more closely: Android reuses the same cadence field with the category locked, dims the non-selected frequency rows, and still lets users adjust the current category's count/month subvalue instead of replacing the whole section with a read-only summary.
- Goal priority selection now mirrors iOS' four-card hierarchy: Android renders `最高 / 高 / 中 / 低` as equal emoji cards with priority-specific selected borders/backgrounds, the same unselected-title tracking, and the `同优先级目标按创建时间排序` helper copy.
- Goal Memo linking now sits in the same form-card hierarchy as iOS: Android moved `关联 Memo 记录` back into the main create/edit form as the final section, uses the shared gray tracked section label plus the iOS-style 11dp plus icon / 12sp text add capsule, and retains real linked-Memo thumbnails with one-by-one removal.
- New/Edit Goal linked-Memo thumbnails now match iOS' `V2NewGoalMemoThumbView` density more closely: Android uses the 110dp card with 68dp thumbnail, 12dp horizontal row spacing, 60% white surface/55% white stroke, placeholder kind icon, remove chip, and the same title + `MM.dd` two-line footer instead of adding an extra source-label row.
- Edit Goal deletion now matches iOS' safety flow and destructive-button treatment: the 48dp red-outline action uses a trash icon, 14dp radius, 8% red fill, and 30% red stroke before opening a confirmation dialog that deletes the goal, its check-in progress, and goal-level Memo links.
- Edit Goal deletion now also matches iOS' context-aware dismissal: deleting from a detail page skips the now-deleted Goal Detail screen and returns to the previous source, such as All Goals, instead of always resetting Android back to Home.
- Goal deletion now also uses an optimistic tombstone like iOS: All Goals/Home lists remove the deleted goal immediately and old Room snapshots cannot briefly reinsert it while the local/cloud delete finishes.
- Goal deletion now also has a save-safe destructive path for edit and All Goals list entries: Android waits for local persistence before dismissing, shows `删除中...`, blocks duplicate delete/cancel/back while in flight, and keeps users in context with localized `删除失败` copy if deletion throws.
- Goal Detail page background and scroll inset now mirror iOS' `V2GoalDetailView` shell: Android uses the vertical `#E8F5E9 -> #F1F8F4 -> #F5F5F0 -> #EDE8E0` gradient, shared room artwork, no extra scroll-top inset, the same 20dp horizontal content padding, and the same 140dp terminal spacer for the card stack instead of the older shorter wash and generic 16dp list inset.
- Goal Detail bottom actions now respect iOS' completed/archived safety behavior and floating bar treatment: pause/resume is only available for active/paused goals, completed goals can no longer be accidentally restored to active, the bottom bar includes status-specific guidance text on the same transparent-to-pale-green gradient background, and the secondary/primary actions share equal width like `V2GoalDetailBottomBar`.
- Goal Detail completed-state bottom chrome now mirrors iOS' exact finish treatment more closely: Android shows the leading `🎉` in `恭喜！目标已完成` and dims the disabled pause/resume-side action to 50% opacity for completed/archived goals.
- Goal Detail's primary bottom action now uses iOS' always-visible/enabled `继续练习` language and `arrow.right.circle.fill`-style affordance while still calling the existing guarded check-in flow, instead of presenting status-specific Android-only labels; Android also matches the overlaid floating placement, 32dp bottom inset, 52dp button height, 16dp radius, white 88% secondary button, black 12% secondary stroke, and 11dp fairway shadow on the primary CTA.
- Goal pause/resume now uses the same optimistic state update path as check-in, so Android detail/list UI reflects pause and restore immediately like iOS instead of waiting for the Room observer round-trip.
- Goal Detail linked Memo card now matches the iOS interaction model and `V2GoalDetailMemoCard` surface: linked records render as horizontal `V2NewGoalMemoThumbView`-style 110x68 thumbnail cards with 12dp row spacing, title + `MM.dd`, placeholder kind icons, and one-by-one unlink controls, the header action uses the same plus icon + `选择已有 Memo` capsule, the picker remains available for bulk changes, and the card uses the same black 8% / 28dp shadow as the iOS detail card stack. Its empty state now uses iOS' 22pt outlined `tray` instead of Android's former filled edit-note substitute; API 37 captures the complete lower section in `goal-detail-memo-empty-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a5bb08d1502f9c84867ebc7500d95f1857c9e67b23b8f43921c91d57ad16d5a0`).
- Goal Detail navigation now mirrors iOS' context header: the top bar shows the current goal title, status dot/text, and clear edit icon on the page gradient, using 36dp translucent white circular back/edit buttons with 14dp gray glyphs instead of a full-width white app bar.
- Goal Detail progress now matches iOS' information hierarchy more closely: Android places the circular progress card before the information table, uses current/total, rounded percent or long-running status, remaining-session chip, three stat columns for completed, weekly, and streak counts, the lighter plain-check / 12dp stat glyph treatment, the exact 20dp internal horizontal inset, and the same 88% white card surface with black 8% / 28dp shadow.
- Goal Detail information card now mirrors iOS' six-row table and surface treatment: Android replaced the older dark-green summary card with a warm white 88% `目标信息` card covering club type, metric kind, target quantity, cadence, priority, and deadline using matching chips/dividers plus the black 8% / 28dp shadow from `V2GoalDetailInfoCard`.
- Goal Detail information card now also matches iOS' small-glyph density and metric semantics: the header calendar icon is 14dp, the completion-method glyph is 11dp, and count/free goals use number/infinity-style glyphs instead of Android-only single-number/trophy symbols.
- Goal Detail practice timeline now mirrors iOS' recent-record card: Android shows the latest check-ins as "完成第 N 次" rows with today/yesterday/date metadata, plain-check completion badges, total count, a cleaner empty state, and the same 88% white card surface with black 8% / 28dp shadow.
- Goal Detail practice timeline now also mirrors iOS' left-side connector treatment: Android places the 8dp colored dot at the same 14dp top offset, draws the vertical line between recent/check-in rows, and leaves the final row as a terminal dot.
- Goal Detail practice timeline keeps iOS' always-visible `查看全部 →` header affordance even when there are no check-ins and now follows the current Swift source exactly: the label remains visually present but inert while iOS' callback is still a TODO, so Android no longer opens an extra platform-only full-history sheet.
- Goal Detail's missing-record route now mirrors iOS instead of using the generic Android error scaffold: it shows the room background, question-folder cue, exact `该目标不存在或已被删除` copy, and fairway capsule `返回` action. API 37 verifies both populated and missing-detail branches; `goal-detail-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `e6cc6afde30714d6baa3d9655aeb05e44a0d18d122a46986ef146aab85d280d0`.
- Goal Detail now has live API 37 evidence for the previously manual state matrix. The timeline was exercised with 0, 1, 3, and 4 check-ins; the 4-check-in page keeps only `完成第 4 / 3 / 2 次`, preserves today/yesterday metadata, and leaves the visible `查看全部 →` affordance inert like the current Swift TODO. Pause and resume update the header and floating guidance immediately, while the always-enabled `继续练习` CTA is proven to remain guarded for paused, completed, archived, and same-day duplicate states. Completed/archived pause controls now also expose a true disabled accessibility semantic, matching SwiftUI's disabled `Button` rather than only looking gray. `goal-detail-timeline-four-plus-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `676d217b5387d52360ca3c05bc5e96f9ba46d00f29044fb8c2e1d0ab4474a870`; `goal-detail-status-contract-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `36c272f8fc5edeb33daecc298b099f846ad69cbeb858f523e400eec8d3b2c1fe`.
- All Goals' zero-goal hierarchy now matches the current iOS render tree exactly: Android removed its extra `还没有训练目标` guidance card, leaving the four-column zeroed stats Bento, open room background, top create action, and floating `新建目标` CTA. Both create routes and the back action now expose explicit accessibility labels and remain functional. API 37 verifies the complete empty page in `all-goals-empty-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `be0dab685ce088b643d3923d6954e5b10df1f686b5407f97ff71817bbcca8009`).
- All Goals navigation chrome now mirrors iOS' `V2AllGoalsNavBar` / `V2AllGoalsCreateFAB` scale more closely: Android adds the 0.5dp bottom divider under the 92% white top bar, uses 14dp glyphs inside the 40dp circular nav actions, and reduces the bottom FAB plus icon/shadow to the iOS-style 14dp / 10dp treatment with the same fairway 38% glow.
- New Goal navigation now mirrors iOS' neutral chrome colors more closely: Android uses the same mid-gray close glyph and deep ink title color instead of tinting both with the greener DS ink token.
- New Goal's AI suggestion banner now mirrors the latest iOS stub: Android shows the same sparkle + semibold `AI 教练为你推荐了目标` + regular `· 查看推荐 →` text hierarchy, uses the fairway 10% -> exact `#40916B` green 16% gradient, 14dp `Radius.md`, 22% stroke, and no extra shadow, opens the `AI 推荐目标功能尚未接入。` explanation when tapped, and exposes the same small close action to dismiss the banner for the current draft.
- All Goals' populated state now has current API 37 evidence in addition to the zero-state proof. The page verifies the same critical/high/normal grouping (including low goals in the normal section), card navigation, real check-in transition, Memo counts, completed-row expand/collapse, and completed-card hierarchy. A render review also found and fixed Android's translucent-card stacking bug: the full-width Material dismiss background had tinted every idle goal card red.
