Android now uses the iOS `V2SwipeToDelete` contract instead—an idle card has no red layer beneath it, a left drag settles at the clipped 76dp action, tapping the revealed action opens the existing second confirmation, and tapping the card while open closes the drawer. Evidence is `all-goals-populated-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `92ba79dc6adfd9bbd2def739fe2b577efaebdb75207f964c5b65999a06e83a7d`) and `all-goals-swipe-delete-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `254ce3d6ae1d62da37a58123182e8f10cd27ad246b629586b446480222b72a86`).
- New/Edit Goal form content now uses the same 20dp horizontal page inset as iOS' `V2NewGoalView` for the AI banner, form card, save CTA, and edit/delete actions instead of the generic 16dp Android section padding.
- New/Edit Goal's page shell now closes the remaining current-source differences: Android uses iOS' exact four gradient stops, 4dp top / 60dp bottom scroll inset, 20dp section rhythm, and -0.45sp navigation-title tracking; the form card no longer adds an Android-only 4dp internal gutter. The AI explanation closes with the exact `好的` action, and edit deletion uses iOS' exact `关联的打卡记录也会一并清除` warning. API 37 verifies the initial new-goal page and interactive AI stub in `new-goal-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5cfa9f7e1578b106ce17524ff13a69f0bb819ec6328c4008f8339bf0cc8eed0a`).
- New/Edit Goal bottom save CTA now mirrors iOS' `bottomCTA` state treatment more closely: Android uses the full fairway-to-#40916B gradient with whole-button 40% disabled opacity, a fairway 40% / 11dp enabled shadow, 8dp icon/text spacing, and the 14dp check-circle glyph at 80% opacity.
- Edit Goal's bottom action stack now follows SwiftUI's actual `VStack(spacing: 12)` plus the delete button's 4dp top inset. Android no longer inserts the system navigation inset between `保存修改` and `删除目标`; their rendered gap is locked to 16dp, while the navigation inset is applied after the complete scroll content so the destructive action remains at least 48dp clear of the gesture area. All five New/Edit Goal API 37 scenarios pass, and the bottom-state evidence is `edit-goal-bottom-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `c988a66eb31e469d7d1da67188b3594b6f9a52baf645e7c2001166a4c59acc00`).
- Bottom tabs now mirror iOS' `person` Me-tab glyph weight more closely: Android uses the outlined Material person icon for both selected and unselected states, so the tab changes tint only like `V2BottomTabBar`.
- Profile now matches the iOS credit-calibration utility and profile QR flows closely enough for V2 launch use.
- Profile settings notification row now mirrors the latest iOS boundary again: Android keeps the static-looking `通知设置 / 敬请期待` treatment instead of opening OS settings, uses iOS' 60% white surface with no shadow or chevron, and still opens the in-app notification/privacy page through the whole row like the SwiftUI `NavigationLink`.
- Profile settings about card now mirrors iOS' quiet about treatment: Android removes the extra info glyph, keeps the three centered text rows only, and uses the same low-strength fairway 4% / 6dp shadow.
- Profile settings cards now mirror iOS' per-row geometry rather than sharing one Android treatment: role/status/favorites rows use the 68dp `settingsRow` height, QR/notification remain 64dp, role switch and QR keep the fairway 8% soft shadow, coach status and learning favorites remain shadowless, learning uses the 17dp glyph, and a blocked role switch retains its chevron while the whole row fades to 62%.
- Profile account-security now mirrors current iOS `ProfileSettingsPage` navigation, row semantics, and section hierarchy: Android uses the leading `‹ 返回` text action rather than the shared circular back control, keeps the centered `设置` title, uses a cropped-account header glyph, true envelope, the exact lock-shield privacy treatment, filled delete glyph, and the fairway gear affordance on the editable nickname row. The lock-shield renderer is shared with both Profile support lists but scales to this page's 14dp glyph inside its 32dp mist tile, so the SF Symbol meaning remains consistent without copying another approximation. The account/about/logout/delete stack uses iOS' 12dp internal rhythm rather than four unrelated 20dp page sections, and the dormant iOS developer-tools block is no longer exposed to Android test accounts. API 37 verifies the complete profile fields, masked phone, password status, legal/deletion routes, developer-row absence, and full page in `account-security-lock-shield-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `ff220e288abb52f527e326a7bcd44637c5e01fae9a3baf7f69bd055a33df6b50`).
- Profile header now mirrors the latest iOS first-screen chrome: Android hides the extra right-side settings circle, leaving the `我的` title plus `My 高尔夫之路` subtitle only, and uses the iOS 11pt-equivalent subtitle size.
- Profile main scroll content now mirrors iOS' page insets more closely: Android keeps the 16dp horizontal and 8dp top padding, and replaces the ad hoc bottom spacer with the same `DSSpacing.xxl + DSSpacing.lg` 48dp bottom content inset used by `V2ProfileView`.
- The signed-in Profile main page now includes the previously missing iOS `个人资料` card immediately after the user card, with formatted birthday, region fallback, bio placeholder, and the shared edit route. It also supports pull-to-refresh, displays the exact `手机号已绑定/未绑定 · 密码已设置/未设置` account-security summary, gives the role card its gold outline, blocks logout with `内容发送中，暂不可退出` while reliable delivery is active, and reuses the same branded iOS logout confirmation already used by account settings. Its active V2 more-list follows the current Swift render tree for internal accounts too: `更多功能 → 账号与安全 → 切换身份 → 退出登录`, without injecting Android's legacy `开发者工具 / 云端一致性` card. All four nontrivial SF Symbol meanings are now explicit rather than nearest generic Material glyphs: `internaldrive.fill`, `arrow.counterclockwise.circle.fill`, the filled double bubble, and the lock shield render as corresponding drive, inverted filled refresh, double-bubble, and lock-shield treatments. API 37 verifies the full scroll hierarchy and interactions in `profile-signed-in-top-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `868cffa56f4471c93a4bf3ff8bc0cfff585017c50c7b88399208bb6e5d6fe917`), `profile-signed-in-actions-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `4059811d360dc46c81d13ccd1320c1934af3662e5f75eeb8fe6b7b811f89e4ff`), `profile-signed-in-logout-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `707d5da4d05dd9729ba3b34f98cd65358149ab938bd4faea1e3355f6ce8fe30b`), and `profile-more-list-sf-symbol-parity.png` (SHA-256 `fb3c1b3efdd94cf700d21d023345abdbfd40953335c0e49d6c3c5a8d659247a2`).
- Profile Settings now has direct ordinary-student evidence in addition to the existing coach-role variants. The production page preserves iOS' QR, muted notification, learning-favorites, and version/about hierarchy over the shared RoomBackground; notification/privacy and QR destinations now survive Android saved-state recreation instead of falling back to the Settings root after rotation or process recreation. Six API 37 role/settings tests pass, including persisted notification preference state. Evidence is `profile-settings-student-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `cea8523ff8cd7d4a798381bed0b42e67cd8a403fc214faf76a5c362e67e31191`).
- Profile user card now matches iOS' premium card polish more closely: Android uses the same fairway-tinted soft shadow treatment, gives the gold 85dp avatar membership ring the iOS `#E0A82E` 30% / 4dp shadow, offsets the 24dp verified badge by 2dp with its own subtle green shadow, uses a 13dp edit pencil, and uses 10dp trophy/star glyphs in the membership and credits chips to stay closer to iOS' crown/star membership treatment.
- Profile user card membership chip now mirrors iOS' crown-style membership cue more closely: Android uses the 10dp `WorkspacePremium` badge glyph instead of the trophy/achievement glyph while keeping the gold gradient capsule.
- Profile user card verified badge now matches iOS' inner glyph scale more closely: Android keeps the 24dp fairway badge and 2dp warm-white stroke, but reduces the checkmark to the iOS 10pt-equivalent size instead of the heavier 12dp mark.
- Profile user card fallback avatar now mirrors iOS' `person.crop.circle.fill` treatment more closely: Android uses a circular account glyph instead of a plain person glyph and applies the same muted shadow-gray 40% tint inside the mist-green avatar fill.
- Profile user card username row now matches iOS' conditional layout: Android only renders the `@username` line when the display value is non-blank, avoiding an empty 13sp row for incomplete account profiles.
- Profile stats card now mirrors iOS' card polish more closely: Android uses the same fairway-tinted 16dp shadow, 10dp bottom-right chevrons, explicit 1dp grid dividers, the 4dp number/label vertical rhythm, and shrinks long statistic values down to the iOS 0.6 minimum scale before ellipsizing.
- Profile More list now matches iOS' row density and card polish more closely: Android uses 40dp icon tiles with 12dp corners, 16dp glyphs, 13dp chevrons, bold 15sp titles, 12sp subtitles with the same 2dp title/subtitle gap, 64dp account/logout cards, the same pale-green dividers starting after the icon column, fairway-tinted soft shadows, and the iOS 16pt logout label size.
- Profile More list row glyphs now match iOS semantics more closely: Android uses a storage-drive glyph for `存储空间` instead of the SD-card symbol, and a filled double-chat glyph for `意见反馈` to mirror `bubble.left.and.bubble.right.fill`.
- Profile golf-record card now mirrors iOS' V2 card details more closely: Android adds the `⛳` heading marker, uses the clipped 16dp white card with fairway-tinted soft shadow, renders the right-side `编辑` as a plain text link instead of a padded Material button, matches the 10sp/18sp chip typography, shrinks long chip values down to the iOS 0.7 minimum scale before ellipsizing, uses the same `DSSpacing.sm + 2` spacing inside the 2x2 chip matrix, and displays dominant hand as `右手 ✋` / `左手 🤚`.
- Profile AI archive card now matches iOS' decorative treatment more closely: Android adds the `🧠` title marker, uses the 124dp minimum green-gradient card height, clips the whole card to the 20dp shape, keeps subtitle text to two lines at iOS' 80% white, renders the low-opacity 80sp brain decoration on the right, uses the iOS 13pt-equivalent chevron inside the 32dp circle, and matches the 0.0/0.6/1.0 green gradient stops.
- Android now has a V2 credit purchase path instead of a calibration-only placeholder: Google Play Billing loads the same three credit tiers as iOS, shows Play-provided localized prices, launches one-time purchases, restores unconsumed credit purchases after reconnect/re-entry, grants credits through `/credits/add-iap` with purchase token/package/quantity metadata, consumes completed purchases, and refreshes the profile balance.
- Credit purchase now has release-safe unavailable states with local routing and rendered API 37 coverage: if Google Play products have not loaded, only partially loaded, or the purchase Activity is missing, the page explains the problem and offers a combined refresh/restore action instead of silently rendering disabled purchase buttons. Missing or partial Play Console configuration stays in the inline gold recovery card instead of also producing a false green success notice or an immediate blocking failure dialog; actual Billing query failures still use localized error handling. Product loading and pending-order restoration are serialized so a late catalog callback cannot erase a real restore/grant notice. `V2CreditsPurchaseAvailabilityTest` locks the loading, missing-Activity, no-products, partial-products, and all-products-ready routing branches.
- Credit purchase now also has safer client recovery around ambiguous Google Play callbacks: an OK result with no purchase exits the "buying" state and points users to refresh/restore, user cancellation silently clears the overlay like current iOS, pending and unspecified purchase states cannot strand `处理中...`, grant failures include an order id or purchase-token suffix for support, consume-confirm failures no longer leave the UI stuck in a purchasing state, and a locally remembered granted purchase token lets restore retry consume confirmation without calling the grant endpoint again. Only confirmed grant/consume completion uses the green success notice and `返回继续`; ambiguous, pending, and restore guidance uses a gold warning notice.
- Credit purchase no longer exposes raw Google Play Billing `debugMessage` or server-grant exception text to release users; Android maps connection, product-load, restore, purchase-launch, purchase-failure, grant-failure, and consume-confirm failures to stable Chinese recovery copy, while debug builds can still append low-level details for internal diagnosis.
- Credit purchase BillingClient teardown now routes through a tested close-failure isolation helper, so leaving the purchase surface cannot surface release-user diagnostics or obscure the main purchase/restore state machine; external Play purchase evidence is still required.
- Credit purchase requests now attach a SHA-256 obfuscated account id to Google Play Billing flow params, so purchase records can be correlated to the current logged-in user without exposing the raw Tencent Cloud user id.
- Credit purchase now reacts to login-state changes while the page is open: after a logged-out user signs in and returns, Android automatically reloads Google Play products and restores pending purchases instead of leaving the充值 list empty until manual refresh.
- Credit purchase success now gives a direct `返回继续` action in the success notice, so users coming from low-credit download can return to the download task without hunting for the top-left back action.
- Credit purchase scroll content now respects Android navigation-bar insets, matching iOS' safe-area breathing room so the bottom notes and recovery actions are not cramped by system navigation.
- Credit reset/calibration scroll content now also respects Android navigation-bar insets, keeping the reset/cache-clear recovery actions away from system navigation.
- Credit reset/calibration cards now mirror iOS' V2 shadow hierarchy more closely: Android applies fairway-tinted soft shadows to the hero, balance, action, and audit cards using the same relative 12/10/8/8dp elevation rhythm and keeps purchase as an independent low-credit-download route rather than an extra action on this page.
- Credit reset/calibration refresh now clears its loading state through a `finally` path and maps thrown balance-refresh/cache-reset failures to stable release-safe recovery copy, so low-credit download recovery cannot leave the calibration buttons permanently busy.
- Credit reset/calibration release-copy handling is now covered by `V2CreditsResetCopyTest`, locking release builds to localized retry guidance without balance endpoint, token, user-path, cache-file, or exception diagnostics while debug builds keep troubleshooting detail.
- Download low-credit states now point users to credit purchase first and keep balance calibration as the recovery path for stale local displays.
- Download action-row low-credit copy now also follows the purchase-first path, so the disabled primary action and credit card no longer send mixed "refresh/calibrate first" guidance.
- Profile stats card taps now route to existing Android V2 surfaces: goals, video Memo list, and swing calendar, with login gating when needed.
- Profile stats card count formatting now matches iOS' V2 compact number style: 10,000+ values render as `万` instead of Android-only `w`, with a stable decimal separator across device locales.
- Video and text Memo secondary-list empty states retain their matching starter guidance and clear-filter recovery. The current all-Memo page instead follows iOS' quiet `暂无 Memo / 回主页用「+ 新建 Memo」记录一次练习` empty state without page-local creation controls.
- Video Memo and text Memo secondary lists retain the latest iOS disabled search placeholder; all-Memo now has no trailing toolbar action, matching its SwiftUI source.
- Goal, Memo, and persisted swing-recording entry points now share a root-level login-first guard: Home quick add, Memo quick add, All Goals create, Memo list create buttons, Home swing recording, and swing calendar/growth-pool entries route logged-out users to login before opening account-scoped creation or library pages.
- Quick Add sheets now reserve Android navigation-bar space under the cancel action, matching iOS' safe-area sheet spacing for Home and Memo creation entry points.
- Quick Add sheets now mirror iOS' `V2MemoCreateSheet` chrome more closely: Android suppresses the extra Material bottom-sheet drag handle so only the custom 40x4 grabber is shown, removes Android-only per-card shadows from the image cards, and uses the 12dp trailing chevron size from the SwiftUI card.
- Video Memo creation no longer auto-links a goal for ordinary camera/album/online selection, matching the current iOS form whose goal helper is not rendered. Existing links loaded in edit mode remain untouched and are written back unchanged.
- Video Memo creation now also mirrors iOS' downloaded-video form prefill for online sources: Android fills a safe fallback title plus duration/source notes only when the user has not typed content, and expands the note field so the imported context is visible before save.
- Video Memo creation online-source prefill now mirrors iOS' platform display names: Android uses `抖音` / `B站` / `小红书` / `YouTube` / `在线平台` in fallback titles and `来源：...` note lines instead of leaking Android-only lowercase provider ids.
- Video Memo and text Memo secondary-list top bars now mirror iOS' custom nav hierarchy more closely: Android uses a Memo-specific 44dp action row with an iOS-style back chevron, keeps only the disabled 44dp search placeholder on the right with a 17dp icon, adds the 4dp title gap, and renders the page title as a 24sp fairway heading beneath the action row.
- Video Memo and text Memo secondary-list stats bars now match iOS' `V2MemoStatsBar` treatment: Android uses the 10dp rounded muted capsule, 1dp list-border stroke, 16x10dp inner padding, regular 12sp fairway text, and the same single-line truncation behavior.
- Video Memo and text Memo secondary-list stats/filter rhythm now matches iOS' `statsAndFilter` stack more closely: Android removes extra vertical padding from the stats capsule and filter row, and keeps the 12dp gap between them at the page level.
- Video Memo and text Memo secondary-list empty states now mirror iOS' lightweight centered treatment more closely: Android removes the extra white card shell and large starter CTA from true-empty states, uses the iOS `暂无...` / `回主页用「+ 新建 Memo」...` copy with the same 8dp stack spacing, and switches filtered empty states to the iOS `当前筛选下没有...` plus 32dp `清除筛选` capsule.
- Memo secondary-list empty-state symbols now match the current iOS semantics instead of relying on Android filled approximations: text true-empty uses an outlined double-bubble, video true-empty uses an outlined tray, and both filtered-empty states use a circled decreasing-lines filter glyph. The nine-test API 37 page class covers both initial/filtered empty pages and every populated iOS text/video filter against its rendered card. Evidence includes `memo-text-list-empty-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `3abfdc248f71df2f02e5b4e9d4f52d6661b9b47f1c1d4acf9fcaf024dbaf68a5`), `memo-video-list-empty-ios-parity.png` (`a599e2fe7883f1eabae228e2b242685acb5c2c14dc53b0233f7fcb7f8b2e68fd`), `memo-text-list-filter-empty-ios-parity.png` (`62d66702221d601d3ec18cff85d177e1bf5e9134362d5caeb9df01425c0fa516`), `memo-video-list-filter-empty-ios-parity.png` (`09210ed67b36a69373589e6a1639431af3e1274bf1778e0c4bc09113e5935572`), `memo-text-list-populated-ios-parity.png` (`5426789de3fa86c5b363a537c9523fad76aae434fdb8eb1a0434312c83b2f68b`), and `memo-video-list-populated-ios-parity.png` (`5a21bd41099a263d0c63554fea504a3fef8066e1d39cd4b9e90e4256c5faa8fa`), all 1440×3120.
- Video Memo secondary-list large cards now mirror iOS' `V2VideoMemoLargeCard` structure more closely: Android uses the 109dp card height, 120dp left thumbnail, 1dp list-border stroke, subtle 8dp black 4% card shadow, top black readability gradient, top-left duration chip, 32dp translucent play circle with a 12dp play glyph offset 1dp to the right, 12dp info padding, 15sp fairway title, 12sp description, 11sp meta text, and iOS-style source chip colors.
- Video Memo secondary-list large-card meta text now follows iOS' `lineLimit(1)` behavior with ellipsis, so long source/date strings stay inside the right info column.
- Text Memo list filters now use the same visible set and order as iOS: `全部 / 心法 / 反思 / 突破 / AI 洞察 / ★已收藏`; Android now also maps cloud text-Memo `source / priority / tags` into V2 topic/accent metadata so 心法, 突破, AI 洞察, and pinned filters work on real synced records rather than only local mock/display state.
- AI insight text Memo cards now display consistently across Android's text list, Memo home rows, and Home recent-Memo row: cloud AI records labeled with `source=ai` or "洞察" metadata show `AI 洞察` instead of falling back to the generic `心法` chip.
- Text Memo secondary-list large cards now mirror iOS' date-column, top-chip, text hierarchy, and footer details more closely: Android uses the 80dp muted date block, 1dp vertical divider, warm-white card surface, list-border stroke, subtle 8dp black 4% card shadow, 16sp fairway date text, 12dp content padding, 11sp medium bordered top chips for reflection/mind/breakthrough/AI/star states, a separated 15sp bold title plus 13sp regular body, and an iOS-style footer row with 11sp gray word count on the left and 11sp rounded tag chips on the right.
- Text Memo secondary-list bottom tag chips now keep iOS' compact one-line footer behavior by ellipsizing long tag names inside each chip instead of letting a single tag crowd the word count row.
- Text Memo secondary-list date-column weekday text now matches iOS' neutral `#888888` and one-line behavior instead of using the greener global shadow-gray token.
- Memo home AI insight card now matches iOS' visible casing for `AI 教练 · 我的 Memo 洞察` instead of rendering Android-only all-caps `MEMO`.
- Text Memo detail includes the iOS-style `心得详情` nav title, title subtitle with date/source, attachments section, two-line created/updated metadata, and decorative `挥呗Memo` footer. Its dashed empty state is now non-interactive like Swift, while imported legacy video/image attachment UUIDs survive local JSON, cloud reads, and subsequent edits and render as the matching count chips.
- Text Memo detail top bar now mirrors iOS' action treatment more closely: Android uses the `关闭` 13sp black outlined capsule instead of a green circular back arrow, keeps the centered `心得详情` title, removes the Android-only star toggle from the top bar, and matches the delete affordance to the 38dp red-tint circle with a 14dp trash glyph.
- Text Memo detail top-bar title now matches iOS' 16pt bold black treatment instead of the lighter green Android title style.
- Text Memo detail top bar now also matches iOS' asymmetric vertical rhythm: Android uses 12dp top and 16dp bottom padding instead of a uniform 12dp vertical inset.
- Text Memo detail now exposes the iOS-style priority chip, parent-category chip, and body counter: Android can cycle low/medium/high priority from the detail page, show the current tag group's parent category, persist `priorityRaw`, recompute the accent so the `突破` filter updates after saving, cap edited body text at 240 characters with a visible count, and disable saving when title or body is blank.
- Text Memo detail header now mirrors iOS' large-title editor more closely: Android replaces the Material outlined title field with an unframed 30sp black `标题` field, keeps the 24-character cap, uses the iOS muted placeholder, and aligns the date/source subtitle with the 2dp top offset.
- Text Memo detail body card now mirrors iOS' `bodyCard` treatment more closely: Android removes the extra `正文` header and `N/240` counter, uses the 18dp white card with 8dp black 7% shadow and 1dp 5% stroke, keeps 20dp inner padding, shows the iOS `开始写今天的感悟…` placeholder, and displays the bottom-right `N字` counter.
- Text Memo detail attachment header now matches iOS' temporarily-unwired affordance exactly: Android renders static gray `暂不支持添加`, uses `暂无附件` in the dashed 88dp placeholder, removes the old click/dialog route, and shows blue video plus green image count chips when preserved attachment relationships exist. API 37 verifies both populated attachment chips and the persistent save bar in `text-memo-detail-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `45f54a293e22e1a57b5dffaf9846fca95170b59b39aa5ebf0384b464a5502b7c`).
- Text Memo detail decorative footer now matches iOS' divider rhythm more closely: the top gold divider keeps the 16dp horizontal inset and 8dp bottom gap without adding an extra top gap before the footer copy.
- Text Memo detail metadata now aligns to the same 16dp page rail as iOS' `metaInfoSection`, removing the extra Android-only 2dp inset before the `创建于` / `更新于` rows.
- Text Memo detail editing keeps the scrollable content and always-present save bar above Android keyboard/system navigation, matching Swift instead of hiding the primary action until the first mutation. The priority/title/date elements are also grouped at iOS' 6dp internal rhythm rather than inheriting the page's 16dp section gaps.
- Text Memo detail save bar now mirrors iOS' `bottomBar` styling more closely: Android uses the warm-white translucent footer with a 1dp top divider, the 16dp vertical capsule padding, fairway 40% save-button shadow, 15dp check-circle icon, and iOS `保存` / `保存中…` copy.
- Text Memo detail save bar text now matches iOS' primary-action weight more closely: Android uses the same 16sp bold label for `保存` / `保存中…` instead of the lighter semibold weight.
- Text Memo detail editing now also uses the same save-safety posture as creation: Android acquires the save lock synchronously before starting persistence, waits for `updateAndWait` before leaving, shows `保存中...`, and makes rapid duplicate taps plus close/back inert. An injected update failure proves that the edited title/body/tags/goals/priority stay visible while the stored record is unchanged; the shared compact 270dp iOS-style `保存失败 / 好` alert permits exactly one successful retry. Coroutine cancellation still does not surface a false failure. API 37 evidence is `text-memo-detail-save-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `77630715aa295e200ade0f82b45a1c81a2f72c96707026aadd3658dd39f21932`).
- Text Memo detail discard and deletion now use the same iOS confirmation-dialog hierarchy as video-Memo editing: the former centered Material alerts are replaced by bottom action sheets with exact punctuation-free title/message copy, a separated destructive row, an independent cancel surface, scrim, and navigation-safe spacing. Deletion acquires its lock synchronously and waits for local persistence before leaving: the confirmation action shows `删除中…`, disables duplicate delete/cancel/back while in flight, and an injected failure proves the original Memo remains present. Recovery uses the same compact alert geometry with localized `删除失败 / 好`, and one retry removes the record and exits exactly once. API 37 verifies the hierarchy/actions in `text-memo-delete-confirm-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `d1918e804d75e906402fee18babf786c121af16cec865a746868c48e6b6884cb`) and failure/retry in `text-memo-delete-failure-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `a1774cff0d3329ad40df7a7dd6d2ed77b2955960380a7ffdc490d7a083e3c83b`).
- Text Memo detail now preserves every unsaved user-controlled edit through Android activity/process recreation: title, body, selected tags, linked goals, priority, dirty state, and open discard/delete confirmation state all use saveable state, while in-flight save/delete flags deliberately restart idle so a cancelled coroutine cannot leave the page locked. API 37 edits the real detail page, selects `上杆`, opens the iOS discard sheet, recreates the composition, and verifies the sheet plus draft survive in `text-memo-detail-restoration-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `3a646d6c1f99f92ecb5acc6be017ba863b4ff2cd113fcbf1020254348dfa3130`).
- Memo secondary-list filter chips now scroll horizontally and match iOS' chip styling more closely: Android uses 32dp capsule height, 14dp horizontal padding, 13sp labels, fairway text for unselected chips, transparent selected borders, and the same horizontal scroll behavior, preventing the text-list filters (`全部 / 心法 / 反思 / 突破 / AI 洞察 / ★已收藏`) from clipping on narrow screens or larger font settings.
- Memo secondary-list time-group headers now match iOS' `V2MemoListSectionHeader`: Android uses the muted full-width strip, 13sp bold fairway text, and 16dp horizontal / 6dp top / 5dp bottom padding.
- Memo secondary-list card rhythm now follows iOS' grouped `LazyVStack`: Android gives each video/text large card a 12dp top offset under its time header and inserts the same 8dp gap before subsequent date groups instead of splitting the spacing into 6dp top/bottom card padding.
- Video Memo and text Memo secondary lists now use the same iOS-style "没有更多了" footer with side divider lines instead of Android's plain dashed text row.
- Text Memo secondary-list large cards now match iOS' conditional top-chip spacing: Android only keeps the 8dp title offset when a category or pinned chip is actually rendered, so plain text entries no longer start with an empty chip-row gap.
- Memo home swing-record cards now mirror iOS' interaction split: tapping the day card opens the swing calendar, while tapping one of the preview thumbnails opens that exact swing clip detail directly.
- Memo home swing-record cards now mirror iOS' `V2MemoSwingSessionCard` visual structure more closely: Android uses the 80dp date block, 1dp vertical divider, 8dp black 4% card shadow, 15sp title, 12sp metadata rows, 14dp live-dot halo, 44dp rounded overlapping clip thumbnails with a `+N` overlay on the final preview, and a 12dp trailing chevron.
- Swing calendar empty states now provide a launch-safe next action: empty all-record views can enter swing recording, and empty retention filters can clear back to the day's full clip list.
- GolfCam preview now follows iOS' build boundary exactly: Release renders only `飞行轨迹 / 导出`; DEBUG additionally shows the disabled `杆头轨迹 / 裁剪` cards with their `稍后` badges. Android's former tappable explanation dialog has been removed because current SwiftUI keeps those debug-only cards inert.
- GolfCam video selection now has a launch-safe Android picker fallback: the Home "轨迹绘制" source sheet stays open with localized retry copy if the system picker cannot launch, and in-flow reselect failures no longer clear the current trajectory-editing state before a new video is actually chosen. Deterministic launch tests lock both success/failure paths without depending on a particular system picker implementation.
- Launch placeholder/source audit now guards the remaining intentional placeholders: `LaunchPlaceholderAuditTest` fails on any empty `onClick` in launch source, while launch placeholder copy remains limited to documented cases such as static notification settings, the iOS AI-goal stub, disabled text voice affordance, and GolfCam deferred badges.
- GolfCam video import now also recovers from thrown workflow-restore or video-config detection failures: Android clears the busy restore/detect state, drops the half-initialized workflow, and returns to the iOS-style source sheet with localized retry guidance instead of hanging on a processing card; cancellation is rethrown silently. Six flow tests and two API 37 source-sheet tests cover launch, restore, detection, success, cancellation, and retry. Evidence is `golfcam-import-recovery-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `aa70e4b517b1f26cf26bd627b924d634e408c227fdbfa993bad17f77bf6def58`).
- GolfCam preview export/save release failures no longer expose raw codec, temp-file, content-URI, user-path, or MediaStore exception text; Android maps them to stable localized recovery guidance while debug builds can append the low-level detail for diagnosis. `GolfCamExportCopyTest` now locks release hiding, debug preservation, and coroutine-cancellation preservation for these export/gallery-save diagnostics.
- Profile settings QR entry now has a working logged-out path: the card stays tappable, explains that login is required to generate the friend QR code, and opens login instead of becoming an inert disabled row.
- Profile settings QR entry now preserves the logged-out user's intent: tapping "我的二维码" while logged out signs in first, then returns to settings and opens the full-screen QR page automatically.
- Profile settings QR gallery save now has release-safe thrown-failure recovery: Android catches thrown MediaStore insert/write/publish operations, shows localized retry guidance, cleans pending entries when available, preserves coroutine cancellation through the shared throwable helper, and `V2ProfileSettingsCopyTest` locks release builds against MediaStore, content-URI, token, local-path, or exception diagnostic leakage while debug builds keep troubleshooting detail.
- Profile settings QR now matches iOS `FriendsUserQRCodeView` instead of using an Android alert: it is a dark three-color gradient full-screen page with the same 52dp safe-area navigation stack and 18dp back glyph, centered title, white user/QR card, 220dp branded QR, exact guidance copy, and the bottom `扫一扫 / 换个样式 / 保存图片` action bar. The former Android-only raw payload, current-style label, copy-link action, and completion button are removed. Its four QR palettes match iOS (`经典 / 紫色 / 蓝色 / 绿色`), `扫一扫` reuses the real CameraX/gallery recognizer and hands a decoded account into the authenticated friend-request surface, and the center logo uses the byte-identical iOS `2ee…a01`/Android `auth_logo` asset without breaking decoding. The saved 1125×1500 (3× iOS) share image now also uses the exact iOS coordinates, type scale, `#333333 / #999999 / #CCCCCC` colors, and unframed QR; its cropped purple-style QR is decoded in instrumentation. API 37 verifies the full hierarchy, style action, and back callback in `profile-qr-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `25dfec276023a7c805d7441993d9501ff0c6285afa032d301e8c1ddb30453f7b`) and decodes both the live branded QR and saved share-image QR back to the exact `huibei://user/{userId}` payload.
- The iOS navigation atlas `PROFILE-001`, `PROFILE-002`, `PROFILE-003`, `PROFILE-004`, `PROFILE-005`, `PROFILE-006`, `PROFILE-007`, `PROFILE-008`, `PROFILE-009`, `PROFILE-010`, `PROFILE-011`, `PROFILE-012`, and `PROFILE-013` is represented on Android's Me surface: profile home, edit profile, AI archive, storage management, credits calibration, AI interface config, feedback, account security, credit purchase, avatar source, location picker, archive manual-add entry, and the V2 settings page all have active routes. The calibration page now keeps iOS' exact two-action structure; Android's Google Play purchase remains a separate low-credit-download destination required for launch.
- Profile settings QR image save now has release-safe gallery feedback: Android distinguishes QR image generation failure, unwritable system album, write failure, and publish failure, and cleans up incomplete MediaStore image entries instead of showing only `保存图片失败`.
- Profile settings QR image save now also clears its saving state through a `finally` path when bitmap generation or MediaStore calls throw before returning a handled gallery result, keeping the full-screen page recoverable with localized iOS-style `提示` feedback.
- Android-only developer diagnostics no longer alter any active V2 Profile or Settings hierarchy, including debuggable internal accounts, matching the current iOS render tree. The guarded cloud-consistency implementation remains in the repository for engineering diagnostics, but its legacy `开发者工具 / 云端一致性` card is not mounted in product UI.
- Storage management now respects Android navigation-bar insets, keeping all six storage rows and the final cache action reachable like iOS' safe-area settings page. API 37 exercises both gesture navigation and the system three-button overlay; the latter is recorded in `storage-management-three-button-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `14da2c16f7371887c76f05b774e838099313e586aff426539b40f8bed3e9b2a6`).
- Storage management now follows iOS' storage taxonomy and destructive boundaries exactly: it reports downloaded videos, thumbnail cache, local AI models, temporary files, avatar cache, and other cache separately; each non-empty row can be cleared individually, while `清理所有缓存` clears the four cache categories without deleting videos or local AI models.
- Storage management refresh and cleanup now recover from thrown filesystem/cache errors: Android clears the loading state through `finally` and shows localized `操作失败` guidance for refresh, cache-only cleanup, and normal video/cache cleanup instead of leaving the page stuck. Persistent video/thumbnail/model/avatar deletion failures are no longer silently reported as success, and destructive-result dialogs wait for the confirmation dialog to finish dismissing so immediate failures cannot produce stacked alerts. API 37 injects refresh, cache-clear, and video-clear throws and proves the page re-enables in `storage-management-failure-recovery-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `71eba890264c8a19f6ca8128249e09f9c9017a139547abeceb2af5256041e8b3`).
- Storage management now mirrors current iOS `StorageManagementView`: its 44dp modal toolbar is `关闭 / 存储管理 / 刷新`, the overview uses the same device-relative ring with iOS' intentional ×10 visual amplification and the three largest sorted categories, the detail list uses all six iOS category names/colors/icons and file counts, and the former Android-only two-action split is replaced by the single 50dp red-gradient `清理所有缓存` action. API 37 verifies the complete hierarchy and visible destructive button in `storage-management-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `bf713590970eab983708c5f6acbafbc4fe292ccfd3f0e10b024482c1cb9ed896`), plus the exact video-deletion warning in `storage-management-video-warning-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `7a111222046359e8a38bc762f571f5eb4d644c50ae0a0765dba079978c7ea34d`).
- Profile AI training archive entry now uses login-first behavior before opening cloud-backed archive content, and its subtitle no longer claims AI already knows the user while logged out.
- The iOS navigation atlas `AI-001`, `AI-002`, and `AI-003` is covered by Android's AI chat, history sheet, and service config surfaces, including login gating, streaming/stop states, session switching/deletion, provider/model/API-key configuration, and contextual profile/Memo/training metadata.
- Profile edit avatar updates now match the iOS source picker more closely: tapping the avatar on Android opens a choice between system camera capture and photo-library selection, both feeding the existing avatar upload path with visible uploading/error states; the default avatar placeholder and bottom save button copy now also follow the iOS `点击更换头像` / `保存资料` hierarchy.
- Profile edit avatar visuals now mirror the iOS `V2ProfileEditView` treatment: Android uses a 108dp gold gradient ring around a clipped 100dp avatar, the fallback is a 40% shadow-gray account circle on mist green, and the 32dp fairway camera badge has the same white outline and compact camera glyph.
- Profile edit's top form now follows the active iOS render tree rather than the former Android Material interpretation: the modal toolbar uses a leading text `取消`, nickname is a plain mist-green field with `输入您的昵称`, birthday keeps its label and switch on one row, the bio editor has no Android-only placeholder, and card/title/label/count/save typography matches the SwiftUI sizes. The save request also follows iOS field semantics: a truly empty nickname is omitted, while clearing gender, birthday, region, or bio submits an explicit empty value so stale cloud data is removed; missing dominant hand defaults to `右手`, and an empty brand set is omitted. The avatar-source overlay uses the same three centered rows, 16dp icons, 16dp corners, and 36dp screen margins. API 37 verifies the page and overlay in `profile-edit-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `50935227da40500b34b9561c25234a24f426b4bd7c4cc11c08636fe23b627ae3`) and `profile-edit-avatar-source-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `29d628aa5e74e3d89b1b0ec870facb4f63a1a1cb314d139b3b31fda90cb6e75a`).
- Profile edit form surfaces now follow the iOS card and field tokens more closely: Android's basic/golf/brand cards use the 16dp white card with fairway 6% light shadow, form labels keep 8dp label-to-control spacing, text/date/location controls use mist-green no-border fills, the region row uses the iOS 13dp muted chevron affordance, and choice chips use the shorter 12dp corner treatment instead of Android-only outlined pills.
- Profile edit save action now uses the iOS gradient-button treatment: Android renders the 52dp `保存资料` button with a fairway-to-deep-green horizontal gradient, 16dp corner radius, fairway 30% shadow, and the same reduced-opacity saving state.
- Profile edit selection controls now match the iOS layout rhythm more closely: gender and dominant-hand choices stretch into equal-width 42dp buttons with 8dp gaps, while golf-brand choices render as a fixed three-column grid with 10dp spacing and the iOS 13sp semibold brand chip treatment.
- Profile edit golf-info inputs now follow the iOS constrained picker model instead of free-form Android text: handicap defaults to `18.0`, is split into integer `0...54` and decimal `0...9` wheel-style selectors in a 120dp mist-green field, and start year defaults to the current year with a `1970...currentYear` wheel-style selector while preserving the existing golf-age hint/save payload.
- Profile edit avatar source selection now also matches the iOS visual hierarchy more closely: Android uses the same centered three-row `拍照` / `从相册选择` / `取消` panel instead of a heavier titled dialog, while keeping the existing camera/gallery upload flow.
- Profile edit avatar photo-library selection now has the same release-safe picker fallback as camera capture: if Android cannot open the system media picker, the edit form remains active and shows localized retry guidance.
- Profile edit avatar replacement now also recovers from thrown image-read, upload, camera-output, and FileProvider errors: the edit form clears the uploading state and shows localized retry copy instead of letting the `PROFILE-010 -> SYSTEM-005` path crash or hang.
- Profile edit save feedback now matches iOS more closely: Android shows an explicit `保存成功` dialog before returning, routes save API failures into a `保存失败` dialog, and blocks top-bar back while the save request is in flight.
- Profile edit release failures no longer expose raw avatar-upload, camera/gallery-launch, FileProvider, or profile-save exception text; Android shows stable localized recovery copy, while debug builds can append low-level detail for internal diagnosis. `V2ProfileEditCopyTest` now locks release hiding of backend/path/exception diagnostics across avatar upload, picker launch, camera launch, and save failures.
- Profile edit save now clears its saving state through a `finally` path even when the profile update call throws before returning a handled failure, keeping the edit form recoverable like iOS' save sheet.
- Profile edit now keeps long forms, region search, and the bottom save action above Android keyboard/system navigation, matching the iOS edit-profile safe-area posture on small screens.
- Profile edit now preserves nickname, gender, birthday, region, bio, handicap, start year, dominant hand, brand selection, and open picker state across Android saved-state restoration. Camera output URI/path is also saved while the system camera is open, so activity recreation cannot orphan the captured file or lose its result. API 37 restores an edited nickname and added brand, then saves and verifies the exact payload.
- Profile edit avatar upload now mirrors iOS' transient preview semantics: a decoded selection appears immediately under the uploading veil, success retains it, and failure restores the previous avatar before showing `保存失败`; camera success/cancel both remove the temporary output. Failure recovery is recorded in `profile-edit-avatar-failure-recovery-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `f98280c38a4f846d0aec0fff65c8fe91ad34f7112c86c1312970cce0aa7a4234`).
- Profile edit region selection now matches the current iOS `V2LocationPickerSheet` rather than extending it with Android-only behavior: it uses the root-sheet title with only the right-side `完成` action, the same mist-green 12dp search field, Room background, white 16dp cards, exact 16/34 counts, fixed four-column popular-city and three-column province grids, selected fairway chips, Chinese substring filtering, matching empty states, filled-circle `xmark.circle.fill` clear treatment, and selection-time dismissal. API 37 verifies the full initial page, clear control, both search-empty cards, selection behavior, and keyboard-safe actions in `profile-location-picker-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `5f31e699089ee71a7253a88a8ff3f7bff20efb254bbf7cfb7a60e37df950c312`) and `profile-location-picker-search-empty-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `85046a0e779f8756cd423e0d56d1bf1f825e1e6e30170ed8240b7d966f1c47e6`). Three-button evidence is `profile-edit-keyboard-three-button-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `9b3eefea439c80e8ae680c8ed329fab9018fb73b3eb293cb0daadc660b999475`) and `profile-location-picker-search-empty-three-button-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `31b56c34882e893e144e0587382c54c6fdf0a86e99d9f8d3f7fcfab1f8963cf5`).
- Account security edit feedback now matches iOS' explicit confirmation posture more closely: nickname edits and phone bind/rebind now close the editor and show an `操作成功` dialog with action-specific copy instead of relying only on the row text changing.
- Account security editor fields now mirror the iOS security dialog treatment more closely: nickname, phone, and SMS-code inputs use 12dp mist-green filled fields with transparent idle borders, fairway focus, ink-green text, and muted placeholders instead of default outlined Material fields.
- Account security nickname editing now matches the current iOS modal structure instead of the former Material alert: Android uses the same 60% dim layer, 320dp white card, 20dp radius, subtle fairway border, 48dp gray input, and paired 44dp actions. API 37 verifies it in `account-nickname-dialog-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `26f543c4092d5ee62a6f5dbccc18d6d221291bf31d554c432de76194e5c2e41e`).
- Phone binding/rebinding now mirrors iOS' compact branded card and backend decision flow: the byte-identical golf-course artwork, phone/code controls, send countdown, continue/cancel hierarchy, and current-number subtitle are aligned; `available`, `already_bound`, and `conflict` responses are handled, with conflict-account details shown before the user explicitly confirms merge-and-bind or chooses another phone number. API 37 covers the normal and conflict branches in `account-phone-binding-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `6ce71de3977c33f4c7519ba688cd0ee8522b548f46df6304a9bca31c17e8a76c`).
- Account security release failures no longer expose raw nickname-update, SMS-code, or phone-bind exception text; Android shows stable localized recovery copy, while debug builds can append low-level detail for internal diagnosis. `V2AccountSafetyCopyTest` now locks release hiding of token/path/backend diagnostics and debug preservation of low-level details.
- Account security nickname, SMS-code, phone-bind, and logout actions now clear their loading/sending states through `finally` paths when repository/profile-refresh/logout calls throw, so dialogs and the sign-out row cannot remain permanently busy after transport or local-store failures; logout failures now also show a localized `退出失败` dialog instead of failing silently, while cancellation is preserved through the shared logout helper.
- Account security nickname and phone-bind dialogs now avoid Android keyboard/system-navigation overlap, keeping confirm/cancel actions reachable while entering account data.
- User agreement and privacy-policy pages now respect Android navigation-bar insets, keeping the final legal paragraphs readable like iOS' safe-area document view.
- Profile logout now matches iOS' complete state and confirmation treatment: active reliable delivery changes the row to `内容发送中，暂不可退出`, removes its chevron, and blocks the action; otherwise Android shows the 24dp rounded branded card with the byte-identical `GolfCourseBackground`, green overlay, exact `退出登录 / 退出后仍可重新登录此账号 / 确认退出当前账号？` copy, and equal `取消 / 确认退出` actions instead of a generic Material alert. Thrown logout failures still show localized recovery and cancellation cannot surface a false failure. API 37 verifies the account toolbar/nickname affordance, blocked-delivery state, and branded dialog in `account-security-logout-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `004fd23061e60d5cd1e5e8eedcfa8bd75d6593d0707cd6ea3d1d61ad7f7b2886`).
- Profile more-list credit entry now matches latest iOS semantics again: Android labels the row `积分重置 / 校准余额与本地缓存` with the refresh glyph because the Me-tab entry opens the calibration/reset screen, while purchase remains available from the low-credit download flow.
- Credits reset now matches the current iOS page structure and refresh semantics: Android shows only `刷新服务端余额` and `清除本地缓存`, removes its former page-local purchase action, and performs the initial balance fetch silently without forcing a server refresh or showing a misleading success/error banner. The credits repository remains the single profile-session update path, avoiding the prior duplicate profile refresh, while manual refresh stays authoritative and uses the exact success copy. Its 32dp bottom inset, clock/status, filled counterclockwise-circle Hero, refresh/trash actions, typography, gradients, and compact audit glyphs follow the same visual hierarchy. API 37 verifies the signed-in 168-credit page in `profile-credits-reset-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `13e7de192acef8ccd976738c6e52b7e492f49ba664a8fc51b9970148fdaa4178`) and the native cache-clear confirmation in `profile-credits-reset-confirm-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `81cbd5acc9f0ff8d0ce7d7e8436553d224eeed751a3fcaf57844c2c893f6c582`).
- Credits calibration time now has the same durable, account-scoped meaning as iOS `CreditsManager.lastUpdateTime`: a server-confirmed refresh is persisted in the session store, restored after leaving/reopening the page or restarting the app, reused by the five-minute cache decision, isolated between accounts, and removed together with the active account's local balance snapshot before reset. A silent cache hit retains its original server-confirmed time instead of pretending the screen-open time was a new sync. Manual refresh success is recorded in `profile-credits-reset-success-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `e86489cc875c83a4cdc28413bf8fa943f45ecec959670cc4784c5733225a9c1d`).
- Credits reset/calibration failures now preserve coroutine cancellation through the shared throwable helper, so leaving the calibration page cannot surface a false `服务端余额同步失败` message while real refresh/reset failures still show release-safe localized recovery copy. The confirmation, success, and error states survive Android saved-state restoration where appropriate, while loading intentionally resets with a cancelled screen coroutine. Six API 37 tests now cover the current hierarchy, refresh success, null-result reset recovery, thrown fetch/reset recovery, spinner teardown, action re-enablement, and confirmation restoration; persisted account isolation remains covered directly by `V2CreditsSessionInstrumentedTest`.
- Online-download low-credit handling now has direct API 37 boundary evidence: both the iOS-matching `充值` card and the URL-row `去充值后下载` action enter purchase first, neither mixes calibration actions into the download hierarchy, and a refreshed balance immediately restores `开始下载` without leaving the page. Evidence is `online-download-low-credit-ios-parity.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `450f4c8a0f3aa68f972519151d7eb63985017da1ca6343a1bf1df425e7e6fbb2`). Stale-display recovery remains on the separate iOS-matching `积分重置` page.
- Credits purchase copy now follows iOS' download-credit framing while preserving Android's Google Play safeguards: the hero says `购买下载积分`, product rows show estimated HD-download counts, the 300-credit badge uses `最划算`, and purchasable rows use the stronger `立即购买` action.
- Credits purchase now follows the complete current iOS `CreditsPurchaseView` render tree instead of the former Android-only light V2 card page: the full surface is black; the overlay top bar uses the 48dp dark close circle and centered `充值积分`; the 60dp gold star, 24sp `购买下载积分` header, dark/gold current-balance card, unwrapped `选择充值档位` section, dark 16dp tier cards, purple radio selection, purple-to-blue `最划算` badge and 52dp unified action, connection hint, and three-row dark purchase note are aligned. Android intentionally substitutes `Google Play` for Apple's payment copy and keeps refresh/restore, missing-Activity, no-product, partial-product, obfuscated-account, idempotent-grant, and support-order safeguards. The selected tier and unavailable-service dialog now survive Android saved-state recreation instead of silently reverting the purchase intent to 300 credits; the guest version keeps all tiers hidden behind the account gate, and a successful grant exposes the iOS-style `返回继续` action back to the originating download task. Nine API 37 tests verify rendering, tier/dialog restoration, guest login routing, success handoff, no/partial/all product states, unavailable recovery, the blocking purchase overlay, recovery-notice semantics, and close action. Evidence includes `credits-purchase-ios-dark.png` (1440×3120, SHA-256 `301bec342ff504de8b4fb0bdaf3e7592f2550f2ae63f5fa6fb5a6c9dea4efd0f`), `credits-purchase-partial-products-android-recovery.png` (SHA-256 `220bcb97e257ca6f1fbe84e5e4b991c1d15b0ba0866767622af63f773979a604`), `credits-purchase-processing-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `d1bf9a752af3dbd846b38a7a7c2670988686e66786dc4805982cd89977956f9a`), and `credits-purchase-recovery-notice-ios-parity.png` (SHA-256 `86f5ef653ac8caca53e7ca0aa9cb69f2a351045213b3059b812b99306d520f71`).
- Credits purchase now mirrors iOS' in-flight purchase posture: Android shows a full-page `处理中...` progress overlay while Google Play purchase launch/grant is active, blocking stray taps until the billing state resolves.
- Account deletion now has Android-side launch safety around the iOS destructive flow: logged-out users cannot start deletion, the first confirm opens a second irreversible-action dialog, and the copy distinguishes deleted account/training records from original videos retained on the device.
- Account deletion warning/list iconography now mirrors iOS' destructive sheet more closely: Android uses the filled warning triangle for the hero alert and filled circle-x glyphs for each data-loss row instead of outline/standalone close icons.
- Account deletion's final destructive confirmation now uses the same data-retention semantics as the main warning and iOS privacy copy: Android says account/cloud/training-note/metadata records are deleted while downloaded original video files stay on the device, avoiding the ambiguous "本机训练记录" phrasing.
- Account deletion release failures no longer expose raw deletion exception/backend text; Android shows stable localized retry/support copy, while debug builds can append low-level detail for internal diagnosis. `V2AccountSafetyCopyTest` covers release hiding of deletion backend/user-path detail and debug diagnostics.
- Account deletion progress now updates from the calling UI coroutine while DB/network cleanup stays on IO, preserves coroutine cancellation through both UI and repository failure helpers, blocks duplicate destructive starts, and clears its deleting state through a `finally` path for thrown cleanup failures. `V2AccountDeletionRepositoryTest` covers the repository boundary directly.
- Password setup now handles Android's separate-page flow more clearly than the previous disabled SMS form: if no phone number is bound, the page shows a direct "return to bind phone" state instead of leaving users on a form that cannot send a code.
- Feedback now mirrors the complete current iOS `FeedbackView` hierarchy and authentication boundary: Android uses the 44dp warm-white modal bar with centered `意见反馈` and leading `取消`, the same 10dp-spaced three-type chips and lightbulb/ladybug/ellipsis-bubble equivalents, exact 14sp labels, per-type colors, 150dp mist-green content editor, 11sp `0/500` counter, optional-contact typography, and selected-type submit gradient/shadow. The former Android-only over-limit explanation and inline error row are removed; submit remains disabled for blank or over-500 content. Every submit now rechecks the live session like iOS `AuthGate`; an expired session opens login and restores feedback type, content, and contact on return. Submission failures keep the complete draft editable and the action available for retry. Four API 37 tests verify hierarchy, type/length transitions, expired-session login restoration, and failure-to-success retry; `feedback-ios-parity.png` is 1440×3120 with SHA-256 `b277194f10eb84ee5701d345a62c4b538a5b56a789f0d64a49204195c7c1f963`.
- Feedback submission now matches iOS' resilient support path more closely: Android first submits through `accountManagement.submitFeedback`, then falls back to the legacy `download.mygolfai.com.cn/api/feedback` endpoint if the cloud function path fails, preserving a support outlet for payment, download, and plaza issues.
- Feedback legacy fallback response handling is now covered by `V2FeedbackRepositoryTest`, including explicit server success, HTTP success without server error, explicit server failure, and cloud+legacy combined diagnostics before release-copy filtering.
- Feedback submission failures now match iOS' alert posture: Android shows a `提交失败` dialog while keeping the typed content/contact intact for retry, instead of relying on an easy-to-miss inline error line near the form.
- Feedback submission release failures no longer expose raw primary/fallback channel diagnostics; Android shows stable localized retry copy, while debug builds can append the combined cloud/legacy failure detail for internal investigation. `V2FeedbackCopyTest` now locks release hiding of cloud-function, HTTP, fallback, exception, and user-path diagnostics.
- Feedback submission now clears its loading state through a `finally` path even when the feedback repository throws before returning a handled failure, so the submit button never remains indefinitely disabled after a transport crash.

### 2026-06-28 Atlas Edge Coverage Pass

This pass checks the iOS navigation atlas at the edge/path level, not only by page ID. Current Android code has a reachable launch path for each launch-critical V2 atlas group; remaining items below are acceptance evidence or intentionally deferred large swing-analysis workstreams, not missing V2 navigation nodes.

| iOS atlas edge group | Android path evidence | Launch note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `APP-001`, `ROOT-001`, `AUTH-001/002/003/005`, `LEGAL-001/002` | `V2RootScreen` boots to login or tabs from session state; login opens register, reset password, terms, and privacy; register has `已有账户？立即登录`; post-auth can route to `CompleteProfile`; legal docs are shared from auth/profile/account security. | Regress login/register/reset-password success, thrown failures, and empty-nickname profile completion. |
| `HOME-001` tab exits plus active `HOME-002/003` | `TabsScaffold` connects Home/Memo/AI/Plaza/Me, plus Home quick add and the current compact common-tool row/configuration sheet. The historical `HOME-004` developer switch is absent from current SwiftUI and Android. | Regress the fixed shortcut row, configuration-sheet persistence, and guest login boundary. |
| `HOME-001 -> TOOL-001/TRAJ-001/DOWNLOAD-001` and `TOOL-001 -> SYSTEM-001/002` | Home common tools route record, replay analysis, trajectory, and download. Replay analysis opens the source dialog for live recording, album import, or existing swing library; trajectory opens GolfCam; download opens `V2OnlineDownloadScreen`. | Covers the highest-risk old Android empty-callback gap. |
| `GOAL-001...006` | Home/Quick Add/All Goals create goals, attach Memo through `V2GoalMemoPickerSheet`, open detail, edit, and delete from all-goal swipe or edit confirmation. | Test login continuation for goal creation from Home, Memo, and All Goals. |
| `DOWNLOAD-001...005` plus `DOWNLOAD-003 -> MEMO-003` | V2 download page covers recognition, credit state, confirmation/cancel, progress, pending restore, async suggestion, history, purchase/calibration, and post-download Video Memo edit handoff. | Play Console product activation and real purchase grant/consume evidence remain release blockers for payment, not route coverage. |
| `MEMO-001...014` | Memo tab, all/video/text lists, quick-add sheet, text/video create, online-video picker, tag sheet, swing library/detail, player, plaza-backed Memo, and comparison player cover the memo atlas paths. | The main player now includes the full continuous inertial scrub timeline; regress local/online duration changes, pause/resume, density switching, and drawing visibility on device. |
| `AI-001/002/003` | AI tab opens full-screen chat; close returns Home; history sheet supports select/new/delete; config opens `V2AIServiceConfigScreen`. `V2AIChatStore` now prepends account profile, golf profile, active goals, recent Memo, and swing-practice summary before streaming. | Regress provider switching, built-in quota exhaustion, stop/timeout, and history login handoff. |
| `PLAZA-001...006` and `PLAZA-004 -> MEMO-007` | Plaza feed, immersive player, comments, save/favorite, like, report/claim, coach profile, saved list, watch history, player download, and plaza-to-Memo confirmation are cloud/local connected. | Regress logged-out gating, comment response shapes, favorite rollback, report/claim failure copy, and no-source player recovery. |
| `PROFILE-001...012` and `SYSTEM-005` | Me tab opens edit profile, AI archive/profile, storage, credit reset/purchase, AI config, feedback, legal docs, account security, avatar source selection, region picker, golf-profile add-entry, and logout/login. | Profile surfaces now have safe-area and release-copy fixes; test avatar camera/gallery failure and region search. |
| `SYSTEM-001...004` shared media edges | Android uses camera capture, system photo/video pickers, FileProvider-backed imports, and release-safe picker/camera fallbacks across Video Memo, replay analysis, and trajectory. `SYSTEM-003` is the trajectory video picker and `SYSTEM-004` is the Video Memo album picker; both preserve their originating flow after selection or launch failure. | Device QA should cover permission denial, unavailable picker/camera handlers, and imported-video metadata. |

### Still Intentionally Deferred

- Android credit purchase still needs Play Console product activation and server-side Google Play receipt/token validation before release-hardening. The client now has BillingClient plumbing, sends purchase-token metadata to the existing credit-grant endpoint, and avoids repeating a local grant after consume-confirm failure, but production purchase safety still depends on backend verification and idempotency.
- Watch connectivity and loop-buffer recording remain large platform-specific swing-analysis workstreams, not quick UI parity fixes. The current iOS professional-analysis body-skeleton overlay is now implemented on Android with the same confidence/cadence/topology behavior; any future semantic pose scoring or swing classification remains a separate coordinated feature rather than being implied by this visual overlay. Audio-based impact-frame candidates are now covered in the Android swing detail page.
- GolfCam trajectory parity is tracked separately in the GolfCam V2 ledger below; the remaining work is device acceptance evidence rather than a new redesign.

### Launch Risk Focus

- Covered 2026-10-08 on API 37: low-credit and refreshed-credit download states remain actionable; the live profile balance update restores `开始下载` without page recreation.
- Covered 2026-10-08 on API 37: both the credit card and URL action row point normal insufficiency to purchase first, while refresh/calibration remains the separate stale-balance recovery page.
- Covered 2026-10-08 by six Credits Reset API tests plus release-copy unit tests: injected fetch/reset throws stop the loading spinner, re-enable actions, and retain localized production guidance without backend diagnostics. The former separate profile-refresh failure boundary was removed when the repository became the single refresh/update path.
- Treat Android credit purchase as a launch blocker until the three product IDs are active in Play Console and real test purchases prove grant/consume/retry behavior. Client code exists, but payment configuration and receipt-validation policy still need release evidence.
- Local UI coverage complete 2026-10-08: injected all/partial/no-product catalogs render the expected purchasable tiers and inline recovery cards, and the active purchase shows the iOS blocking overlay. Real Play Console activation/propagation evidence for those states remains an external launch gate.
- Local callback/UI coverage complete 2026-10-08: user-cancel silently clears the blocking state; Google Play OK with an empty purchase list and unspecified/pending purchase states clear the overlay and expose non-success recovery guidance; ordinary terminal failure clears any stale success notice; purchase launch includes a non-empty obfuscated account id rather than the raw user id. Real Play/backend evidence is still required for grant API failure, consume failure after grant, restore of an already-granted unconsumed purchase after app re-entry without a duplicate grant call, and restore of a never-granted unconsumed purchase.
- Test credit purchase failure copy in a release build: disconnect/network/product-unavailable/launch-failure/grant-failure/consume-failure states should show localized recovery guidance, keep an order id or token suffix when support follow-up is needed, and should not expose raw Google Play `debugMessage` or backend exception strings.
- Test credit purchase login transition: open purchase while logged out, trigger login, return to the purchase page, and confirm products load/restore automatically without requiring a manual refresh.
- Test credit purchase success handoff: enter purchase from a low-credit download state, complete a test purchase, tap `返回继续`, and confirm the download page still has the original URL/task context and refreshed balance.
- Test account-content creation handoff: while logged out, tap new Goal, Text Memo, and Video Memo from Home, Memo lists, All Goals, and Quick Add; after login/register/reset-password success, Android should open the originally requested creation page and back should return to the original context.
- Post-auth profile-completion routing is covered deterministically for blank/nonblank nicknames, forced registration, and every pending business continuation; one real backend account with a raw empty nickname remains useful production acceptance evidence rather than an unguarded client rule.
- Test profile login handoff: while logged out, tap profile edit/avatar, goal stats, video-Memo stats, swing stats, AI archive, credit center, and account security; after login/register/reset-password success, Android should open the originally requested profile destination instead of returning only to the Me tab.
- Test plaza login handoff: while logged out, tap the Plaza `我的收藏` entry and the watch-history login card; after login/register/reset-password success, Android should open the independent favorites sheet or remain/open the watch-history page without duplicating the back stack.
- Learner bottom tabs have direct API 37 visual and interaction coverage: Home/Memo/Tools/Me keep their glyphs while tint/dot selection changes, and the raised `挥呗` entry verifies its complete 58dp selected ring, connection label, `99+` cap, and real callback. Evidence: `student-bottom-tabs-ios-parity.png`.
- Test auth failure copy in a release build: local helper tests now lock release-safe copy and debug diagnostics for shared auth failures; still verify SMS send, login, register, reset-password, and change/set-password paths on device to confirm each screen re-enables actions and preserves typed fields.
- Regress the verified login fallback routes after auth/navigation changes: SMS mode must retain `不想登录？先看看`, password mode must retain `还没有账号？现在注册`, and only the password link should open registration.
- Test auth thrown-error recovery: inject SMS, login, registration, reset-password, and reset-password auto-login throws; each screen should re-enable its primary action, preserve typed fields, and show localized retry copy instead of staying on loading.
- Regress reset-password support tips as informational rows only; the current iOS page does not attach mail or dial actions to them.
- Test feedback submission with the primary cloud function available and unavailable; the unavailable case should still succeed through the legacy feedback endpoint, while debug builds may append fallback failure detail when both channels fail.
- Test feedback submission failure UI by forcing both primary and fallback feedback endpoints to fail; Android should show `提交失败`, keep the typed content/contact, and allow a retry after dismissing the dialog.
- Test feedback submission failure copy in a release build: local helper tests now lock release-safe copy for primary cloud-function and legacy fallback diagnostics; still force both channels to fail on device to confirm the dialog keeps typed content/contact intact.
- Test feedback thrown-exception recovery: inject a repository throw and confirm the submit button re-enables with the localized failure dialog.
- Test plaza interactions against real cloud data, especially logged-out like/favorite/comment/report/claim/download/create-Memo login gating from the player, plaza grid, coach profile, and header saved-list entry; also cover like/favorite rollback, comment response shapes, and report/claim submission failures.
- Test plaza like/favorite cancellation and rollback: cancel the background sync coroutine during player/list lifecycle changes and confirm no false error or extra rollback appears; force real cloud/local failures and confirm the optimistic state rolls back with localized retry copy.
- Test plaza player login handoff: while logged out, tap comment, download, and report/claim from the player; after login/register/reset-password success, Android should open the comments sheet, the V2 download page with that video URL, or the feedback dialog respectively.
- Test the iOS navigation atlas `PLAZA-003/005` sheets specifically: load and reload comments, send a comment, report another user's comment, submit a video report, and submit a content-claim request with and without required contact info.
- Test plaza comments dialog specifically: comment loading failure should show in the dialog, send failure should keep the typed draft and show an inline error, editing should clear the error, and retry should still append the new comment on success.
- Test plaza comments failure-with-content states: after comments have loaded, force send/report failure and confirm the error banner appears above the existing comment list instead of replacing the discussion context.
- Test plaza comments thrown-error recovery: inject `fetchComments` and `addComment` throws outside normal `Result` failures, confirm loading/submitting indicators stop, the draft remains, and cancellation is not converted into an inline error.
- Test plaza player failure copy in a release build: Memo creation, plaza refresh, comment load/send/report, and report/claim cloud failures should show localized guidance and should not expose raw exception or backend diagnostic strings.
- Test plaza feed state hierarchy: first-load spinner should show `正在加载视频…`, refresh failure with no cached videos should show the error icon/title/explanation and `重试`, search empty should show the query-specific empty guidance, and saved/category empty states should keep their own guidance copy.
- Plaza favorites empty state is covered on API 37 together with populated, loading, failure/retry, medium-detent, close, and login-continuation behavior. The independent sheet shows `还没有收藏的视频` plus the bookmark guidance and leaves the underlying discovery category/search/pagination state owned by the unchanged Plaza page.
- Test plaza list/coach failure copy in a release build: refresh, load-more, like/save rollback, and coach-profile load failures should show localized retry guidance and should not expose raw exception or backend diagnostic strings.
- Test plaza list/coach thrown-error recovery: inject category/video refresh, load-more, coach-profile, and coach-video throws outside normal `Result` failures; confirm feed/coach spinners stop, existing cached content stays visible where available, and cancellation is not converted into user-visible failure copy.
- Test plaza coach profile states: first load should show `正在加载教练主页…`, an empty coach should show `暂无视频作品` with the video icon, and a failed coach/video fetch with no local videos should show `加载失败` plus a working `重试` action.
- Test plaza coach profile navigation exits: open a coach from the plaza player, tap a coach video and confirm it opens that video player, return to the coach profile, then tap the top-bar Home action and confirm Android lands on the V2 Home tab instead of merely popping back to the previous plaza screen.
- Test plaza comments visual hierarchy with comments that have/omit avatars and span fresh, same-day, yesterday, and older dates; the dialog title should show the count and rows should keep name/content/time readable.
- Test plaza comments and report/claim dialogs with the keyboard open on gesture and three-button navigation devices; send/submit/cancel actions should remain reachable.
- Test plaza comment reporting: own comments should not expose report, other users' comments should ask for confirmation, logged-out report taps should route to login, success should show `举报已提交，感谢反馈`, and cloud failure should keep the comments dialog visible with an inline error.
- Test plaza report/claim dialogs specifically: missing claim contact should show an inline error, cloud failure should keep all fields editable with an error banner, and success should show the success banner briefly before closing.
- Test plaza report/claim thrown-error recovery: inject `reportComment`, `reportVideo`, and `claimVideo` throws outside normal `Result` failures, confirm submit buttons re-enable, entered report/claim fields stay editable, and cancellation is not converted into user-visible failure copy.
- Test plaza watch history logged out and logged in: logged-out playback should not create anonymous history, direct history entry should show login, and logged-in playback/progress/remove/clear should still persist per account.
- Test plaza watch history on gesture and three-button navigation devices: logged-out/login-required, empty, and populated states should keep actions clear of the system navigation area, and the clear action should only appear while logged in with history.
- Test plaza video download with real VOD URLs because Android now opens the same download server flow in plaza-source mode; backend rejection should fall back visibly rather than stranding the user.
- Test online download completion: after a local or online download Memo is generated, tap the completed-state CTA and success-dialog primary action, confirm Android opens the generated video Memo edit page with source locked, saves title/note/tag/goal changes, then lands in the video Memo player.
- Test plaza save-to-Memo sheet from the iOS navigation atlas: tap the player Memo action, verify `保存到视频备忘录` opens with default tags, toggle tags, save, and confirm the resulting video Memo uses the selected tags; repeat on an already-saved plaza Memo and confirm it reopens without duplicating records.
- Test plaza full-screen playback on gesture and three-button navigation devices; the progress scrubber should stay above the system navigation area and remain easy to drag.

(`稳 / 细 / 帧` belongs only to current iOS comparison mode, not the main player.)
- Video Memo player delete safety is automated on API 37: duplicate delete/cancel taps are blocked during `删除中…`, injected persistence failure leaves the player and record intact with localized recovery, and the next retry removes the record and exits exactly once.
- Test Video Memo player on gesture and three-button navigation devices; bottom controls, more menu, drawing tools, and compare picker should remain above system navigation.
- Video Memo unavailable playback hierarchy is automated on API 37: missing local media exposes only the current iOS read-only `返回` action, playback error exposes `重试 / 返回`, and the disabled central tap layer cannot intercept either card. The obsolete Android-only `编辑 / 重新选择视频` and source-replacement route remain absent.

- Test Memo tag creation and video-Memo online URL entry with keyboard open on gesture and three-button navigation devices; create/use/cancel actions should remain reachable.
- Test Text Memo attachment affordances against their current per-page contracts: the create-page `挂个视频参考？` row must remain visibly locked, expose `挂视频参考暂未接入`, and have no click action; detail must show static `暂不支持添加`, render `暂无附件` or preserved video/image counts, and never open an attachment fallback dialog.
- Test Video Memo camera permission recovery after permanent denial: tap `打开设置`; normal devices should open the app settings page, and restricted/failed launches should show `系统设置暂时无法打开，请稍后重试`.
- Video Memo camera permission-prompt failure is automated on API 37: an injected unavailable permission UI keeps the entered title and complete form open, shows `无法请求相机权限，请稍后重试或改从相册选择`, and returns to an unchanged form with album recovery still available.
- Video Memo camera launch failure after permission is granted is automated on API 37: an injected unavailable camera deletes the temporary `capture_` output, keeps the entered title and form open, and shows `无法打开相机，请稍后重试或改从相册选择` in the compact iOS-style alert.
- Video Memo album-picker failure recovery is automated on API 37: an injected missing picker closes the keyboard, keeps the entered title and complete form open, shows `无法打开相册，请稍后重试`, and returns to the unchanged form after dismissal. Edit mode intentionally has no picker because current iOS locks the source.
