## Confirmed Module Mapping

| Product area | Android | iOS reference |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Root tabs | `V2RootScreen.kt`, `V2BottomTabBar.kt` | `V2RootView.swift`, `V2BottomTabBar.swift` |
| Home | `ui/home/V2HomeScreen.kt` | `V2/Views/Home/V2HomeView.swift` |
| Memo | `ui/memo/*` | `V2/Views/Memo/*` |
| AI chat/config | `ui/ai/*`, `ui/settings/V2AIServiceConfigScreen.kt` | `V2/Views/AI/*` |
| Plaza | `ui/plaza/*` | `V2/Views/Plaza/*` |
| Profile | `ui/profile/*` | `V2/Views/Profile/*` |
| Swing records | `ui/swing/*`, `data/model/V2SwingSession.kt` | `Views/JustSwing/*`, `V2/Controllers/V2MemoBridge.swift` |
| Shared drawing kit | `ui/common/V2DrawingKit.kt` | `Views/Common/DrawingKit.swift` |
| Design tokens | `ui/designsystem/DSColors.kt` | `V2/DesignSystem/DSColors.swift` |

## Current Swing Player Parity

Android now matches the current iOS frame-player hierarchy locally:

- Full-screen black player shell, safe insets, 44dp toolbar, push-mode `返回`, and modal chevron-only close chrome.
- Paused-first Media3 playback with current/duration labels, `帧 current/total`, exact `-10/-3/-1/+1/+3/+10` frame-aligned stepping, four slow-play rates, and a 44dp absolute-frame scrubber.
- Audio impact-candidate detection, up to three candidates, collapsed-by-default `击球帧` toggle, timeline markers, over-video candidate buttons, and exact candidate seeking.
- Drawing overlay and complete drawing tools, retained annotations after closing tools, and the iOS confirmation hierarchy before clearing marks.
- The seven-row `功能说明` sheet and details panel for time, duration, runtime fps, file size, and retention status.
- Favorite, growth-pool promotion/demotion confirmation, gallery save, details, and delete actions with the current iOS copy and toast behavior.
- Reciprocal `切换到回看 / 切换到逐帧` routing into the shared professional analysis player.

The remaining swing-player work is acceptance evidence rather than a missing local UI feature: verify exact stepping on physical high-frame-rate and variable-frame-rate recordings, validate impact candidates against clear/muted/noisy real range audio, and exercise gallery plus retention persistence against real device storage/backend state.

## Data / Capability Gaps

Android `V2SwingClip` currently stores only:

- `id`
- `sessionId`
- `videoPath`
- `thumbnailPath`
- `timestamp`
- `durationSec`
- `retention`

The compact stored model intentionally remains unchanged. Android now derives fps, total frames, and file size from the current local media and runs the iOS-aligned audio impact detector when the page opens; malformed or absent capture-rate metadata falls back to the same 30fps default as iOS. These derived values are recomputed after reopening rather than persisted, so physical-device variable-frame-rate accuracy remains an acceptance item rather than a missing page capability.

## Recommended Swing Acceptance Goals

1. Run the same 30/60/120fps and variable-frame-rate source clips through iOS and Android, then compare stepping, end-frame clamping, and scrub landing on physical devices.
2. Validate the shared impact-detection contract with clear-strike, muted, and noisy-range clips; candidate absence must remain a quiet no-chip state.
3. Exercise favorite, growth-pool, gallery save, and delete failure/retry against real account and MediaStore state without stale list/detail UI.
4. Compare the same source in both frame and professional-review modes, including reciprocal switching, annotation retention, audio, screenshot, recording, and video comparison.

## Working Rules

- Keep each implementation batch small enough to finish, verify, and explain within about 2 hours.
- Commit after meaningful code changes.
- Prefer the full Android gate before code commits:

```bash
JAVA_HOME='/Applications/Android Studio.app/Contents/jbr/Contents/Home' ./gradlew testDebugUnitTest
JAVA_HOME='/Applications/Android Studio.app/Contents/jbr/Contents/Home' ./gradlew lintRelease --rerun-tasks
JAVA_HOME='/Applications/Android Studio.app/Contents/jbr/Contents/Home' ./gradlew assembleDebug assembleRelease
```

- When a device is connected, install and launch the debug build so the visual result can be checked on the Android phone.

### Current Code-Side Coverage Highlights

- Workflow state now prunes invalid downstream progress after real video config updates and coerces the visible step back to the next open iOS step.
- The V2 overview authority rows now point to local evidence for the six-step resume/order gates, no-skip step badges, continuous completion requirements, and cache restore normalization before direct preview.
- Workflow cache decode now rejects non-finite restored P0/P3/P1/P2, landing time, candidate confidence/time, preset alpha, style defaults, and render tuning before resume state can qualify for preview.
- Video metadata rotation is now normalized modulo 360 before visual dimension calculation, keeping source detection consistent with export orientation handling for equivalent angles such as `450` or `-90`.
- Workflow cache restore now uses the same modulo rotation normalization as detection/export, so restored `450` or `-90` metadata cannot flip preview/export orientation evidence back to `0`.
- `GolfCamVideoConfig()` now represents unknown geometry as `1x1` instead of a fake portrait `1080x1920`, so direct preview callers cannot render a misleading initial aspect before detection.
- Video config detection now keeps missing width/height metadata as `1px` placeholders instead of inventing a portrait size, so the entry redetection/preview guard can catch untrusted visual geometry.
- ExoPlayer timing backfill now only updates duration/fps and preserves unknown visual geometry as `1px` placeholders, so player callbacks cannot bypass the detected-config requirement for preview/export.
- Entry preview config validation now also rejects non-finite or out-of-range fps, keeping trajectory timing on detected `1...120fps` source cadence before preview/export can open.
- Workflow cache restore now preserves missing or invalid fps as an invalid placeholder instead of defaulting, clamping, or inferring from cached frame/time fields, so real source cadence must be redetected before preview/export.
- Workflow completion now treats an existing but invalid duration as incomplete for trim validation, so detected/configured duration must be trustworthy before downstream impact, curve, style, preview, or export state can survive.
- Workflow completion now treats an existing but invalid fps as incomplete for impact-frame validation, so invalid cached timing cannot be silently interpreted as 1fps and keep downstream curve/style outputs alive.
- Video frame-rate detection now estimates fps from sorted, deduplicated sample PTS median delta with overflow-safe even-sample averaging, so duplicated frames, mild timestamp reordering, extreme bad timestamps, or one delayed decoded sample cannot pull every downstream frame/time conversion away from the source cadence.
- Shared frame-timeline helpers, metadata config creation, and ExoPlayer timing backfill now collapse malformed non-finite frame-rate inputs to a finite 30fps fallback before applying the existing `1...120` clamp, so trim, impact, landing, curve, and export calculations cannot emit unstable frame counts or timestamps from invalid numeric metadata.
- Shared frame-timeline helpers now also saturate extreme range duration, frame count, frame-time addition, and relative time offsets instead of overflowing `Long`/`Int`, so restored/cache timing outliers cannot corrupt the common trim, impact, landing, curve, preview, or export clock.
- Impact candidate conversion, impact-frame selection, curve seek/thumbnail sampling, and preview fallback clipping now reuse the shared safe end-exclusive and range-duration helpers instead of hand-rolling `start + 1`, keeping all step tools on the same overflow-safe trim boundary.
- Candidate conversion/cache restore now distinguish non-empty trim range duration from last-anchor elapsed delta, so a one-frame clip still has `0.0s` drawable candidate span instead of accidentally admitting a synthetic `0.001s` candidate.
- Export frame count now delegates to the shared frame-timeline helper, and output PTS/source-frame sampling use the same shared finite fps fallback as preview/timeline helpers, so malformed frame-rate metadata or extreme durations cannot make preview and MP4 export diverge.
- Preview/export millisecond-to-microsecond conversion, elapsed-time subtraction, source-frame timestamp selection, and audio zero-origin PTS now use saturated arithmetic; audio passthrough receives the same saturated trim microsecond range as video source-frame sampling, preserving valid pre-impact negative elapsed values while preventing extreme restored/cache timestamps from overflowing the half-open trim range or flipping presentation-time deltas.
- The V2 preview/export authority doc now also names the shared crop as `[trimStart, trimEnd)`, matching Android's video frame sampling, audio sample filtering, and zero-origin output PTS helpers.
- Workflow cache restore sanitizes malformed JSON and no longer fabricates a complete video config when legacy cache lacks real metadata.
- Workflow cache restore now also prunes unreachable downstream fields after decode, so invalid legacy trim/video state cannot leave stale impact, landing, curve, or style selections waiting behind the reset step.
- Workflow cache restore now only applies frame-accurate tail clamping when a cached fps exists; older caches without fps keep half-open trim bounds until video detection supplies the real frame rate.
- Entry preview routing now derives `TrackStyleSelection`, impact time, and trim bounds only from a current complete workflow with detected duration and visual dimensions, so stale preview/export parameters cannot survive upstream re-edit pruning or missing legacy video config.
- Entry preview state now carries the already verified video config snapshot into the overview, so preview timing, 4K availability, and export gating use the same detected fps/visual geometry that made the six-step workflow eligible for preview.
- Trim output generation now has its own range and minimum-duration guard, so the saved `TrimResult` stays valid even if future UI drag code passes reversed or out-of-bounds fractions.
- Trim fraction helpers now reject non-finite direct-call values before handle, playhead, seek, and `TrimResult` output math, falling back to the iOS default `0...min(duration,30s)` posture instead of allowing malformed fractions into downstream timing.
- V2 trim authority now points to Android's actual `TRIM_THUMBNAIL_COUNT` constant and its local evidence, so the 15-cell trim strip is covered by `trimThumbnailSamplingMatchesIosFifteenEvenlySpacedFrames` instead of only manual observation.
- V2 trim authority now also points the `m:ss.d` time display row to the tested `formatTrimTimeLabel` helper; the remaining dim-mask/border row stays screenshot-driven.
- Trim dim-mask, selection-border, selected-fill, strip/selection radii, bar-height, handle-width, touch-extra, and safe-margin values are now exposed as tested spec constants and aligned to the iOS V2 `CustomTrimmerSlider` implementation, so the remaining dim-mask/border acceptance is limited to screenshot-level visual confirmation rather than unguarded magic numbers.
- Impact-position updates now clamp P0 at the workflow-state boundary, so drag/double-tap and future callers cannot write coordinates outside the video-normalized `0...1` range before landing/curve/style are cleared.
- The V2 impact-position authority rows now point to local evidence for marker size, drag radius, label formatting, direct P0 write, downstream clearing, and preserving the selected impact-frame time while editing only P0.
- Impact/landing overlay component state now also normalizes restored P0/P3 coordinates at initialization, so malformed direct-call state cannot put markers at non-finite Compose offsets before the workflow-state guards run.
- Workflow-state and shared track-style clamps now reject non-finite coordinates, landing time, speed, distance, color alpha, preset alpha, and render-tuning values before preview/export can consume them; malformed values fall back to iOS production defaults such as centered anchors, 4.5s flight time, 0.45 speed, 150 distance, and default render tuning.
- StepTool formatting, overlay coordinate normalization, and shared V5 sampler helpers now also reject non-finite values, so Android cannot show `NaN%`/`NaNs` in the six-step UI or feed non-finite head progress, distance, or tail progress into preview/export rendering.
- Preview and export use the same clipped playback timebase for impact, landing, progress markers, animation sampling, and export frame timestamps.
- Overview preview and Track Style live preview timing now use the shared frame timeline for finite fps fallback and saturated landing-time addition, so malformed restored flight-time/fps values cannot make preview clocks diverge from export timing guards.
- Audio passthrough now exposes and tests its copy-buffer fallback/minimum sizing, keeping malformed `KEY_MAX_INPUT_SIZE` values from unnecessarily dropping the source audio track during best-effort export.
- Audio muxer writes now use tested helpers requiring positive sample size and half-open trimmed source PTS, while empty extractor samples are skipped instead of ending audio passthrough early; audio output PTS remains zero-origin and aligned with the video export range.
- Audio muxer sample flags are now explicitly translated from `MediaExtractor.SAMPLE_FLAG_*` to `MediaCodec.BUFFER_FLAG_*` before `BufferInfo.set`, keeping the export path lint-clean and avoiding constant-domain drift in audio passthrough.
- The expanded local gate now passes `lintDebug` and `assembleDebug`; the export audio flag conversion and the localized Media3 `UnstableApi` opt-in keep the GolfCam path from blocking the debug build/lint acceptance gate.
- Release-level local verification now also passes `lintRelease` and `assembleRelease`, so the current GolfCam changes do not block the release lint or minified APK packaging gate before device acceptance.
- Audio impact detection now normalizes malformed trim start/end seconds before PCM extraction and keeps candidate base times finite, so detector output cannot seed non-finite impact anchors.
- Impact-frame selection, candidate conversion, cache restore, and workflow completion now share the same half-open trim boundary as export/audio, so `trim.endMs` is not accepted as a drawable/exportable impact anchor and out-of-range frame indexes are clamped to the last valid frame.
- The V2 impact-frame authority doc now explicitly states the same rule: `trim.endMs` is a playback/export boundary, while selected impact anchors must clamp to the last displayable frame inside `[trim.startMs, trim.endMs)`.
- Impact-frame async candidate adoption now explicitly replaces only the automatic middle-frame fallback, so late detector results can satisfy the iOS priority order without stealing a frame the user already moved to manually.
- The V2 impact-frame authority rows for true-fps conversion and state write now point to local evidence: 60fps trim-relative frame/time conversion, candidate-list normalization, and workflow-state preservation when only candidate frames refresh.
- Workflow cache restore now recomputes the restored impact frame index from the restored impact time when a valid fps is available, so legacy frame/time drift does not unnecessarily invalidate otherwise recoverable progress.
- Impact candidate conversion now discards detector times before `trim.startMs` or at/after `trim.endMs` instead of clamping them into the first/last frame, so abnormal detector/cache output cannot create a false boundary impact candidate.
- Impact-frame selection now normalizes its candidate list against the current trim and detected fps before writing state, so stale cache/direct-call candidates cannot carry out-of-range frame indexes, out-of-range relative times, or unclamped confidence into candidate badges.
- Workflow cache restore now applies the same candidate-list normalization when a valid trim and fps are available, and clears cached candidates when fps must be redetected, so old candidate badges cannot survive with a different frame clock.
- Impact-frame candidate handling now rejects non-finite relative/absolute candidate times, converts non-finite confidence to `0`, and keeps candidate percentage/time-label UI finite before badges, one-tap seek, landing, preview, or export can consume the selection.
- Impact-frame previous/next controls now call a tested one-frame helper, so button taps cannot skip frames or write outside `0...totalFrames-1` even if future UI refactors bypass Compose disabled states.
- Impact-frame thumbnail strip sampling now uses the same frame timeline as selection, so the first/last thumbnail cover the first and last valid trim-relative frames without sampling exactly at `trim.endMs`.
- The V2 authority rows for impact-frame thumbnail cache fill, P0 clamp, and P3 clamp now point to local Android evidence, leaving only device smoothness/drag acceptance for those rows.
- V2 authority now requires Android thumbnail timelines to match iOS cache-first behavior: video-level prewarm starts from the entry once usable duration is known, nearest cached frame fills empty slots, and background exact-frame replacement is prioritized around the current/visible frame.
- Shared Android thumbnail cache is now consumed by trim, impact-frame, landing, and flight-curve timelines, so thumbnail strips can render cached/nearest frames immediately after first-step prewarm and refine exact samples in place; impact, landing, and curve timelines restart exact-frame priority sorting when their current focus moves to a different thumbnail cell instead of on every millisecond-level focus change, and curve thumbnails now also repeat nearest-fill after every exact-frame callback instead of leaving untouched cells blank until their own decode finishes.
- Thumbnail decode priority is now quantized to the nearest visible sample time before calling the shared loader, so trim-to-impact preload, impact-frame, landing, and flight-curve timelines keep iOS-style cell-level priority ordering instead of letting millisecond-level playhead movement churn background extraction order.
- Trim-to-impact navigation now mirrors the current iOS `prepareForImpactFrameStep`: Android pauses preview, shows the preparation overlay, runs the missing trim-scoped thumbnail fill and impact-sound detection together, and transitions into Step 2 only after both finish. A complete thumbnail cache starts the overlay at `100%` while sound detection finishes instead of bypassing the gate.
- Trim-to-impact preparation sets a synchronous gate before launching its coroutine, so a fast double-tap on Next cannot start duplicate `MediaMetadataRetriever` or audio-detection work while the first preparation is running.
- Video-level thumbnail prewarm now also allows only one active preload per selected video key, so entry/container duration backfill jitter cannot launch overlapping `MediaMetadataRetriever` work for the same source while the first prewarm is still filling the shared cache.
- Stale video-level prewarm markers are now ignored when the selected video key has already changed, so a fast A -> B -> A reselect can start a fresh A prewarm instead of being blocked by the old cancelled task's active flag.
- Video-level thumbnail loads and active-preload markers now both carry a selected-video generation token, so an old A preload cannot resume cache writes, UI callbacks, or clear the new A active marker after an A -> B -> A reselection just because the URI key matches the final selection again.
- A03 now has API 37 proof for the real cold-cache preparation overlay, completion before Step 2, exact current-iOS copy/geometry, Step 2/4/5 nonblank cache-first strips, and a concurrent real-video A -> B -> A run where only the final A cache completes.
- The Step 2/4/5 thumbnail-preload acceptance note must now explicitly cover impact-frame, landing, and flight-curve strips, preventing a Step 2-only review from satisfying the broader iOS cache-first timeline requirement.
- A10 export acceptance now rejects `export-inspection.md` reports whose `Video size` is not one of the iOS production export dimensions (`1920x1080`, `1080x1920`, `3840x2160`, `2160x3840`), so resolution UI/badge selection must be reflected in the actual MP4 before export can pass.
- API 37 now exercises the real production GolfCam exporter with an embedded 0.6-second H.264 + AAC fixture: the generated MP4 is non-empty and decodable at `1920x1080`, its midpoint frame contains the configured mint trajectory pixels, export progress reaches `1.0`, its AAC track is preserved, and the first audio sample starts within `0...150ms` of the zero-origin output timeline. This closes the automated Android export/audio proof while leaving the same-source iOS visual comparison and human listening pass open.
- Trim-to-impact preparation overlay metrics are named and tested against current iOS: `#1C1C1E` card, `20dp` corner/content spacing, `32dp` inner padding, `18/14/16sp` title/subtitle/percent text, `200x8dp` progress bar, and clamped `0%...100%` percent formatting.
- Trim thumbnail extraction is keyed by video/sample list rather than playhead time, so normal preview playback or handle seek updates cannot repeatedly cancel and restart the 15-cell cache fill.
- High-frequency GolfCam numeric Compose state now uses primitive state holders for video frame rate, trim fractions, and flight-curve control points, removing the GolfCam `AutoboxingStateCreation` lint hints from drag/playback/thumbnail-adjacent UI paths.
- Step thumbnail timelines now record exact cached samples already displayed during initialization and skip only those duplicate cached callbacks during background loading, while still accepting exact frames that arrive from concurrent video-level prewarm after the initial cache read.
- Shared thumbnail loading now stops stale background work from writing cache entries or UI callbacks after the selected video key changes, so an old video's late thumbnail task cannot evict the new video's prewarm cache during fast reselect/restore flows.
- Shared thumbnail cache now clamps negative sample times to `0` and saturates millisecond-to-microsecond conversion before `MediaMetadataRetriever`, so malformed direct callers cannot request negative frames or overflow retriever timestamps.
- Shared thumbnail sampling now caps malformed oversized requests to the iOS 120-frame prewarm bound, avoids end-time overflow, and uses saturated nearest-frame distance sorting when given extreme restored/cache timestamps.
- Shared thumbnail cache capacity now reserves enough room for the full 120-frame video-level prewarm plus the largest 60-frame focused step strip and the 15-frame trim strip, so entering a later thumbnail-heavy step does not immediately evict the just-prewarmed first-step cache.
- Video-level thumbnail prewarm now uses the same `>=0.5s` usable-duration gate as the trim minimum and samples up to the last displayable millisecond before `duration`, matching iOS prewarm's non-inclusive end-frame stride and avoiding slow/empty retriever work at the source end boundary.
- Trim's 15-cell thumbnail strip now reuses the same bounded millisecond sampler and saturated retriever timestamp conversion, so malformed durations cannot overflow the old `duration * 1000` microsecond path.
- Step-zero import status constants now match the iOS authority messages for restoring cached progress, detecting video ratio/fps, and generating preview from a completed restored workflow.
- Export source-frame sampling now uses a tested half-open trim helper, so decoded video frame timestamps cannot move beyond `trimEnd` while output PTS remains zero-origin.
- Export progress now reports completed frames over total frames, so the final rendered frame emits `1.0` instead of leaving the export UI just below 100%.
- Landing timeline seek and thumbnail sampling now clamp to the last valid frame using the detected frame rate, removing the previous fixed 33ms tail offset.
- Landing output updates now share tested merge helpers, so sliding the landing timeline cannot reset P3 coordinates, direct time changes clamp to `0.5...8.0`, and dragging/double-tapping the marker cannot reset the selected flight time or write P3 outside `0...1`.
- The V2 landing authority rows now point to local evidence for the 56dp timeline height, default/range/virtual-segment rules, `tTail = clipEnd - impactTime`, merged `LandingOutput` updates, and preserving the current P3 into curve completion.
- Landing updates now clamp P3 coordinates and flight time at the workflow-state boundary, so curve/style generation cannot consume out-of-range landing output from future callers.
- Flight-curve updates now clamp P1/P2 at the workflow-state boundary and bind P0/P3 to the current completed impact/landing selections, keeping style generation, preview, and export on the same curve anchors.
- The V2 authority row for flight-curve defaults now points to the tested GolfCam-like suggested-control helper and tool readout fallback, and the curve-stroke row points to the tested 180-segment width/color/butt-cap/shadow spec.
- The V2 flight-curve authority rows for P0/P3 sources and `FlightCurveSelection` output now point to local evidence proving curve writes rebind to current anchors and track-style completion requires the same curve.
- Shared GolfCam curve spec now also rejects non-finite suggested/dragged control points and clamps stroke segment indexes before width/color interpolation, so direct curve-spec callers cannot create NaN control handles, opacity, or color channels.
- Flight-curve tool readouts now use the same GolfCam-like suggested P1/P2 as the video overlay when curve state has not been written yet, avoiding a temporary old-control-point display during initialization.
- Workflow cache restore now fills missing legacy flight-curve P1/P2 controls with the same GolfCam-like suggested defaults as the live curve step, so restored previews/exports do not fall back to old hardcoded curve handles.
- Flight-curve timeline seek now uses the same half-open trim and detected-fps frame clock, and programmatic frame indexes are clamped before display/timeline centering, so frame buttons, thumb selection, restored state, and impact-frame jumps cannot seek to `trim.endMs` or show out-of-range frame labels.
- Flight-curve and impact-frame thumbnail centering now use a shared `Long` frame-index distance helper, so extreme restored/direct-call frame indexes cannot overflow `abs(Int)` and select the wrong nearest thumbnail.
- Flight-curve thumbnail sampling now uses the same half-open trim helper as frame seek, so the first/last thumbnails cover drawable frames without sampling exactly at `trim.endMs`.
- Flight-curve timeline cell spacing is now a named, tested `0dp` constant matching the iOS V2 `FlightCurveStepTool` spec, so future visual-density changes cannot silently insert gaps between curve thumbnails.
- Flight-curve tool vertical padding is now a named, tested `8dp` constant applied at the root tool column, matching the iOS V2 compact tool-area baseline instead of relying on parent layout padding.
- Track-style main-flow tabs are now locked to the iOS production five-category set, so trajectory/effect/tuning controls cannot reappear as primary Android entries without breaking tests.
- Production `videoPlaza/getTrackStyleConfig` was rechecked again on 2026-10-06 through the same CloudBase endpoint used by Android and returned `success=true`, version `1781963575066`, `lastUpdated=2026-06-20T14:09:42.681Z`, default preset `青蓝薄荷`, default color `#55F3D8`, `defaultOpacity=0.75`, style defaults `40/70/0.45/false/150/yards`, 16 presets, and all 21 render-tuning keys. The response remains identical to the exact production payload covered by `productionTrackStyleCloudSnapshotParsesAllDefaultsPresetsAndRenderTuning`.
- Dedicated API 37 instrumentation tests now feed a real non-empty MP4 through GolfCam's production `saveExportedVideoToGallery` path, verify a new readable non-empty `GolfSwing_*.mp4` row appears in system MediaStore, and remove only the row they created. The end-to-end overview flow now covers both branches: save confirms MediaStore stays unchanged during the temporary preview and publishes only after `保存到相册`; share opens the real `com.android.intentresolver` target list with the temporary MP4 while MediaStore remains unchanged, captures the rendered chooser, returns to the same preview, and cleans up only any rows created by the test. This closes `A12` on API 37.
- GolfCam export preview now includes iOS' secondary system-share action instead of stopping at gallery save. Export temp files are created under the app's FileProvider-scoped `cache/videos` directory, shared as a `video/mp4` content URI with temporary read-only permission, and keep the preview close/back/save/share controls locked while save or share is active. The Android back control also uses the iOS-style left chevron and the same 40-point circular treatment.
- Cloud config parsing now rejects non-finite `defaultOpacity`, style defaults, preset `glowBlur/glowAlpha/bandAlpha`, and render tuning values back to the same production defaults used by iOS/fallback config before the style tool, preview, or export can consume them.
- Track-style distance editing now uses shared commit/unit-toggle helpers, keeping the iOS rule that raw numeric input is not clamped while typing but final submitted values are limited to `10...1000`.
- Track-style distance visibility changes now also clamp legacy or direct-call distance values before preview/export state sees them, while restoring the iOS 150 default when enabling from an empty or non-positive value.
- Track-style distance unit parsing now accepts both iOS/cloud lowercase values (`yards` / `meters`) and Android enum names, so cached or remote meter selections keep the same `YDS/M` preview/export label after restore.
- Track-style slider display values now reject non-finite or out-of-range direct state before Compose Slider receives them, keeping opacity, width, speed, and tail controls inside their iOS ranges even if a future caller bypasses workflow/cloud clamps.
- Track-style updates now clamp final style values at the workflow-state boundary, force the production V5 algorithm path, and bind the style selection to the current flight curve before preview/export can treat the workflow as complete.
- Cloud config parsing, workflow cache restore, and final track-style completion now share the same production render-tuning clamp, keeping preview/export glow, highlight, taper, and apex thinning parameters from drifting by source.
- Track-style remote config parsing now accepts either an object payload or a JSON-string object inside the successful CloudBase envelope, so backend serialization differences do not unnecessarily fall through to cache or local defaults.
- Cloud config refresh now also clamps render tuning at the merge helper boundary, so direct config snapshots or future cache changes cannot inject out-of-range glow/highlight/taper values into preview/export before the final workflow-state guard.
- Preview/export entry now requires the completed track-style selection to already match the iOS production clamps, including render tuning, so direct or restored stale values cannot open the overview or export path until normalized.
- Preview/export rendering now also normalizes its received `TrackStyleSelection` at the component boundary, so direct callers cannot bypass the production clamps before Compose preview or MP4 export sampling.
- Preview/export selection normalization is now tested as idempotent, matching the runtime boundary where the overview passes the normalized preview selection into export and export applies the same guard again without changing the result.
- Preview/export rendering now also refuses invalid `initialVideoConfig` at the component boundary and falls back to unknown geometry while redetecting, so direct callers cannot seed preview/export timing or aspect ratio from stale metadata.
- Shared preview/export ribbon geometry now also clamps malformed `TrackStyle`, render tuning, head/tail progress, video dimensions, stroke width, band alpha, highlight ratio, and head-glow alpha at the renderer/math boundary, so direct Paint/Path drawing cannot diverge or emit non-finite values even if an upstream caller misses normalization.
- Shared preview/export head-glow alpha byte conversion now also rejects NaN/Infinity before Android color creation, keeping Compose preview and Canvas export on the same finite opacity behavior.
- Shared Bezier geometry and arc-length lookup now reject non-finite direct-call control points, progress, dimensions, arc targets, and malformed sample counts before producing offsets/head points, so malformed preview/export inputs cannot leak NaN coordinates into Compose or Canvas drawing even if an upstream workflow clamp is bypassed.
- Shared preview/export distance-label specs and video display fit geometry now reject non-finite heights, coordinates, text metrics, and aspect ratios before calculating font sizes, shadows, label positions, or fitted video bounds, keeping Canvas export and Compose preview on finite iOS-scaled layout numbers.
- Shared preview/export frame sampling now rejects non-finite legacy-algorithm progress callbacks and the shared flying-head visibility guard only draws for finite `0..<1` progress, so old-model fallback paths cannot leave a glowing head visible from malformed progress values.
- The V2 preview/export authority doc now explicitly points the 15-frame, 50%, and near-landing acceptance checkpoints to `trajectoryOverlaySamplerCoversPreviewExportAcceptanceFrames`; device playback/export comparison is still required for real MP4 evidence.
- Overlay preview video-card shadow radius is now a named, tested `16dp` constant alongside the existing `18dp` card radius, `1dp/0.35` border, and `190dp` bottom panel height, matching the iOS V2 overview-page visual spec.
- Preview bottom-panel playback progress, export progress, and impact/landing marker layout now also reject non-finite progress before Compose width/offset/progress calculation, so malformed direct-call state cannot destabilize the iOS-style overview timeline.
- Preview bottom-panel export is now gated on a trusted video config in both action-state modeling and click handling, so a failed or pending redetection cannot start MP4 export from unknown geometry/timing.
- GolfCam's current overview page now has direct API 37 evidence rather than helper-only coverage. Android removes the extra home-button surface, lets the full-width aspect-fit video use the same card geometry as SwiftUI, exposes a continuously draggable progress strip with timed impact/landing labels and current-position dot, keeps the 190dp content panel outside the navigation safe area, and mirrors iOS' Release/DEBUG action boundary. The export-settings sheet now uses the same top completion hierarchy and source-only 4K gate, while export progress uses the 56dp rainbow ring and a real coroutine-cancel action. `golfcam-overlay-preview-current-ios-parity.mp4` exercises 10% / 50% / 90% seeks, settings, edit and playback against a real MP4; the paired screenshot freezes the 50% state.
- Export settings, the top resolution badge, and the export call now share a tested trusted-video-config state, so `4K` cannot appear selected or be passed into export while source geometry/fps is still unknown or being redetected.
- Export settings now also keep unknown or untrusted video geometry on the HD landscape baseline until detection is trusted, so a `1x1` placeholder cannot show a premature portrait `1080 × 1920` size before metadata redetection completes.
- Export settings now also expose a named, tested `需要4K源视频` unavailable reason for the `4K` row, so source-size gating remains visible in the sheet instead of silently disabling the option.
- Export target dimensions now normalize rotation metadata at the low-level dimension helper, so equivalent angles such as `450` and `-90` map to the same visual canvas as `90`/`270`.
- Export frame drawing and all workflow thumbnails now share the same normalized rotation rule as detection, so equivalent angles like `450`/`-90` are honored and malformed non-cardinal angles like `45` do not rotate preview/export evidence away from iOS visual orientation.
- Export target metadata parsing now uses the same tolerant numeric fallback posture as video detection, so malformed raw width/height/rotation/duration strings do not abort the temp-MP4 export path.
- Preview bottom-panel actions are now modeled against both iOS build variants: Release exposes exactly two active actions, while DEBUG inserts the two disabled `稍后` actions between them. The 190dp content panel places the Android navigation safe area outside its fixed content height, so action titles no longer clip on gesture-navigation devices.
- Canvas export and Compose preview share the same sampler/ribbon/distance/head-glow specifications for the production V5 path.
- The local gate now checks that GolfCam export and gallery save still have a single production path in `GolfCamOverlayPreviewScreen.kt`: one `MediaMuxer`, one MediaStore video insertion, one `exportVideoWithTrajectory` declaration/call pair, and one `saveExportedVideoToGallery` declaration/call pair. This guards the V2 doc requirement that dead or alternate export paths must not bypass export preview before gallery save.
- The production export path check now also verifies that `shouldOpenExportPreview(exportedFile)` guards `exportedVideoFile = exportedFile` and `shouldAllowExportSave(...)` guards `saveExportedVideoToGallery(...)`, so future edits cannot skip the iOS-style temporary preview or save-button gate while keeping the same function names.
- Export first creates a temporary MP4 preview, opens and saves that preview only when the temp file exists and is non-empty, deletes invalid temp preview files before showing an export failure, writes to MediaStore only after the user confirms save, and treats successful save as an iOS-style `onSaveComplete` transition that clears preview state and closes the overview page.
- Gallery save now first verifies that the exported preview file exists and is non-empty, and failed saves delete any inserted MediaStore row before reporting the failure, so a failed or truncated temp-MP4 export cannot be published or left behind as a successful gallery save.
- Export and gallery-save failure messages now use stable release-safe recovery copy for null/blank platform errors and raw codec/file/MediaStore exceptions, so the preview flow never shows an empty suffix or low-level system detail to release users.
- Export preview now consumes system back with the same saving gate as the close button, so the Android back path cannot interrupt the iOS-style `正在保存到相册...` overlay.
- Export preview save/share actions and button visual state now also wait for saving/sharing state, the failure-result dialog, and a valid temp preview file to clear/pass, so a failed MediaStore insert, missing preview file, or active system share cannot look like a ready action.
- Current repository reality: the old GolfCam acceptance-helper scripts referenced by earlier notes are not present in this worktree. Local verification currently means `scripts/android_release_preflight.sh --min-version-code=1 --min-target-sdk=36` plus the unit tests named in this ledger.
- Device acceptance evidence remains manual until dedicated GolfCam evidence tooling is restored or rebuilt. Do not rely on absent helper-script names as proof of source inspection, preview frame extraction, export comparison, or acceptance-record validation.
- Manual evidence packages should still be concrete and auditable: include the tested APK/AAB SHA from release preflight, tester/device/Android version/date, source-video filename, screenshots or recordings for each `A01...A12` row, exported MP4 files where required, gallery screenshots for MediaStore publication, and notes tying each artifact to the row it proves.
- If evidence tooling is restored later, add a local test that all script names referenced by this document exist in `scripts/`, and document the exact command output required before treating scripted reports as acceptance proof.

### Not Yet Proven Complete

The Android implementation is not yet fully accepted against V2 because the following evidence is still missing:

- `A01`: record the exact source-video identity and restored/new-flow configuration result on device.
- `A10`: export the same source on a physical iPhone and compare early/50%/landing frames with Android.
- `A11`: perform a human listening check on a representative real swing with audio; automated API 37 AAC/trim timing already passes.
- Repeat the production cloud-config fetch if the release/acceptance date advances beyond 2026-10-06.

Do not mark GolfCam V2 complete until the missing acceptance evidence above is attached or otherwise recorded.
